## 800001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぉふぉふぉ、<line-break>妻に話しかけるんなら<line-break>わしにも話しかけてほしいのぉ。<line-break>昔っから、よく、<line-break>夫婦は一心同体というからのぉ。<end>"
EN: "Ho ho ho! If you're going to talk to my wife, I'd like you to talk to me too. As the old saying goes, husband and wife are one in body and soul.<end>"
KO: "호호호, 아내에게 말을 걸 거면 나한테도 말 걸어 주면 좋겠구먼. 예전부터 부부는 일심동체라고 하잖나.<end>"

## 800002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>そーですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ohohoho! Quite so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇군요~.<end>"

## 800003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%3わしの祖先は、<line-break>ストラ＝フゴーと言ってのぉ。<line-break>えらい義賊だったそうじゃのぉ。<line-break>名誉なことじゃのぉ。<line-break>でも、わしは気にせんぞぉ。<end>冒険者ギルドの基礎は、<line-break>ストラ＝フゴーが作ったそうじゃのぉ。<line-break>えらい人じゃのぉ。<end>ストラ＝フゴー愛用の<line-break>短剣がどこかに<line-break>あるそうじゃのぉ。<line-break>どこにあるのかのぉ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%3My ancestor was named Stra-Hugo, you know. They say he was a renowned noble thief. Quite an honor, isn't it? Not that I care.<end>They say Stra-Hugo laid the foundations of the Adventurers' Guild. A great man, he was.<end>They say Stra-Hugo's favorite dagger is somewhere out there. I wonder where it could be.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%3내 조상은 스트라=휴고라고 했지. 훌륭한 의적이었다는구먼. 명예로운 일이지. 하지만 난 신경 안 쓴다네.<end>모험자 길드의 기초는 스트라=휴고가 만들었다는구먼. 대단한 사람이야.<end>스트라=휴고가 애용하던 단검이 어딘가에 있다더군. 어디에 있는 걸까.<end>"

## 800004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>そーですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho-ho-ho! Yes, quite so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇군요~.<end>"

## 800005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここはフゴー様のお屋敷です。<end>"
EN: "This is Master Hugo's estate.<end>"
KO: "여기는 휴고 님의 저택입니다.<end>"

## 800006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ああ、あなたは、あの有名な<line-break><value:$2B>の<value:$28>！<line-break>どうぞ、お通りください。<end>"
EN: "Y-you're <value:$28>, the famous <value:$2B>! Please, go right in.<end>"
KO: "아, 당신은 그 유명한 <value:$2B>의 <value:$28>! 지나가십시오.<end>"

## 800007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたのような無名の方を<line-break>通すわけにはまいりません。<line-break>どうぞお引き取りください。<end>"
EN: "I cannot allow someone as little-known as you to enter. Please be on your way.<end>"
KO: "당신처럼 이름 없는 분을 들여보낼 수는 없습니다. 돌아가 주십시오.<end>"

## 800008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どんな意味のなさそうな言葉にも<line-break>言霊の力があり、<line-break>その力は意外な所で実を結ぶもの…。　<line-break>私の言葉を聞きなさい…。<end>"
EN: "Even the most seemingly meaningless words carry spiritual power, and that power may bear fruit in unexpected places. Heed my words...<end>"
KO: "아무 의미 없어 보이는 말에도 언령의 힘이 있고, 그 힘은 뜻밖의 곳에서 결실을 맺는 법…. 내 말을 들으세요….<end>"

## 800009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…大いなる静寂が迫っているわ。<line-break>急いで時を進めないと<line-break>すべてが静寂に飲み込まれてしまう…。<end>"
EN: "The Great Silence draws near. Unless you hurry and advance time, the silence will swallow everything...<end>"
KO: "…거대한 정적이 다가오고 있어. 서둘러 시간을 진행시키지 않으면 모든 것이 정적에 삼켜져 버려….<end>"

## 800010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "日と月の運行は腕輪に影響を及ぼす…。<line-break>次に金環食が起こるのは<line-break>１２０７年８月２３日よ…。<end>"
EN: "The courses of the sun and moon affect the bracelet. The next annular eclipse will occur on August 23, 1207...<end>"
KO: "해와 달의 운행은 팔찌에 영향을 줘…. 다음 금환식은 1207년 8월 23일이야….<end>"

## 800011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "日と月の運行は腕輪に影響を及ぼす…。<line-break>次に金環食が起こるのは<line-break>１２０５年４月２５日よ…。<end>"
EN: "The courses of the sun and moon affect the bracelet. The next annular eclipse will occur on April 25, 1205...<end>"
KO: "해와 달의 운행은 팔찌에 영향을 줘…. 다음 금환식은 1205년 4월 25일이야….<end>"

## 800012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "日と月の運行は腕輪に影響を及ぼす…。<line-break>次に金環食が起こるのは<line-break>１２０４年１月２日よ…。<end>"
EN: "The courses of the sun and moon affect the bracelet. The next annular eclipse will occur on January 2, 1204...<end>"
KO: "해와 달의 운행은 팔찌에 영향을 줘…. 다음 금환식은 1204년 1월 2일이야….<end>"

## 800013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "日と月の運行は腕輪に影響を及ぼす…。<line-break>次に金環食が起こるのは<line-break>１２０２年１０月１８日よ…。<end>"
EN: "The courses of the sun and moon affect the bracelet. The next annular eclipse will occur on October 18, 1202...<end>"
KO: "해와 달의 운행은 팔찌에 영향을 줘…. 다음 금환식은 1202년 10월 18일이야….<end>"

## 800015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょうど金環食が始まった。<line-break>あと、Ｘ分以内に、開かずの扉に<line-break>たどり着かないといけない。<line-break>…だけど、これはシャロームの罠だよ。<line-break>ボクの巫女の知識が<line-break>そう教えてくれている。<end>"
EN: "The annular eclipse has just begun. We have to reach the door that never opens within Ｘ minutes. But this is a trap set by Shalom. My knowledge as a priestess tells me so.<end>"
KO: "마침 금환식이 시작됐어. 앞으로 X분 안에 열리지 않는 문에 도착해야 해. …하지만 이건 샬롬의 함정이야. 내 무녀의 지식이 그렇게 알려 주고 있어.<end>"

## 800016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのことをよく考えてね。<line-break>自分を封じているマノンを<line-break>キミに始末させようとしているんだ。<end>"
EN: "Think carefully about that. Shalom wants you to dispose of Manon, who is keeping him sealed away.<end>"
KO: "그 점을 잘 생각해 봐. 자기를 봉인하고 있는 마논을 네게 처리시키려는 거야.<end>"

## 800017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょうど金環食が始まった。<line-break>あと、Ｘ分以内に、開かずの扉に<line-break>たどり着かないといけない。<line-break>だけど、たどり着いてもムダだよ。<line-break>キミには、まだ、<line-break>扉を開けるソウル的な資格がない。<end>"
EN: "The annular eclipse has just begun. We have to reach the door that never opens within Ｘ minutes. But even if we get there, it won't do any good. Your Soul does not yet qualify you to open the door.<end>"
KO: "마침 금환식이 시작됐어. 앞으로 X분 안에 열리지 않는 문에 도착해야 해. 하지만 도착해도 소용없어. 너에겐 아직 문을 열 소울의 자격이 없거든.<end>"

## 800018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁーて、金環食が始まったぞ。<line-break>あと、ざっとみて<line-break>Ｘ分ってとこかな。<end>"
EN: "Well, the annular eclipse has begun. I'd say there are roughly Ｘ minutes left.<end>"
KO: "자아, 금환식이 시작됐다. 이제 대충 봐서 X분 정도인가.<end>"

## 800019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（金環食が始まる…。<line-break>　あとＸ分で終わりそうだ。）<end>"
EN: "(The annular eclipse is beginning... It looks like it will end in Ｘ minutes.)<end>"
KO: "(금환식이 시작된다…. 앞으로 X분이면 끝날 것 같다.)<end>"

## 800020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はぁっ！？　なに？　どうして？　　　<line-break>あああ、ソウルが…ソウルが…<line-break>ソウルが共鳴する…。<line-break>ああ、私の力が弱まる金環食の時に<line-break>アガスティアとブラックバイパーの<line-break>ソウルを持つ者が…ここに来るなんて！<end>"
EN: "What!? What is this? Why? Ahhh, the Souls... the Souls are resonating... Of all times, during the annular eclipse, when my power is weakened... someone bearing the Souls of Agastia and Black Viper has come here!<end>"
KO: "뭐어!? 왜? 어째서!? 아아아, 소울이… 소울이… 소울이 공명한다…. 아아, 내 힘이 약해지는 금환식 때 아가스티아와 블랙 바이퍼의 소울을 가진 자가… 여기에 오다니!<end>"

## 800021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかも、それが…私と同じ…<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者だなんて…！　　<line-break>ソウルが共鳴して…！　はうぅっ！<end>"
EN: "And on top of that... a Bearer of the Infinite Soul, just like me...! The Souls are resonating... Ahh!<end>"
KO: "게다가 그게… 나와 같은… 무한의 소울을 지닌 자라니…! 소울이 공명해…! 하우우!<end>"

## 800022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやあっ！<line-break>…扉が、開くぅ！<end>"
EN: "No! The door... it's opening!<end>"
KO: "싫어! …문이, 열려어!<end>"

## 800023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやあっ！<line-break>ソウルが共鳴して…<line-break>入って…きちゃう！<end>"
EN: "No! The Souls are resonating... You're... getting in!<end>"
KO: "싫어엇! 소울이 공명해서… 안으로… 들어와 버려!<end>"

## 800024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャロームの手の者…かしら？<line-break>ムダよ。<line-break>その扉は開かないわ。<line-break>金環食の間、いくら力が弱まっても<line-break>あなたにその扉を抜けられるほど<line-break>弱くはならないの。<end>"
EN: "An agent of Shalom, perhaps? It's useless. That door will not open. However weak I grow during the annular eclipse, I will never become weak enough for you to pass through it.<end>"
KO: "샬롬의 수하…일까? 소용없어. 그 문은 열리지 않아. 금환식 동안 아무리 힘이 약해져도 네가 그 문을 통과할 만큼 약해지진 않거든.<end>"

## 800025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "導精魔道マノンをなめないで。<line-break>殺されたくなければ<line-break>おとなしく引き上げるのね。<end>"
EN: "Do not underestimate Manon, the Spirit-Guiding Mage. If you don't want to die, turn around and leave quietly.<end>"
KO: "도정마도 마논을 얕보지 마. 죽기 싫으면 얌전히 물러나는 게 좋아.<end>"

## 800026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…シャロームの手の者？<line-break>それとも、好奇心に脳が冒された　　　　　<line-break>冒険者…かしら…？<line-break>私は導精魔道マノン…<line-break>ソウル永続維持のため腕輪から失礼するわ。<line-break>愚かなる侵入者さん？<end>"
EN: "An agent of Shalom...? Or merely an adventurer whose brain has been consumed by curiosity? I am Manon, the Spirit-Guiding Mage. Forgive me for addressing you from within the bracelet; it is necessary to preserve my Soul indefinitely. Well, foolish intruder?<end>"
KO: "…샬롬의 부하? 아니면 호기심에 뇌가 먹힌 모험가…일까…? 나는 도정마도 마논…. 소울을 영구 유지하기 위해 팔찌 안에서 실례할게, 어리석은 침입자 씨?<end>"

## 800027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた、私のこのソウルが<line-break>目当てみたいだけど、<line-break>そうはいかない…消えてもらうわ…。　　　<line-break>私の力が弱まっている上に<line-break>ソウルの共鳴事故で力がほとんど出ないの。<line-break>手加減してあげられないから、死んで？<end>"
EN: "It seems you're after this Soul of mine, but you won't have it. I'll make you disappear... My power is already weakened, and an accident in the Soul resonance has left me almost powerless. I can't afford to go easy on you, so would you kindly die?<end>"
KO: "당신, 내 이 소울이 목적 같지만 그렇게는 안 돼… 사라져 줘…. 내 힘이 약해진 데다 소울 공명 사고로 힘이 거의 나오지 않아. 봐줄 수가 없으니까, 죽어 줄래?<end>"

## 800028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな…！<line-break>これで…シャロームが…！　ああ…！<line-break>数百年の…私の孤独はなんだった…の…？<end>"
EN: "No...! Now Shalom will...! Ah... What was the point of all those centuries... all that loneliness...?<end>"
KO: "그럴 수가…! 이걸로… 샬롬이…! 아아…! 수백 년 동안의… 내 고독은 대체 뭐였던… 거야…?<end>"

## 800029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくやった。<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者よ。<line-break>さあ、来たれ…！<end>"
EN: "You have done well, Bearer of the Infinite Soul. Now, come forth...!<end>"
KO: "잘했다. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여. 자, 오너라…!<end>"

## 800030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくやった、<line-break>これで英雄ソウル、マノンを<line-break>手に入れられよう。<end>"
EN: "Well done. Now you may claim Manon, the Hero Soul.<end>"
KO: "잘했다. 이제 영웅 소울 마논을 손에 넣을 수 있겠군.<end>"

## 800031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は<line-break>英雄ソウル、マノンを修得した。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> acquired Manon, a Hero Soul.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 영웅 소울 마논을 습득했다.<end>"

## 800032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これで、汝は強大なる源羅の力を得た。<line-break>…いよいよの活躍、期待しているぞ。<line-break>人類の革新のために、な…。<end>"
EN: "Now you possess the mighty power of Genra. I look forward to seeing what you accomplish―for Humanity's Evolution...<end>"
KO: "이로써 그대는 강대한 원라의 힘을 얻었다. …앞으로의 활약을 기대하마. 인류의 혁신을 위해서 말이지….<end>"

## 800033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの廃城で<line-break>暴れておるレムゲドンの亡霊も<line-break>源羅の力でしか倒せぬ。<line-break>行け。そして奴を倒し<line-break>己が得た力に恍惚となるとよい。<line-break>力こそが超人類への道ゆえ、な…<end>"
EN: "Even the ghost of Remgedon, rampaging through Ancient's Ruined Castle, can be defeated only with the power of Genra. Go. Slay it, and revel in the power you have gained. For power alone is the path to superhumanity...<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 폐성에서 날뛰는 렘게돈의 망령도 겐라의 힘으로밖에 쓰러뜨릴 수 없다. 가라. 그리고 놈을 쓰러뜨려 네가 얻은 힘에 황홀해져라. 힘이야말로 초인류로 가는 길이니….<end>"

## 800034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ～っ！<line-break>お客さんだよ！<end>"
EN: "Ah! A customer!<end>"
KO: "아~! 손님이다!<end>"

## 800035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、このリルビーか。<line-break>ベテランの冒険者だよ。<line-break>名前はクリスピー…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, this Lilubee? He's a veteran adventurer. His name's Crispy...<end>"
KO: "아, 이 릴비 말인가. 베테랑 모험자야. 이름은 크리스피….<end>"

## 800036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぼくはレルラ＝ロントン。<line-break>キレイを探して旅をする<line-break>さすらいの吟遊詩人だよ。<line-break>さて、それじゃ、<line-break>きみの方は<line-break>何を求めているのかな…？<end>"
EN: "I'm Relra-Lonton, a wandering minstrel traveling in search of beauty. So, what are you searching for...?<end>"
KO: "나는 렐라 론톤. 아름다움을 찾아 여행하는 방랑 음유시인이야. 자, 그럼 너는 무엇을 찾고 있는 걸까…?<end>"

## 800037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きみを守って姿を消した大事な人？<end>"
EN: "Someone dear to you who vanished after protecting you?<end>"
KO: "널 지키고 사라진 소중한 사람?<end>"

## 800038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親の仇？　凶々しい魔道器？<line-break>聖杯？　妖艶な美女？<line-break>それとも…<end>"
EN: "The one who killed your parents? A sinister magical artifact? The Grail? A bewitching beauty? Or perhaps...<end>"
KO: "부모의 원수? 흉흉한 마도기? 성배? 요염한 미녀? 아니면….<end>"

## 800039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界に潜む、謎という謎？<end>"
EN: "Every mystery hidden in the world?<end>"
KO: "세상에 숨어 있는 온갖 수수께끼?<end>"

## 800040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 800046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml

## 800052

## 800055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "우와~! 있잖아, 아저씨. 아무렇게나 말한 것 중 몇 개는 맞았던 모양이야.<end>"

## 800056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ははは、こういう奴だが、<line-break>弓の腕は一流だぜ。<line-break>連れていってやりなよ。<end>"
EN: "Ha ha! He may be a bit much, but he's a first-rate archer. Take him with you.<end>"
KO: "하하하, 이런 녀석이지만 활 솜씨는 일류야. 데려가 줘.<end>"

## 800057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やったぁ！<line-break>それじゃ、町の外で待ってるよ。<end>"
EN: "Yes! Then I'll wait for you outside town.<end>"
KO: "해냈다! 그럼 마을 밖에서 기다릴게.<end>"

## 800058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気をつけるんだぜ。<end>"
EN: "Be careful, all right?<end>"
KO: "조심하라고.<end>"

## 800059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やったぁ！<line-break>でも…今は、満員みたいだね。<end>"
EN: "Yes! But... it looks like you're full up right now.<end>"
KO: "해냈다! 하지만… 지금은 만원인 것 같네.<end>"

## 800060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え、猫屋敷で呼んでくれるの？　　　<line-break>でも猫屋敷って…。<line-break>　<line-break>すごいよ！<line-break>きみは猫屋敷にも入れたんだ！<line-break>ますます、おもしろくなってきたよ！<end>"
EN: "What, you'll call for me at Cat Manor? But Cat Manor is... That's amazing! You managed to get into Cat Manor too! This just keeps getting more interesting!<end>"
KO: "어, 고양이 저택에서 불러 줄 거야? 그런데 고양이 저택이라니…. 대단해! 너는 고양이 저택에도 들어갔구나! 점점 더 재밌어졌어!<end>"

## 800061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぼく、ちょっと近くの森まで行って<line-break>今の感動を曲にしてくるよ！<end>"
EN: "I'm going to head to the nearby woods and turn this excitement into a song!<end>"
KO: "나, 잠깐 근처 숲에 가서 지금 이 감동을 곡으로 만들어 올게!<end>"

## 800062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気をつけるんだぜ。<end>"
EN: "Be careful, all right?<end>"
KO: "조심하라고.<end>"

## 800063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きみからは無限の可能性を感じる…。<line-break>そしてきみはその可能性に怖じてない。<line-break>それは、とても、キレイなことなんだ。<end>"
EN: "I sense limitless potential in you... and you aren't afraid of it. That's a truly beautiful thing.<end>"
KO: "너에게서는 무한한 가능성이 느껴져…. 그리고 넌 그 가능성을 두려워하지 않아. 그건 정말 아름다운 일이야.<end>"

## 800064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ。クリスピーというのは<line-break>レルラ＝ロントンの本名だよ。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>本人に言わせるとキレイじゃないから<line-break>いにしえの詩人、レルラとロントンの<line-break>名前を合わせてつけたんだそうだよ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Crispy is Relra-Lonton's real name. He says it isn't beautiful, so he took the names of two ancient poets, Relra and Lonton, and joined them together.<end>"
KO: "아, 크리스피라는 건 렐라 론톤의 본명이야. 본인 말로는 예쁘지 않아서 옛 시인 렐라와 론톤의 이름을 합쳐 지었다고 하더군.<end>"

## 800065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レルラ＝ロントンはリルビーだから　<line-break>毒に強い。毒を持つ敵が多い所の<line-break>探索では頼りになるぜ。<end>"
EN: "Relra-Lonton is a Lilubee, so he's highly resistant to poison. You can count on him when you're exploring places full of enemies that use poison.<end>"
KO: "렐라 론톤은 릴비라 독에 강해. 독을 가진 적이 많은 곳을 탐색할 땐 믿음직하지.<end>"

## 800066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そっか…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... all right.<end>"
KO: "그렇구나….<end>"

## 800067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぼく…ちょっと近くの森まで行って<line-break>新しい曲をつくってくるね…。<end>"
EN: "I'll... go to the nearby woods and write a new song.<end>"
KO: "나… 근처 숲에 가서 새 곡을 만들고 올게…<end>"

## 800068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気をつけるんだぜ。<end>"
EN: "Be careful, all right?<end>"
KO: "조심하라고.<end>"

## 800069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれで、結構いい奴なんだぜ。<line-break>一緒に連れてってやればよかったのに。<end>"
EN: "He's actually a pretty good lad. You should've taken him with you.<end>"
KO: "저래 보여도 꽤 괜찮은 녀석이야. 같이 데려갔어도 좋았을 텐데.<end>"

## 800070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつ見ても、<line-break>いい絵じゃのぉ。<end>"
EN: "No matter how often I look at it, it's a fine painting.<end>"
KO: "언제 봐도 좋은 그림이구나.<end>"

## 800071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>そーですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho-ho-ho! It certainly is.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇군요~.<end>"

## 800072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "名画であると同時に、<line-break>歴史資料としても<line-break>非常に価値がございます。　　　　　　<line-break>この７枚の絵に描かれた戦神ソリアスと<line-break>破壊神ウルグの戦いの跡地は、今も<line-break>大陸各地に残っているとのことです。<end>"
EN: "Besides being masterpieces, these paintings are also extremely valuable as historical records. The sites where the War God Solias and Ulugh, the God of Destruction, fought―as depicted in these seven paintings―are said to survive across the continent to this day.<end>"
KO: "명화인 동시에 역사 자료로도 대단히 귀중합니다. 이 일곱 그림에 그려진 전신 솔리아스와 파괴신 울그의 전투 흔적은 지금도 대륙 각지에 남아 있다고 합니다.<end>"

## 800073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは興味深いのぉ。<line-break>いつか全部行ってみたいのぉ。<end>"
EN: "How fascinating. I should like to visit every one of them someday.<end>"
KO: "그거 흥미롭구먼. 언젠가 전부 가 보고 싶구먼.<end>"

## 800074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>そーですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho-ho-ho! I suppose so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇군요~.<end>"

## 800075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かしこまりました。<line-break>至聖賢君ノクサも巡ったと伝えられる<line-break>神々の戦場、調べさせておきましょう。<end>"
EN: "Certainly. I shall have inquiries made into those battlefields of the gods, which the Most Holy Sage-King Noxa is also said to have visited.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 지성현군 녹사도 돌았다고 전해지는 신들의 전장을 조사해 두겠습니다.<end>"

## 800076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んー。<line-break>楽しみじゃのぉ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. I shall look forward to it.<end>"
KO: "음~. 기대되는구먼.<end>"

## 800077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>そーですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ohohoho! Yes, quite so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇군요~.<end>"

## 800078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お邪魔いたしました。<line-break>ゆっくりとご鑑賞ください。<end>"
EN: "Forgive the intrusion. Please enjoy the paintings at your leisure.<end>"
KO: "실례했습니다. 천천히 감상해 주세요.<end>"

## 800079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大地神バイアスが、<line-break>破壊神ウルグに敗れた様子を描いた作品。<line-break>この後、闇の時代が始まったと伝えられる。<line-break>荘厳な城塞内がその舞台となっている。<end>"
EN: "A work depicting the Earth God Vyas's defeat at the hands of Ulugh, the God of Destruction. It is said that an age of darkness began afterward. The scene is set inside a majestic fortress.<end>"
KO: "대지신 바이아스가 파괴신 울그에게 패배하는 모습을 그린 작품. 이후 어둠의 시대가 시작되었다고 전해진다. 장엄한 성채 내부가 무대다.<end>"

## 800080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦神ソリアスと２匹の従者により、<line-break>破壊神ウルグが退けられた戦いを描いた作品。<line-break>両者の激突は、岩山をひとつ吹き飛ばすほどの<line-break>激しさであったと伝えられる。<end>"
EN: "A work depicting the battle in which War God Solias and two attendants drove back Ulugh, the God of Destruction. Their clash is said to have been fierce enough to obliterate an entire rocky mountain.<end>"
KO: "전신 솔리아스와 두 종자가 파괴신 울그를 물리친 싸움을 그린 작품. 양자의 격돌은 바위산 하나를 날려 버릴 만큼 격렬했다고 전해진다.<end>"

## 800081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ソリアスと２匹の従者が撤退を余儀なくされた<line-break>ウルグの１２の円卓騎士襲来を描いた作品。<line-break>この戦いでうがたれた穴に水がたまり、<line-break>乙女の鏡ができたと伝えられる。<end>"
EN: "A work depicting the attack by Ulugh's twelve Knights of the Round Table, which forced Solias and two attendants to retreat. It is said that water collected in the hole gouged out during the battle, forming Maiden's Mirror.<end>"
KO: "솔리아스와 두 종자가 철수를 강요당한 울그의 12원탁기사 습격을 그린 작품. 이 싸움으로 뚫린 구멍에 물이 고여 소녀의 거울이 생겼다고 전해진다.<end>"

## 800082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "６種の武器をたずさえた戦神ソリアスが、<line-break>１２の円卓騎士を倒した戦いを描いた作品。<line-break>ソリアス渾身の一撃は、<line-break>大地を深く貫いたと伝えられる。<end>"
EN: "A work depicting the battle in which War God Solias, bearing six types of weapons, defeated the twelve Knights of the Round Table. It is said that a strike delivered with all Solias's might pierced deep into the earth.<end>"
KO: "6종의 무기를 든 전신 솔리아스가 12명의 원탁기사를 쓰러뜨린 전투를 그린 작품. 솔리아스의 혼신의 일격은 대지를 깊이 꿰뚫었다고 전해진다.<end>"

## 800083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔剣ソルベンジュを手に入れたウルグが、<line-break>ソリアスの従者を倒した戦いを描いた作品。<line-break>ソルベンジュの黒き光は地を裂き、谷を作り、<line-break>従者は、その底で息絶えたと伝えられる。<end>"
EN: "A work depicting the battle in which Ulugh, having obtained the demonic sword Solvenge, defeated Solias's attendant. It is said that Solvenge's black light split the earth and carved out a valley, and that the attendant died at the bottom of the valley.<end>"
KO: "마검 솔벤주를 손에 넣은 울그가 솔리아스의 종자를 쓰러뜨린 전투를 그린 작품. 솔벤주의 검은 빛은 땅을 갈라 계곡을 만들었고, 종자는 그 밑바닥에서 숨을 거뒀다고 전해진다.<end>"

## 800084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec080-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦神ソリアスと破壊神ウルグの<line-break>２度目の戦いを描いた作品。<line-break>大地は震え、火を噴き、流れ出した溶岩は、<line-break>地を覆ったと伝えられる。<end>"
EN: "A work depicting the second battle between War God Solias and Ulugh, the God of Destruction. It is said that the earth shook and spewed fire, and the lava that poured forth covered the land.<end>"
KO: "전신 솔리아스와 파괴신 울그의 두 번째 싸움을 그린 작품. 대지가 흔들리고 불을 뿜으며 흘러나온 용암이 땅을 뒤덮었다고 전해진다.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 820001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec082-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここはフゴー様のお屋敷だ。<end>"
EN: "This is Lord Hugo's residence.<end>"
KO: "여기는 휴고 님의 저택이다.<end>"

## 820002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec082-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ああ、あなたは、あの有名な<line-break><value:$2B>の<value:$28>！<line-break>どうぞ、お通りください。<end>"
EN: "Y-you're <value:$28>, the famous <value:$2B>! Please, go right in.<end>"
KO: "아, 당신은 그 유명한 <value:$2B>의 <value:$28>! 지나가십시오.<end>"

## 820003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec082-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Who are you? Lord Hugo won't see some nobody off the street. Go home.<end>"
KO: "누구냐, 넌? 어디서 굴러온지도 모르는 놈을 휴고 님께서 만나실 리 없다. 돌아가.<end>"

## 830001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お待ちしておりました。<line-break><value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "We have been awaiting you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "기다리고 있었습니다, <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 830002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく来たのぉ。<line-break>ほんとは来てほしくなかったがのぉ。<end>"
EN: "Good of you to come. Truth be told, I was hoping you wouldn't.<end>"
KO: "잘 왔구먼. 사실 오지 않았으면 했지만 말이야.<end>"

## 830003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>そーですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho-ho-ho! Quite so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇군요~.<end>"

## 830004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フゴー様、そのようなことを<line-break>言われてはいけません。<line-break><line-break>やっとご先祖様のご遺言を<line-break>果たせるかもしれないのですから。<end>"
EN: "Lord Hugo, you mustn't say such things. We may finally be able to fulfill your ancestor's dying wish.<end>"
KO: "휴고 님, 그런 말씀을 하시면 안 됩니다. 드디어 선조님의 유언을 이룰 수 있을지도 모르니까요.<end>"

## 830005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とまどわれるのも無理はありません。<line-break>すこし、私の話をお聞きください。<end>"
EN: "Your confusion is perfectly understandable. Please, hear me out.<end>"
KO: "당황하시는 것도 무리는 아닙니다. 잠시 제 이야기를 들어 주세요.<end>"

## 830006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フゴー家の伝承に、こうあります。<line-break>　力・英知・勇気・博愛心、<line-break>　すべてを備えしものに、<line-break>　我が遺品ストラスエッジを与えよ<end>"
EN: "The Hugo family tradition says: “Give my heirloom, Stra's Edge, to one who possesses strength, wisdom, courage, and benevolence.”<end>"
KO: "휴고 가문의 전승에는 이렇게 적혀 있습니다. 힘·지혜·용기·박애심, 모든 것을 갖춘 자에게 나의 유품 스트라스 엣지를 주어라.<end>"

## 830007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私たちは、あなた様を<line-break>この遺言に値する人物と<line-break>見込んだのでございます。<end>"
EN: "We believe you are worthy of this bequest.<end>"
KO: "저희는 당신을 이 유언에 걸맞은 인물이라고 판단한 것입니다.<end>"

## 830008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この伝承を遺されたのは、<line-break>フゴー様のご先祖、ストラ＝フゴー様。<line-break>そして、ストラスエッジとは、<line-break>ストラ様のソウルが宿された<line-break>短剣でございます。<end>"
EN: "This tradition was left by Lord Hugo's ancestor, Lord Stra-Hugo. Stra's Edge is a dagger imbued with Lord Stra's Soul.<end>"
KO: "이 전승을 남기신 분은 휴고 님의 선조 스트라 휴고 님. 그리고 스트라스 엣지는 스트라 님의 소울이 깃든 단검입니다.<end>"

## 830009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たくさん珍しい品を<line-break>持ってきてくれたのぉ。<end>"
EN: "You've brought us a great many rare items.<end>"
KO: "희귀한 물건을 많이 가져왔구나.<end>"

## 830010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは、私たちに<line-break>英知を示してくれました。<end>"
EN: "You have shown us your wisdom.<end>"
KO: "당신은 우리에게 지혜를 보여 주었습니다.<end>"

## 830011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのタルテュバを<line-break>助けようとしたのぉ。<end>"
EN: "You tried to save Tartuba.<end>"
KO: "그 타르튜바를 구하려고 한 거야.<end>"

## 830012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、博愛心も。<end>"
EN: "And your benevolence as well.<end>"
KO: "그리고 박애심도.<end>"

## 830013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力と勇気ですか？<line-break>あなたがお持ちのルナシャドウと<line-break>ホワイトファングのソウルが、<line-break>力の半分を示しています。<end>"
EN: "Strength and courage, you ask? The Souls of Luna Shadow and White Fang in your possession are proof of half your strength.<end>"
KO: "힘과 용기인가요? 당신이 가진 루나 섀도와 화이트 팽의 소울이 힘의 절반을 나타내고 있습니다.<end>"

## 830014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、残りの力の半分と勇気は、<line-break>ここで示していただきます。<line-break>私たちと戦って、勝つことで。<end>"
EN: "You will demonstrate the other half of your strength and your courage here by fighting us and winning.<end>"
KO: "그리고 남은 힘의 절반과 용기는 여기서 보여 주십시오. 우리와 싸워 이기는 것으로.<end>"

## 830015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういうわけで勝負じゃのぉ。<line-break>あれは、いい短剣じゃからのぉ。<line-break>ほんとは、渡したくないんじゃのぉ。<end>"
EN: "So, a contest it is. That's a fine dagger, you know. Truth be told, I don't want to give it up.<end>"
KO: "그런 이유로 승부다. 저건 좋은 단검이거든. 사실 넘겨주고 싶지 않아.<end>"

## 830016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>そーですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho-ho-ho! Quite so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇군요~.<end>"

## 830017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃから、悪いが、<line-break>ここで倒しちゃおうかと<line-break>思っとるんじゃ。<end>"
EN: "So, sorry about this, but I think we'll defeat you here.<end>"
KO: "그래서 미안하지만 여기서 쓰러뜨릴까 생각 중이야.<end>"

## 830018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>そーですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho-ho-ho! Quite so.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 그렇군요~.<end>"

## 830019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、勝負です。<end>"
EN: "Then let the contest begin.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 승부입니다.<end>"

## 830020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むにゅー…。<end>"
EN: "Munyuu...<end>"
KO: "우뉴우….<end>"

## 830021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>むにゅー、ですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ho-ho-ho! Munyuu, indeed.<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 말랑말랑하군요~.<end>"

## 830022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見事です。あなたこそ、<line-break>ストラスエッジの所有者に<line-break>ふさわしいお方。<end>"
EN: "Splendid. You are truly worthy to possess Stra's Edge.<end>"
KO: "훌륭합니다. 당신이야말로 스트라스 엣지의 소유자에 어울리는 분입니다.<end>"

## 830023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "英雄ストラ＝フゴーのソウルが<line-break>封じられた短剣、<line-break>ストラスエッジを手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "Obtained Stra's Edge, a dagger containing the sealed Soul of the hero Stra-Hugo.<end>"
KO: "영웅 스트라=휴고의 소울이 봉인된 단검, 스트라스 엣지를 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 830024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>に<line-break>英雄ストラ＝フゴーのソウルが宿った。<end>"
EN: "The Soul of the hero Stra-Hugo now dwells within <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>에게 영웅 스트라=휴고의 소울이 깃들었다.<end>"

## 830025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むにゅー…。<end>"
EN: "Mnyuu...<end>"
KO: "우뉴우….<end>"

## 830026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほーっほっほっ、<line-break>むにゅー、ですわねー。<end>"
EN: "Ohohoho! Mnyuu, is it?<end>"
KO: "호호호호, 말랑말랑하군요~.<end>"

## 830027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec083-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、お強いですね。<line-break>まいりました。<end>"
EN: "Still, you are remarkably strong. I admit defeat.<end>"
KO: "하지만 정말 강하시군요. 졌습니다.<end>"

## 840001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう逃げられないからね。<line-break>あの娘をどこへやったか<line-break>言いなさいよ！<end>"
EN: "There's no getting away now. Tell me where you took that girl!<end>"
KO: "이제 도망칠 수 없어. 그 아이를 어디로 데려갔는지 말해!<end>"

## 840002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あん？　娘？<line-break>なんのことだ？<end>"
EN: "Huh? What girl? What are you talking about?<end>"
KO: "응? 딸? 무슨 소리야?<end>"

## 840003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へ～え、とぼけるつもり？<line-break>往生際が悪い男ってサイテー。<line-break>鎌を持つ手も震えるって。<end>"
EN: "Oh? Playing dumb, are we? Men who don't know when they're beaten are the worst. Makes my sickle hand twitch.<end>"
KO: "헤에~ 시치미 떼려는 거야? 미련한 남자는 최악이야. 낫 든 손도 떨린다니까.<end>"

## 840004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おわっ！<end>"
EN: "Whoa!<end>"
KO: "우왓!<end>"

## 840005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "正直に言ったほうが<line-break>いいんじゃなーい？<line-break>あたし、こう見えても短気だから。<end>"
EN: "Don't you think you'd better come clean? I may not look it, but I've got a short temper.<end>"
KO: "솔직하게 말하는 게낫지 않아? 나, 이래 보여도 성질이 급하거든.<end>"

## 840006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん。<line-break>いっそ、その方が気持ちイイし。<line-break>勝負しようじゃない。<end>"
EN: "Yeah. Actually, this'll feel better. Come on, let's settle this.<end>"
KO: "응. 차라리 그쪽이 속 시원하고. 승부하자.<end>"

## 840007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと、あんた！<line-break>何ボーっと見てんのよ！<end>"
EN: "Hey, you! What are you standing there gawking for?<end>"
KO: "잠깐, 너! 뭘 멍하니 보고 있어!<end>"

## 840008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、あんたのことよ。<line-break>こいつら人さらいなの。<line-break>さっさと手を貸しなさいよ。<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml

## 840011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 840012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml

## 840015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…それにしてもくやしいな。　　<line-break>一足遅かったか。<line-break>　<line-break>こいつの話じゃ、連れてこられた<line-break>女の子は、もうペウダのところに<line-break>運び込まれたってさ。<end>"

## 840016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 840017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "８月１１日修正予定：やーだなー。<line-break>勘違いやめてくれる？　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>城に忍び込んで助けるに<line-break>決まってんじゃない。<line-break>そーゆーの得意だったりするのよ。<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "8월 11일 수정 예정: 싫다 정말. 착각 좀 그만해 줄래? 성에 숨어들어 구하는 게 당연하잖아. 나 그런 거 꽤 잘하거든.<end>"

## 840018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうそう、あたしカルラ。　　　<line-break>一応、ディンガルの兵士なんだけど、<line-break>こうやって外をぶらつくのが任務なの。<line-break>…って言っても、人さらい追跡は<line-break>あたしの任務じゃないんだけどね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, right. I'm Karula. I'm technically a soldier of Dyneskal, but my assignment has me wandering around out here. Not that chasing kidnappers is actually part of it.<end>"
KO: "그래 그래, 난 카를라야. 일단 딘갈 병사인데 이렇게 밖을 돌아다니는 게 임무거든. …뭐, 유괴범 추적은 내 임무가 아니지만.<end>"

## 840024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、言い忘れてたけど、<line-break>ネメア様に負けてから<line-break>ペウダの奴、ビビっちゃってさ。<line-break>城の中に戦闘モンスターを<line-break>放してるんだってさ。<line-break>ま、気をつけていこーね！<end>"

## 840025

## 840035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんでそんなこと知ってるかって？<line-break>ちっちっち。ディンガルの情報網を<line-break>なめちゃいけないね。<line-break>ロセンの守りが堅いって言っても<line-break>抜け道のひとつやふたつくらい<line-break>調べはついてんの。<end>"

KO: "왜 그런 걸 아냐고? 쯧쯧쯧. 딘갈의 정보망을 얕보면 안 되지. 로센 방비가 단단해도 샛길 하나둘쯤은 이미 조사 끝났어.<end>"

## 840036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 840037

## 840042

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 840043

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 840044

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>4저 사람과 함께 가면 위험할 것 같고 같이 안 가도 돼서 다행일지도.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5젠자아앙! 왜 나한테 명령하는 거야! 게다가 왜 나는 한마디도 대꾸를 못 하는데!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6정말 제멋대로인 사람이네. …그래도 퇴로가 없어지면 확실히 곤란하지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12대단한 여자야. 확실히 퇴로 확보는 전투의 기본이지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…저 여자, 뭐라도 된 줄 아나….<end><if><value:$01><equal>10딩갈 병사인지 뭔지 모르겠지만 우리한테 명령 좀 안 했으면 좋겠어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14억지스러운 아가씨군. 머리는 잘 돌아가는 듯하지만.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15…대단한 사람이네. 혼자 결정하고 얼른 가 버렸어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17………………. 뭐, 뭐, 뭐야! 엘프인 이 몸을 상대로 그 태도. 절대로 용서 못 해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19가 버렸네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20……………….<end><if><value:$01><equal>11……………….<end><if><value:$01><equal>21……………….<end><if><value:$01><equal>23……………….<end><if><value:$01><equal>27…상당히 억지스러운 사람이네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26억지스러운 아가씨군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24……………….<end><if><value:$01><equal>25엄청난 기세… 아, 혼자 가 버렸어….<end><if><value:$01><equal>193뭔가 대단한 사람이네요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191제대로 퇴로를 확보하다니. 보기와 달리 머리가 잘 돌아가는 사람이네.<end>뭔가 대단한 사람을 따라와 버렸네.<end>"

## 840049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>4それじゃ、私はここで。<line-break>ご愁傷様。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5くやしいけど俺はここに残る。<line-break>あいつにカッコいいところ<line-break>見せつけてやれよ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>6気をつけて行ってよ。<line-break>あの人、何やらかすか<line-break>わからないから。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>12多少、強引だが、<line-break>間違ったことは言ってない。<line-break>まっ、気をつけて行ってこいや。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13早く行け。<line-break>ここは引き受けた。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>10もとはと言えば、姉ちゃんが　　　　<line-break>首突っ込むからいけないんだぞ！<line-break>　<line-break>まぁ、今に始まったことじゃないか。<line-break>…早く行ってこいよ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>14わしはここに残る。<line-break><value:$28>、気をつけてな。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>15でも悪い人じゃないみたい。<line-break><value:$28>、がんばってね。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>17どーして高貴なアタクシが<line-break>こーんな薄汚い地下道で<line-break>あんたたちを待ってなきゃいけないの？<line-break>いいわよ！　行きなさいよ！<line-break>もしアタクシの身に何かあったら<line-break>責任とってもらいますからね！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19でも、人さらいの王様なんて　　<line-break>許せないもん。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>、しっかりね。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>20早く行け。<line-break>俺はここを守る。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>11<value:$28>、気をつけて。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>21ここは引き受けた。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>23<value:$28>、ここは引き受けた。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>27<value:$28>、気をつけてな。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>26<value:$28>、気をつけてな。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>24ここは引き受けました。<line-break><value:$28>様、お気をつけて。<end><if><value:$01><equal>25じゃあ、ここに残るね<line-break>気をつけて行ってね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193それじゃあ私はここで待ってます。<line-break>気をつけてくださいね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191私はここを守ってるわ。<line-break>気をつけてね。<end>でも、人さらいなんて　　<line-break>キレイじゃないよ。<line-break>そんな王様なんて許せない。<line-break><value:$28>、しっかりね。 <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>4Well, I'll stay here. My condolences.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5I hate it, but I'll stay here. Go show her how impressive you can be.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6Be careful. There's no telling what that woman might do.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12She's a bit forceful, but she isn't wrong. Just be careful in there.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Get moving. I'll handle things here.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10This is all because you had to stick your nose into it in the first place! Well, that's nothing new. Go on, get moving.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14I'll remain here. Take care, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15She doesn't seem like a bad person, though. Do your best, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Why must someone as noble as I wait for you in this filthy underground passage? Fine! Go, then! But if anything happens to me, I shall hold you responsible!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19But I can't forgive a king who kidnaps people. Stay sharp, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20Get moving. I'll guard this place.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11Be careful, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21I'll handle things here.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23I'll handle things here, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Take care, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Take care, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24I'll take care of things here. <value:$28>, please be careful.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25I'll stay here, then. Be careful.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193Then I'll wait here. Please be careful.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191I'll guard this place. Be careful.<end>But kidnapping people is an ugly thing to do. I can't forgive a king like that. Stay sharp, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>4그럼 난 여기서. 애도를 표할게.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5분하지만 난 여기 남을게. 그 녀석한테 멋진 모습 제대로 보여줘.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6조심해서 가. 그 사람, 무슨 짓을 할지 모르니까.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12좀 막무가내긴 해도 틀린 말은 안 했어. 뭐, 조심해서 다녀와.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13빨리 가. 여긴 맡겨.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10애초에 누나가 끼어든 게 문제라고! 뭐, 하루 이틀 일도 아니지만. …빨리 다녀와.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14난 여기 남으마. <value:$28>, 조심하거라.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15그래도 나쁜 사람은 아닌 것 같아. <value:$28>, 힘내.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17어째서 고귀한 이 몸이 이 지저분한 지하도에서 너희를 기다려야 하는 거죠? 됐어요! 가세요! 이 몸한테 무슨 일이라도 생기면 책임지게 할 거니까요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19그래도 사람을 납치하는 왕이라니 용서 못 해. <value:$28>, 잘해.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20빨리 가. 난 여길 지킨다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11<value:$28>, 조심해.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21여긴 맡겨.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23<value:$28>, 여긴 맡겨.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27<value:$28>, 조심해.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26<value:$28>, 조심하거라.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24여긴 맡겠습니다. <value:$28>님, 조심하세요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25그럼 난 여기 남을게. 조심해서 다녀와.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193그럼 저는 여기서 기다릴게요. 조심하세요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191난 여길 지키고 있을게. 조심해.<end>그래도 사람을 납치하다니 아름답지 않네. 그런 왕은 용서 못 해. <value:$28>, 잘해.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 840056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 840057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml

## 840059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、ま、ま、待て！<line-break>余の話を聞け！<line-break>余はこの国の、こ、国王であるぞ！<end>"

KO: "자, 자, 잠깐! 짐의 말을 들어라! 짐은 이 나라의 구, 국왕이니라!<end>"

## 840061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、余は毎日<line-break>政務に追われ<line-break>神経をすり減らしておる。<line-break>余がいるからこそ、<line-break>ロセンという国がある。<line-break>ち、違うか？<end>"
EN: "E-every day, I am hounded by affairs of state until my nerves are worn raw. Rocen exists as a kingdom because of me. Am I w-wrong?<end>"
KO: "여, 여는 매일 정무에 쫓겨 신경이 닳고 있다. 여가 있기에 로센이라는 나라가 있는 것이다. 아, 아니냐?<end>"

## 840062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…それで？<end>"
EN: "And?<end>"
KO: "…그래서?<end>"

## 840063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その余が、民の１０人や２０人、<line-break>どうしようと勝手ではないか！<line-break>　<line-break>それで、余の気分が晴れるなら<line-break>構わんではないか！！<end>"
EN: "Then surely I may do as I please with ten or twenty of my subjects! What does it matter if it helps relieve my frustrations?!<end>"
KO: "그런 여가 백성 열 명이나 스무 명쯤 어찌하든 상관없지 않느냐! 그걸로 여의 기분이 풀린다면 됐지 않느냐!!<end>"

## 840064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Now I understand.<end>"
KO: "아~ 그렇구나.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…아, 알았어. …정말로?<end>"

## 840066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よーくわかったわよ。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あんたが、生きる価値もない<line-break>最低最悪の人間だってことがね！<end>"
EN: "Oh, I understand perfectly. You're the vilest kind of scum, and you don't deserve to live!<end>"
KO: "잘 알겠어. 네가 살아갈 가치도 없는 최악의 인간이라는 걸!<end>"

## 840067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひいっ！<end>"
EN: "Eek!<end>"
KO: "히익!<end>"

## 840069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "命まで取る必要はない？<line-break>何を甘ちゃんなことを<line-break>言ってんの。<line-break>こういう連中はヤれるときに<line-break>ヤっとかないと、泣きをみる<line-break>人が増えるだけだって！<end>"
EN: "You think taking a life is going too far? Don't be so naive. You have to take people like this out while you can, or more people will only end up suffering!<end>"
KO: "목숨까지 빼앗을 필요는 없다고? 무슨 물러터진 소리야. 이런 놈들은 처리할 수 있을 때 처리하지 않으면 우는 사람이 늘 뿐이야!<end>"

## 840070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どいて！！<line-break>邪魔をするなら…！<end>"

KO: "비켜!! 방해한다면…!<end>"

## 840071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イヒヒヒヒ！<end>"
EN: "Eeh heh heh heh!<end>"
KO: "이히히히!<end>"

## 840073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Ow...! Heeey! You two okay?<end>"
KO: "아야…! 여어. 둘 다 괜찮아?<end>"

## 840074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…は、はい。私は大丈夫です。<end>"
EN: "Y-yes. I'm all right.<end>"
KO: "…네, 네. 저는 괜찮습니다.<end>"

## 840075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イヒヒヒヒ、大丈夫だろうとも！<line-break>そこで死なれては<line-break>楽しみがなくなってしまうわ。<end>"
EN: "Ehehehehe! Of course you are! It would spoil all my fun if you died down there.<end>"
KO: "이히히히, 괜찮고말고! 거기서 죽어 버리면 재미가 없어지잖아.<end>"

## 840076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと、どーゆーことよ？<end>"
EN: "Wait, what are you talking about?<end>"
KO: "잠깐, 이게 무슨 일이야?<end>"

## 840077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さっきはよくも<line-break>余に冷や汗をかかせてくれたな。<line-break>だが、今度は貴様らの番だ。<line-break>ここは、用済みの娘を落として、<line-break>余のかわいいモンスターに<line-break>食わせるための落とし穴なのだ。<end>"
EN: "You had the gall to make me break out in a cold sweat earlier. But now it is your turn. This is where I cast girls who have outlived their usefulness, so that my darling monster may devour them.<end>"
KO: "아까는 감히 짐에게 식은땀을 흘리게 했겠다. 하지만 이번엔 네놈들 차례다. 여긴 쓸모없어진 계집을 떨어뜨려 짐의 귀여운 몬스터에게 먹이는 함정이다.<end>"

## 840078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "サイテー！　この悪趣味！<end>"
EN: "You're disgusting! What a sick bastard!<end>"
KO: "최악이야! 이런 악취미!<end>"

## 840079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イヒヒヒヒ…！<line-break>では、ひとつ楽しませてもらおう！<end>"
EN: "Ehehehehe...! Now, let the entertainment begin!<end>"
KO: "이히히히…! 그럼 한번 즐겨 볼까!<end>"

## 840080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…だんだん暗闇に目が<line-break>慣れてきたわね。<line-break>　<line-break>やーだなー。<line-break>見たくないものが<line-break>見えちゃったりするわけ？<end>"
EN: "My eyes are starting to adjust to the dark. Oh, great. Does that mean I'm about to see something I really don't want to?<end>"
KO: "…점점 어둠에 눈이 익숙해지네. 싫다~. 보고 싶지 않은 게 보이기라도 하는 거야?<end>"

## 840081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お互い甘ちゃんだよね～。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あたしを止めたあんた…。<line-break>あんたを倒してでも<line-break>奴を殺さなかったあたし…。<end>"
EN: "We're both too soft, aren't we? You were soft enough to stop me... and I was soft enough not to take you down and kill him anyway...<end>"
KO: "…우리 둘 다 물러터졌네~. 나를 말린 너…. 너를 쓰러뜨려서라도 그 녀석을 죽이지 못한 나….<end>"

## 840082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こうなったら、<line-break>スケベおやじのペットちゃんを<line-break>かわいがってあげるっきゃないね！<end>"
EN: "Well, I guess we'll just have to show that lecherous old man's little pet some affection!<end>"
KO: "이렇게 된 이상 그 호색한 아저씨의 애완동물이나 귀여워해 줄 수밖에 없겠네!<end>"

## 840083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ペウダの奴、今ごろ泡ふいて<line-break>ぶっ倒れてるわよ。<end>"
EN: "Peuda's probably foaming at the mouth and flat on his back by now.<end>"
KO: "페우다 녀석, 지금쯤 거품 물고 쓰러져 있을걸.<end>"

## 840084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…と言っても、<line-break>追っ手がかかるのは時間の問題。<line-break>急がないとね。<end>"
EN: "Still, it's only a matter of time before the pursuit begins. We'd better hurry.<end>"
KO: "…그렇다 해도 추격자가 붙는 건 시간문제야. 서둘러야 해.<end>"

## 840085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、大丈夫！？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, are you all right?!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 괜찮아!?<end>"

## 840086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "モンスターの声を聞いて<line-break>駆けつけたんだけど<line-break>…遅かったみたい。<line-break>ごめんなさい。<line-break>役に立たなくて。<end>"
EN: "I heard the monster and came running, but... it looks like I was too late. I'm sorry. I wasn't any help at all.<end>"
KO: "몬스터 소리를 듣고 달려왔는데… 늦은 것 같아. 미안해. 도움이 못 돼서.<end>"

## 840087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ううん、大丈夫。<line-break>まだ終わったわけじゃないから。<end>"
EN: "No, it's all right. This isn't over yet.<end>"
KO: "아니, 괜찮아. 아직 끝난 게 아니니까.<end>"

## 840088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？<end>"
EN: "What?<end>"
KO: "어?<end>"

## 840089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ペウダの奴、<line-break>追っ手をかけるはずよ。<line-break>さあ、急いで逃げるわよ。<end>"
EN: "Peuda's bound to have us pursued. Come on, we need to hurry and get out of here.<end>"
KO: "페우다 녀석, 추격자를 보낼 거야. 자, 서둘러 도망치자.<end>"

## 840090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

It looks like you're all right.<end><value:$28>, are you all right?!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>5<value:$28>, 괜찮아!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>12괜찮아, <value:$28>!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…아무래도 무사한 모양이군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10누나, 괜찮아!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14<value:$28>, 무사한가!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>20무사한 모양이군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11무사한 것 같네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23무사한 모양이군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27괜찮아, <value:$28>!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26<value:$28>, 무사한가!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>24<value:$28> 님, 무사하십니까?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25괜찮아!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193괜찮으세요, <value:$28> 씨!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191다행이야, 무사한 것 같네.<end><value:$28>, 괜찮아!?<end>"

## 840091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなところまで<line-break>駆けつけてくれるなんて、<line-break>心の通じ合った仲間は違うねー。<line-break>でも、なんか、あたしたちが<line-break>怪物を倒すのを待ってたような<line-break>タイミングだよね～。<end>"
EN: "Coming all the way down here for us―now that's what I call comrades who really understand each other. Though with that timing, it almost looks like you waited for us to beat the monster first.<end>"
KO: "이런 곳까지 달려와 주다니, 마음이 통하는 동료는 역시 다르네. 그런데 우리가 괴물을 쓰러뜨리길 기다렸던 것 같은 타이밍이네.<end>"

## 840092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>4そんなつもりは…<line-break>なくもないですけど。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>5なにおおおおお！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>6そんな言い方ないよ！<line-break>助けにきてやったんだぞ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>12わりぃ、わりぃ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13…なんだと？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>10そんな言い方ないだろ！<line-break>助けにきてやったんだぞ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>14どういう意味じゃ？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15そんな言い方ひどいよ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>17ちょっと、何よそれ！<line-break>それが助けにきてあげたアタクシに<line-break>言う言葉なの！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19そんな言い方ってないよぉ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>20…すまぬ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>11………………。 $<line-break><end><if><value:$01><equal>21…なんだと？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>23………………。 $<line-break><end><if><value:$01><equal>27…どういう意味かな？<end><if><value:$01><equal>26どういう意味じゃ？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>24…ごめんなさい。<end><if><value:$01><equal>25せっかく助けにきたのに<line-break>その言い方はないんじゃない？<end><if><value:$01><equal>193そんなことないですよ！<line-break>私、<value:$28>さんのことが<line-break>心配で走ってきたのに！<end><if><value:$01><equal>191せっかく助けに来たのに、<line-break>その言い方はないと思うんだけど…。<end>せっかく助けにきたのに<line-break>そんな言い方ってないよ。 <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>4I wouldn't say that was my intention... though I can't say it wasn't.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Why, youuuu!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6That's no way to talk to me! I came here to rescue you!<end><if><value:$01><equal>12Sorry, sorry.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13What did you say?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10That's no way to talk to me! I came here to rescue you!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14What do you mean by that?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15That's a horrible thing to say.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Excuse me?! Is that any way to speak to someone who came all this way to rescue you?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19That's no way to talk to me...<end><if><value:$01><equal>20Forgive me.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11...<end><if><value:$01><equal>21What did you say?<end><if><value:$01><equal>23...<end><if><value:$01><equal>27And what exactly do you mean by that?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26What do you mean by that?<end><if><value:$01><equal>24I'm sorry.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25I came all this way to help, and that's what you say to me?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193That's not true! I was worried about you, <value:$28>, so I came running!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191I came all this way to help, and I really don't think that's any way to talk to me...<end>I came all this way to help, and that's no way to talk to me.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>4그럴 생각은… 아주 없진 않지만요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5뭐라고오오오오!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6그런 말이 어디 있어! 구하러 와 줬잖아!<end><if><value:$01><equal>12미안, 미안.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…뭐라고?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10그런 말이 어디 있어! 구하러 와 줬잖아!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14무슨 뜻이냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15그런 말은 너무해.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17잠깐, 뭐야 그게! 그게 구하러 와 준 이 몸에게 할 말이야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19그런 말이 어디 있어어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…미안하네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11………………. $<end><if><value:$01><equal>21…뭐라고?<end><if><value:$01><equal>23………………. $<end><if><value:$01><equal>27…무슨 뜻일까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26무슨 뜻이냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>24…미안해요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25모처럼 구하러 왔는데 그런 말은 너무한 거 아냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193그렇지 않아요! 저, <value:$28> 씨가 걱정돼서 달려왔는데요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191모처럼 구하러 왔는데 그런 말은 너무한 것 같은데….<end>모처럼 구하러 왔는데 그런 말은 너무해.<end>"

## 840093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うそうそ。　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>すっごく助かっちゃった。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、安心するのはまだ早いわ。<line-break>ペウダの奴、追っ手をかけるはずよ。<end>"
EN: "I'm kidding. You were a huge help. But it's too soon to relax. Peuda's bound to have us pursued.<end>"
KO: "거짓말, 거짓말. 정말 큰 도움이 됐어. 그래도 안심하기엔 아직 일러. 페우다가 추격대를 보낼 거야.<end>"

## 840094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここで追っ手をくい止めるわ。<line-break>先行っててくんないかな？<end>"
EN: "I'll hold off our pursuers here. Could you go on ahead?<end>"
KO: "여기서 추격자들을 막을게. 먼저 가 줄래?<end>"

## 840095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっとちょっと、<line-break>反対行かないと、<line-break>またペウダの部屋に逆戻りよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, hey! We need to go the other way, or we'll wind up right back in Peuda's room.<end>"
KO: "잠깐, 반대로 가지 않으면 또 페우다의 방으로 되돌아가게 돼.<end>"

## 840131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰？<end>"
EN: "Who?<end>"
KO: "누구?<end>"

## 840132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーっ、<line-break><value:$28>じゃん。<line-break>　<line-break>久しぶり。死んでない？<end>"
EN: "Oh! It's <value:$28>. Long time no see. Still alive?<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>잖아. 오랜만이네. 안 죽었어?<end>"

## 840133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、冗談よ。ま、墓地だし、<line-break>もしかしてってこともあるじゃない？<line-break>　<line-break>でも、悪いんだけどさ～、<line-break>今、忙しいから、でっきれば<line-break>外してほしいかな～。<end>"
EN: "Heh, I'm kidding. But we are in a cemetery, so you never know, right? Sorry, but I'm a little busy at the moment. If you could make yourself scarce, that'd be great.<end>"
KO: "후훗, 농담이야. 뭐, 묘지니까 혹시라는 일도 있잖아? 그런데 미안하지만 지금 바빠서, 가능하면 빠져 줬으면 좋겠네~.<end>"

## 840134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴメーン。　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>でもさ、こんなとこで会えたんだから、<line-break>また、どっかで会えるよ。<line-break>そんときにはゆっくりね。<end>"
EN: "Sorry! But hey, if we ran into each other in a place like this, I'm sure we'll meet again somewhere. We can take our time then.<end>"
KO: "미안~. 그래도 이런 데서 만났으니 또 어디선가 만나겠지. 그때는 천천히 이야기하자.<end>"

## 840135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ、あんた。<line-break>悪いけど、どっか行ってくれる？<line-break>こーゆーの、人に見られたくないんだ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you. Sorry, but could you go somewhere else? I don't want anyone seeing this sort of thing.<end>"
KO: "아, 너구나. 미안한데 어디 좀 가 줄래? 이런 모습은 남에게 보이고 싶지 않아.<end>"

## 840136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、名前教えてよ。<line-break>ちょっと、気になるからさ。<line-break>　<line-break>あたしはカルラ。<line-break>これでもディンガルの軍人。<line-break>で、あんたは？<end>"
EN: "Oh, tell me your name. You've got me curious. I'm Karula. Believe it or not, I'm a Dyneskal soldier. And you are?<end>"
KO: "아, 이름 알려 줘. 좀 궁금하거든. 난 칼라. 이래 봬도 딘갈의 군인이야. 그래서 넌?<end>"

## 840137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、ね。<line-break>そんじゃね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, huh? See you around, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 그렇지. 그럼 안녕, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 840138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>また、あんた？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... You again?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 또 당신이야?<end>"

## 840139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何度も墓場に来るなんて、<line-break>ほんと、気持ち悪いわね。<end>"
EN: "You keep coming to the graveyard. It's really creepy.<end>"
KO: "몇 번이나 묘지에 오다니, 정말 기분 나쁘네.<end>"

## 840140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行ってくれない？<line-break>今日は日が悪くてイライラするの。<end>"
EN: "Would you go away? I'm having a bad day, and it's making me irritable.<end>"
KO: "가 줄래? 오늘은 운이 나빠서 짜증 나거든.<end>"

## 840141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪魔だわ。<line-break>悪いけど、行ってくれない。<line-break>今、忙しいの。<end>"
EN: "You're in the way. Sorry, but would you leave? I'm busy right now.<end>"
KO: "방해야. 미안하지만 가 줄래? 지금 바빠.<end>"

## 840142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アハハ、<value:$28>。<line-break>また来てくれたんだ。<end>"
EN: "Ha ha, <value:$28>. You came back!<end>"
KO: "아하하, <value:$28>. 또 와 줬구나.<end>"

## 840143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "墓場ってやーね。<line-break>なんで、こんなに静かなんだろ。<line-break>つい、ものを考えちゃうじゃない…。<end>"
EN: "Graveyards are awful. Why do they have to be so quiet? It gets you thinking about things...<end>"
KO: "묘지는 싫어. 왜 이렇게 조용한 걸까. 괜히 생각에 잠기게 되잖아….<end>"

## 840144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つい会いに来ちゃうのよね。<line-break>哀しくなるだけなのにさ。<line-break>ばっかみたいだよねー。<end>"
EN: "I can't help coming back here to visit, even though it only makes me sad. Pretty stupid, huh?<end>"
KO: "자꾸 만나러 오게 되네. 슬퍼질 뿐인데 말이야. 바보 같지.<end>"

## 840145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、悪いわね。<line-break>しんみりしちゃって。<line-break>じゃ、あたし行くわ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Sorry for getting all gloomy. Well, I'd better go.<end>"
KO: "후후, 미안해. 분위기가 처졌네. 그럼 난 갈게.<end>"

## 840146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>じゃない。<line-break>どうしたのよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't <value:$28>. What brings you here?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>잖아. 무슨 일이야?<end>"

## 840147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた墓場好きよねー。<line-break>ま、あたしも人のこと言えないけど。<end>"
EN: "You really like graveyards, don't you? Not that I'm one to talk.<end>"
KO: "당신 묘지 좋아하네. 뭐, 나도 남 말할 처지는 아니지만.<end>"

## 840148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これだけ人が死んだのかって<line-break>思うとすごいよねー。<line-break>　<line-break>死んじゃうと静かだよね。<line-break>沈黙はきれいだろうけど、<line-break>あたしは黙りたくない。<end>"
EN: "It's incredible to think this many people have died. Everyone's so quiet once they're dead. Silence may be beautiful, but I don't want to be silent.<end>"
KO: "이렇게 많은 사람이 죽었다고 생각하면 대단하지. 죽으면 조용하네. 침묵은 아름답겠지만 난 입 다물고 있고 싶진 않아.<end>"

## 840149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴメン、ちょっとさ…。<line-break>行ってくれるとうれしーかな。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, but... I'd kind of appreciate it if you left.<end>"
KO: "미안, 좀…. 가 줬으면 좋겠는데.<end>"

## 840150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホント、ゴメンね、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I'm really sorry, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "정말 미안해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 840151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>じゃない。<line-break>ホント墓場好きだよね、あんたってさ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, it's <value:$28>. You really do love graveyards, don't you?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>이잖아. 정말 묘지를 좋아한다니까, 너.<end>"

## 840152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーん、あんたって、　　　　　　<line-break>ひょっとして、<line-break>あたしのこと気になってる？<line-break>ま、墓場によくいる女だもんねー。<line-break>わけありに見えても<line-break>しゃーないやね。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. Don't tell me you're interested in me? Well, I am a woman who spends a lot of time in graveyards. Can't blame you for thinking I've got a past.<end>"
KO: "흐음, 너 혹시 나한테 관심 있어? 뭐, 묘지에 자주 있는 여자니까. 사연 있어 보인다고 생각해도 어쩔 수 없지.<end>"

## 840153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は？<line-break>どうして、あたしが軍人に<line-break>なったのかって？<line-break>…どーしても、聞きたいわけ？<end>"
EN: "Huh? You want to know why I became a soldier? You really have to know?<end>"
KO: "뭐? 왜 내가 군인이 됐냐고? …정말 그렇게 듣고 싶어?<end>"

## 840154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…同情したら、殺すよ？<end>"
EN: "Pity me and I'll kill you. Got it?<end>"
KO: "…동정하면 죽일 거야?<end>"

## 840155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、あんたってさー、<line-break>割とバカだよね。<line-break>いいわ。教えてあげる。<line-break>そのかわり、同情したら<line-break>ホントに殺すかんね？<end>"
EN: "Heh. You're kind of an idiot, you know that? Fine, I'll tell you. But if you pity me, I really will kill you. Got it?<end>"
KO: "후후, 너 말이야 꽤 바보지. 좋아, 알려 줄게. 대신 동정하면 정말 죽여 버릴 거야, 알았어?<end>"

## 840157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール王妃エリスや　　　　　　　<line-break>リベルダムの武器商人に<line-break>そそのかされてね。<line-break>あたしの村もロセン軍に略奪されたわ。<line-break>何もかも奪い尽くされ、<line-break>みんな殺されたわ…。<end>"
EN: "Queen Eris of Rostorl and the arms merchants of Liberdam put him up to it. The Rocen army pillaged my village too. They took everything and killed everyone.<end>"
KO: "로스톨 왕비 에리스와 리벨덤의 무기상에게 부추김을 당해서 말이야. 내 마을도 로센군에게 약탈당했어. 모든 걸 빼앗기고 모두 죽었지….<end>"

## 840158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "父さんも兄さんも友達も<line-break>妹も姉さんも母さんも…。<line-break>そして、好きだった人も、みんな。<line-break>あたしは納屋の隅で転がされて、<line-break>ただ、声も出さずに泣いてた。<line-break>ロセン兵のバカ笑いをみつめながら。<end>"
EN: "My father, my brother, my friends, my little sister, my older sister, my mother... and the person I loved. Everyone. I was thrown into a corner of a barn, and all I could do was cry without making a sound as I watched the Rocen soldiers laughing like idiots.<end>"
KO: "아버지도, 오빠도, 친구도, 여동생도, 언니도, 어머니도…. 그리고 좋아했던 사람도 모두. 나는 헛간 구석에 내던져져 아무 소리도 못 내고 울기만 했어. 로센 병사들의 낄낄거리는 웃음을 바라보면서.<end>"

## 840159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのとき、あたし、思ったんだ。<line-break>他のロセン兵もロセン王ペウダも<line-break>みんな、笑ってるんだろうなって。<line-break>ロストールの貴族も、<line-break>リベルダムの武器商人も、<line-break>みんな、みんな…。<end>"
EN: "And then I thought: the rest of the Rocen soldiers must be laughing too. King Peuda too. The nobles of Rostorl, the arms merchants of Liberdam―every last one of them.<end>"
KO: "그때 난 생각했어. 다른 로센 병사들도 로센 왕 페우다도 다 웃고 있겠지 하고. 로스톨의 귀족도, 리벨덤의 무기상도, 모두 모두….<end>"

## 840160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたし、その時、強烈に思ったんだ…。<line-break>いつでも、笑っていたいって。<line-break>　<line-break>だからね、<line-break>強くなくちゃいけないの。<line-break>誰よりもね…。<end>"
EN: "That thought struck me with such force... I wanted to keep laughing, always. That's why I have to be strong. Stronger than anyone...<end>"
KO: "나, 그때 강하게 생각했어… 언제나 웃고 싶다고. 그래서 강해야 해. 누구보다도…<end>"

## 840161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、誰にも負けちゃいけないのよ。<line-break>強くなきゃいけない。<line-break>だからかな、軍人になったのは。<end>"
EN: "That's right. I can't let anyone beat me. I have to be strong. Maybe that's why I became a soldier.<end>"
KO: "그래, 누구에게도 져선 안 돼. 강해야 해. 그래서였을까, 군인이 된 건.<end>"

## 840162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのことを確認しにくるのよ。<line-break>この冷たい死の静寂の中にね。<end>"
EN: "I come here to remind myself of that, amid the cold silence of the dead.<end>"
KO: "그걸 확인하러 오는 거야. 이 차가운 죽음의 정적 속으로.<end>"

## 840163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぅ、つまんない昔話はおしまい。<line-break>悪いね、不幸自慢みたいに<line-break>なっちゃって。<line-break>じゃ、バイバイ。<line-break>聞き上手の冒険者さん。<end>"
EN: "Whew. That's the end of that boring old story. Sorry. That sounded like I was bragging about how miserable my life was. Well, bye-bye, adventurer. You sure know how to listen.<end>"
KO: "후, 시시한 옛날이야기는 끝. 미안하네, 불행 자랑처럼 돼 버려서. 그럼 안녕, 이야기 잘 들어 주는 모험자 씨.<end>"

## 840173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　ディンガル帝国の東、　　　　　　<line-break>　　　バイアシオン内海に面した地方には　　　<line-break>　　　　　ロセン王国をはじめとする　　　　　<line-break>　　東方６カ国と呼ばれる小国家群があった。　<end>"
EN: "East of the Dyneskal Empire, along the shores of the Vyashion Inland Sea, lay a group of small states known as the Six Eastern Nations, among them the Kingdom of Rocen.<end>"
KO: "딘갈 제국의 동쪽, 바이아시온 내해에 접한 지방에는 로센 왕국을 비롯한 '동방 6개국'이라 불리는 작은 국가들이 있었다.<end>"

## 840174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "皇帝ネメアの命により　　　　<line-break>東方諸国攻略の任に就いた　　　　<line-break>ディンガルの青竜将軍カルラは、　　　<line-break>この国々をわずか数週間で滅ぼした。　　　<end>"
EN: "At Emperor Nemea's command, Dyneskal's Azure Dragon General Karula was charged with conquering the eastern nations. Within mere weeks, she had destroyed them all.<end>"
KO: "황제 네메아의 명을 받아 동방 제국 공략 임무를 맡은 딩갈의 청룡장군 카를라는 이 나라들을 불과 몇 주 만에 멸망시켰다.<end>"

## 840175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンの暗愚王ペウダは、カルラの力を<line-break>過小評価したため、その才能と実力を<line-break>戦場で思い知ることとなった。<line-break>敗北と自らの死によって…。<end>"
EN: "Peuda, the foolish king of Rocen, underestimated Karula's power and learned the true extent of her talent and prowess on the battlefield―through defeat and his own death...<end>"
KO: "로센의 암군 페우다는 칼라의 힘을 과소평가했고, 전장에서 그 재능과 실력을 뼈저리게 깨닫게 되었다. 패배와 자신의 죽음으로….<end>"

## 840176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラがこれらの国々に対して取った<line-break>制圧後の態度は徹底しており、　　<line-break>特にロセン王家に対しては、　　<line-break>子供、老人に至るまでその血を根絶した。<line-break>これ以降、人々は青竜将軍カルラを指し、<line-break>畏敬を込めて神速の戦術家と、あるいは<line-break>恐怖を込めて、青い死神と呼んだ。　<end>"
EN: "Karula was utterly ruthless in her treatment of these nations after their conquest. In particular, she exterminated every member of Rocen's royal bloodline, including children and the elderly. From then on, people referred to Azure Dragon General Karula with awe as the Strategist of Godlike Speed, or with terror as the Blue Reaper.<end>"
KO: "카를라가 이 나라들을 제압한 뒤 취한 태도는 철저했다. 특히 로센 왕가에 대해서는 아이와 노인까지 그 혈통을 모조리 끊었다. 이후 사람들은 청룡장군 카를라를 경외를 담아 신속의 전술가라 부르거나, 공포를 담아 푸른 사신이라 불렀다.<end>"

## 840177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここはロセン王国である！<line-break>現在、ペウダ国王陛下の命により<line-break>一切の人の出入りが禁じられている！<line-break>お前らを通すわけにはいかん。<line-break>立ち去れ！<end>"
EN: "This is the Kingdom of Rocen! By order of His Majesty King Peuda, no one is permitted to enter or leave! You will not be allowed through. Leave at once!<end>"
KO: "여기는 로센 왕국이다! 현재 페우다 국왕 폐하의 명으로 모든 사람의 출입이 금지돼 있다! 너희를 통과시킬 수 없다. 물러가라!<end>"

## 840178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、あの方はおひとりで<line-break>大丈夫なのでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "Thank you. But will that woman be all right on her own?<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 하지만 그분 혼자서 괜찮으실까요?<end>"

## 840179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとか抜け出したわね。<end>"
EN: "We made it out somehow.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 빠져나왔네.<end>"

## 840180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。<line-break>すべて、あなた方のおかげです。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. It's all thanks to you.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 모두 여러분 덕분입니다.<end>"

## 840181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな…、私たちは　　　　　　<line-break>カルラさんに力を貸しただけ。<line-break>　<line-break>カルラさん、大丈夫なのかな…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, no... We only helped Karula. I wonder if she'll be all right...<end>"
KO: "그럴 수가… 우린 카를라 씨를 도왔을 뿐인데. 카를라 씨, 괜찮은 걸까….<end>"

## 840182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>4Whew, looks like we made it.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6We should be safe here.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15We made it out somehow.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Honestly, what a terrible ordeal.<end><if><value:$01><equal>19We should be all right here, shouldn't we?<end><if><value:$01><equal>22We should be all right here, shouldn't we?<end>Looks like we made it out.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 840186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml

## 840187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 840188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "네, 네. 이 근처 마을에 부모님과 남동생이….<end>"

## 840189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、家族と一緒に　　<line-break>今すぐディンガルへ逃げて。<line-break>　<line-break>奴らに家を知られている以上、<line-break>村にいるのは危険だわ。<end>"
EN: "Then take your family and flee to Dyneskal right now. They know where you live, so staying in the village would be dangerous.<end>"
KO: "그럼 가족과 함께 지금 당장 딘갈로 도망쳐. 녀석들이 집을 아는 이상 마을에 있는 건 위험해.<end>"

## 840190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 840191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 840192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、急いで。<line-break>ペウダの奴、逆上してるから<line-break>何するかわからないわよ。<end>"

KO: "자, 서둘러. 페우다가 흥분했으니 무슨 짓을 할지 몰라.<end>"

## 840193

## 840195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml

## 840196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、いーのいーの。<line-break>宝石なんてなくても<line-break>思い出は残るから。<line-break>でも、あの娘には<line-break>あれが必要だもん。<line-break>…でしょ？<end>"

## 840197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…っと、いけない。　　　　<line-break>任務に戻らなきゃ！<line-break>　<line-break>今日はありがと。<line-break><value:$28>のおかげで<line-break>助かっちゃった。<end>"

## 840198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 840199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml

## 840201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 840204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml

## 840205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 840207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私もいつか、カルラさんみたいな<line-break>りっぱな騎士になりたい！<line-break>そう思ってるんです。<end>"

## 840208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

That's worth quite a lot, isn't it?<end><if><value:$01><equal>21That was an extraordinarily valuable thing to be carrying. You didn't take it from the castle, did you?<end><if><value:$01><equal>23That jewel... You were carrying something quite valuable.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27I'm surprised you had a jewel like that.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26You had a jewel on you.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24It was a very beautiful jewel. Why were you carrying something like that?<end><if><value:$01><equal>25I was surprised you had such a beautiful jewel.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193It was such a beautiful jewel... I was so surprised!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191That surprised me. That jewel is worth a great deal.<end>Did you see that, <value:$28>? That was a pretty jewel. Was it really yours?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>4이 사람이 비싼 보석을 들고 다닌다니 좀 의외일지도….<end><if><value:$01><equal>5굉장하네. 통도 크다니까. 저거 진짜야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>6저렇게 예쁜 보석, 줘 버려도 괜찮아?<end><if><value:$01><equal>12그 보석… 성에서 훔쳐 왔다고 하진 않겠지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13네 같은 여자가 보석을 들고 다니다니….<end><if><value:$01><equal>10그런데 그 보석은 어디서 난 거야? 설마 성에서 훔쳐 왔다거나….<end><if><value:$01><equal>14참 통도 크구나. 아니, 설마 그 보석… 장물은 아니겠지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>15보석 같은 걸 잘도 갖고 계셨네요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17그나저나 당신 같은 여자가 그런 보석을 갖고 있다니, 별일도 다 있네요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19저렇게 예쁜 보석을 갖고 있다니, 루루안타 깜짝 놀랐어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20그 돌… 상당히 비싼 물건이군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11예쁜 보석…. 저거, 꽤 비싼 물건이겠지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21상당히 비싼 물건을 갖고 있었군. 설마 성에서 가져온 건 아니겠지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>23그 보석… 상당히 비싼 물건을 갖고 있었군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27보석 같은 걸 잘도 갖고 계셨네요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26보석 같은 걸 들고 다니고 있었나.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24정말 아름다운 보석이네요. 그런 보석을 왜 들고 다니고 있었나요?<end><if><value:$01><equal>25깜짝 놀랐어. 저렇게 예쁜 보석을 갖고 있다니.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193정말 예쁜 보석…. 저, 깜짝 놀랐어요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191놀랐어. 그 보석, 상당한 가치가 있어.<end><value:$28>, 봤어? 꽤 예쁜 보석이었어. 저거 정말 네 거야?<end>"

## 840210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なーによ、　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>私が宝石持ってたら<line-break>おかしいっての？<line-break>こんな格好でもね、<line-break>母親の形見くらい持ってんのよ。<end>"
EN: "What? Is it so strange for me to have a jewel? I may dress like this, but I do have a keepsake from my mother.<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 내가 보석 갖고 있으면 이상해? 이런 차림이어도 엄마의 유품 정도는 가지고 있거든.<end>"

## 840211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

Are you sure?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Are you sure you should give it away so easily?<end>No way! Are you sure you should give away something that precious?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>4그렇게 소중한 걸 생판 남한테 주다니 너무 통 큰 거 아냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5어이, 괜찮은 거야? 그렇게 소중한 걸 줘 버리고.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6괜찮아? 그렇게 소중한 걸….<end><if><value:$01><equal>12…미안, 그런 거였나.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…유품을 줘 버린 건가. 고작 마을 처녀를 위해서.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10어이, 괜찮아? 그렇게 소중한 걸….<end><if><value:$01><equal>14그렇게 소중한 걸…!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15어, 그렇게 소중한 걸….<end><if><value:$01><equal>17이 여자 무슨 생각인 거야? 그렇게 소중한 걸 쉽게 남에게 넘기다니!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…유품인가.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11그래, 유품이었구나…<end><if><value:$01><equal>21괜찮은가? 그렇게 소중한 걸….<end><if><value:$01><equal>23…유품인가.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27유품….<end><if><value:$01><equal>26그렇게 소중한 것을…<end><if><value:$01><equal>24괜찮으신가요? 그렇게 소중한 것을…<end><if><value:$01><equal>25어, 그렇게 소중한 걸….<end><if><value:$01><equal>193그렇게 소중한 걸요!? 괜찮으세요!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191그렇게 쉽게 줘도 괜찮아?<end>거짓말! 그렇게 소중한 걸 줘 버려도 돼?<end>"

## 840212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、いーのいーの。<line-break>宝石なんかなくても<line-break>思い出は残るから。<line-break>でも、あの娘には<line-break>あれが必要だもん。<line-break>…でしょ？<end>"
EN: "No, no, it's fine. I don't need a jewel to remember her. But that girl needed it. Right?<end>"
KO: "아, 괜찮아 괜찮아. 보석이 없어도 추억은 남으니까. 하지만 그 아이에겐 그게 필요하잖아. …그렇지?<end>"

## 840213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヤバっ！<line-break>そろそろ任務に戻らなきゃ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, damn! I need to get back to my mission.<end>"
KO: "큰일! 이제 임무로 돌아가야 해.<end>"

## 840214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日はありがと！<line-break>あんたたちのおかげで<line-break>助かっちゃった。<end>"
EN: "Thanks for today! You all really saved me.<end>"
KO: "오늘 고마웠어! 너희 덕분에 살았어.<end>"

## 840215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また会おうね！<line-break>今度は歴史の大舞台でさ。<end>"
EN: "See you again! Next time, it'll be on history's grand stage.<end>"
KO: "또 만나자! 다음엔 역사의 큰 무대에서.<end>"

## 840216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よっし、追いついた。<line-break>さ、早く逃げましょ。<end>"
EN: "All right, I've caught up. Come on, let's hurry and get out of here.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 따라잡았다. 자, 빨리 도망치자.<end>"

## 840217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、ちゃうちゃう。<line-break>ペウダの部屋は<line-break>そっちじゃないって。<end>"
EN: "No, no, wrong way. Peuda's room isn't over there.<end>"
KO: "아, 아니야 아니야. 페우다의 방은 그쪽이 아니야.<end>"

## 840218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっとちょっと、<line-break>また戻ってどうすんの。<line-break>　<line-break>しっかりしてよね、<line-break>非常時なんだから。<end>"
EN: "Hey, what are you doing going back again? Pay attention―this is an emergency.<end>"
KO: "잠깐, 또 돌아가서 어쩌려고. 정신 차려, 비상사태잖아.<end>"

## 840219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec084-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぅ～ん。<line-break>どうもこのカルラ様は信用できない<line-break>ってな感じの動きよねぇ。<line-break>でも、今はそんなこと<line-break>言ってる場合じゃないんじゃない？<line-break>ほら、さっさとペウダのとこ行くよ！<end>"
EN: "Hmm. Looks like you're acting as though you can't trust Lady Karula here. But this really isn't the time for that, is it? Come on, let's hurry over to Peuda!<end>"
KO: "흐음~. 아무래도 이 카를라 님은 믿을 수 없다는 느낌의 움직임이네. 그래도 지금 그런 말 할 때는 아니지 않아? 자, 얼른 페우다에게 가자!<end>"

## 850001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "早く！<line-break>総督府に知らせてください！<end>"
EN: "Hurry! Please notify the Governor-General's Office!<end>"
KO: "빨리! 총독부에 알려 주세요!<end>"

## 850002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受ける<end>"
EN: "Accept<end>"
KO: "수락한다<end>"
