jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けない<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "맡지 않는다<end>"

## 850004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんな！　こんな時に？<end>"
EN: "N-no! At a time like this?<end>"
KO: "그, 그런! 이런 때에?<end>"

## 850005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何？　それは本当か？<end>"
EN: "What? Is that true?<end>"
KO: "뭐? 그게 사실인가?<end>"

## 850006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ブゾリは賄賂、陰謀、やり放題だ。<line-break>誰も、ロセンではブゾリに逆らえない。<end>"
EN: "Buzori bribes and schemes with impunity. No one in Rocen can stand against Buzori.<end>"
KO: "부조리는 뇌물, 음모, 뭐든 제멋대로야. 로센에서는 아무도 부조리에게 거역하지 못해.<end>"

## 850007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、ブゾリは有能だぞ。<line-break>あの破壊されたロセンをここまで<line-break>あっと言う間に復興させた。<end>"
EN: "But Buzori is capable. Just look how far Buzori brought devastated Rocen in so little time.<end>"
KO: "하지만 부조리는 유능해. 저렇게 파괴된 로센을 순식간에 이만큼 복구했으니까.<end>"

## 850008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ブゾリはペウダが傾けたロセン経済を<line-break>あっと言う間に立て直したしな。<line-break>愚王ペウダよりは実績がある分マシだ。<end>"
EN: "Buzori also turned Rocen's economy around in no time after Peuda brought it to the brink. At least there are results to show for it―more than can be said for that fool King Peuda.<end>"
KO: "부조리는 페우다가 기울인 로센 경제를 눈 깜짝할 사이에 다시 세웠으니까. 어리석은 왕 페우다보다는 실적이 있는 만큼 낫지.<end>"

## 850009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカ言え。<line-break>ペウダより有能な分、始末が悪いんだ。<line-break>俺たちはブゾリの家畜みたいなもんだ。<end>"
EN: "Don't talk nonsense. The fact that Buzori is more capable than Peuda only makes matters worse. We're little more than Buzori's livestock.<end>"
KO: "바보 같은 소리 마. 페우다보다 유능한 만큼 더 골치 아픈 놈이야. 우리는 부조리의 가축이나 마찬가지야.<end>"

## 850010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "物騒じゃな～い？　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>その体に巻いた魔法爆雷を広場で<line-break>爆発させたらアタシや<line-break>そこの女の子まで死んじゃうでしょ？<end>"
EN: "Isn't that a little dangerous? If you set off those magical blasting charges wrapped around your body here in the square, you'll kill me and that girl over there too, won't you?<end>"
KO: "위험하지 않아~? 몸에 두른 마법 폭뢰를 광장에서 터뜨리면 나랑 저 여자아이까지 죽잖아?<end>"

## 850011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死にたいなら<line-break>ひとりで死んでちょうだい？<line-break>　<line-break>アタシには、あんたのとは<line-break>比べものにならない、<line-break>すばらしーい人生があるの。<end>"
EN: "If you want to die, please do it alone. I have a simply marvelous life, nothing like yours.<end>"
KO: "죽고 싶으면 혼자 죽어 줘? 나한텐 네 것과는 비교도 안 되는 멋진 인생이 있으니까.<end>"

## 850012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何がお望みかしら？<end>"
EN: "And what might you want?<end>"
KO: "뭘 원하시나요?<end>"

## 850013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセンの解放だ。<line-break>ディンガルの豚め！<end>"
EN: "The liberation of Rocen, you Dyneskal swine!<end>"
KO: "로센 해방이다. 딩갈의 돼지 놈들!<end>"

## 850014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人にものを頼むときは、<line-break>もう少し、お行儀よくするものよ？<end>"
EN: "When you ask someone for a favor, you really ought to mind your manners.<end>"
KO: "남에게 부탁할 때는 조금 더 예의 바르게 해야지?<end>"

## 850015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ペウダ王の仇にそんな真似ができるか。<end>"
EN: "How could I show courtesy to King Peuda's enemy?<end>"
KO: "페우다 왕의 원수에게 그런 짓을 할 수 있겠나.<end>"

## 850016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ペウダ王、ねぇ？　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>聞いて。そのペウダ王は酒色に溺れ、<line-break>国民を苦しめてたの。<line-break>町はさびれ、悲惨な状況だったわ…。<end>"
EN: "King Peuda, you say? Listen. That King Peuda wallowed in drink and women while his people suffered. The town had fallen into decay. It was a wretched place...<end>"
KO: "페우다 왕이라…? 들어 봐. 그 페우다 왕은 주색에 빠져 백성을 괴롭혔어. 도시는 쇠락하고 비참한 상태였지…<end>"

## 850017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それをアタシがピンクの<line-break>かわいい町にしたの。<line-break>感謝されても、殺される覚えはないわ。<end>"
EN: "I turned it into a cute little pink town. I deserve thanks, not death.<end>"
KO: "그걸 내가 분홍색의 귀여운 마을로 만든 거야. 감사받을 일은 있어도 죽임당할 이유는 없어.<end>"

## 850018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うるさい！<line-break>いつか貴様らディンガルを殺し尽くす。<line-break>そしてロセン王家がこの国を再興するのだ！<end>"
EN: "Shut up! One day, you Dyneskals will be slaughtered to the last! Then the royal house of Rocen will restore this country!<end>"
KO: "시끄러워! 언젠가 너희 딘갈 놈들을 모조리 죽여 버리겠다. 그리고 로센 왕가가 이 나라를 다시 세울 것이다!<end>"

## 850019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロセン王家…？　カルラ様が<line-break>根絶やしにしたはずだけど？　　　<line-break>　<line-break>もしかして、愚王ペウダの叔母が<line-break>嫁いでいたリベルダムの武器商人<line-break>ロティの娘、クリュセイスのこと？<end>"
EN: "The royal house of Rocen...? Lady Karula was supposed to have wiped it out. Unless you mean Khryuseis, daughter of Lottie, the Liberdam arms merchant who married that fool King Peuda's aunt?<end>"
KO: "로센 왕가…? 카를라 님이 씨를 말렸을 텐데? 혹시 우왕 페우다의 숙모가 시집간 리벨덤의 무기상 로티의 딸, 크류세이스를 말하는 거야?<end>"

## 850020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ！<end>"
EN: "That's right!<end>"
KO: "맞아!<end>"

## 850021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "武器商人は暴力で王の地位を買う気ね。<line-break>本当にいい犬を手に入れたこと。<line-break>理想を信じ、命を何とも思わない。<end>"
EN: "So that arms merchant means to buy a throne with violence. What a fine dog he's found: one who believes in ideals and thinks nothing of life.<end>"
KO: "무기상인은 폭력으로 왕의 자리를 사려는 거군. 정말 좋은 개를 손에 넣었네. 이상을 믿고 목숨 따윈 아무렇지도 않게 생각하는.<end>"

## 850022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "殺せ！<end>"
EN: "Kill!<end>"
KO: "죽여!<end>"

## 850023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言われなくてもそうするわ。<line-break>こないだの爆破もあんたたちでしょ？<end>"
EN: "I hardly need you to tell me that. You lot were behind the last bombing too, weren't you?<end>"
KO: "말 안 해도 그렇게 할 거야. 지난번 폭파도 너희 짓이지?<end>"

## 850024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ。これで終わったと思うな！<line-break>ブゾリ！<end>"
EN: "We were. Don't think this ends here, Buzori!<end>"
KO: "그래. 이걸로 끝났다고 생각하지 마라! 부조리!<end>"

## 850025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゲスねぇ。美しくないわ。<line-break>命はもっと尊いものよん。<end>"
EN: "How vulgar. Not beautiful at all. Life is far more precious than that.<end>"
KO: "천박하네. 아름답지 않아. 목숨은 훨씬 소중한 거야.<end>"

## 850026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "パレードを続けるわよ。<line-break>死体は片付けて。美しくないわ。<end>"
EN: "We'll carry on with the parade. Clear away the bodies. They're not beautiful.<end>"
KO: "퍼레이드를 계속해. 시체는 치워. 아름답지 않잖아.<end>"

## 850027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん<line-break>…ですね。<end>"
EN: "You must be <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨… 맞죠.<end>"

## 850028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたの名声を聞いて、<line-break>ぜひ、頼みたいことがあってやってきました。<line-break>むろん報酬はたっぷりと、させてもらいます。<line-break>暗殺…<line-break>引き受けてもらえませんかね？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I've heard of your fame, and I've come with a request. You would, of course, be handsomely rewarded. An assassination... Would you be willing to take it on?<end>"
KO: "당신의 명성을 듣고 꼭 부탁드리고 싶은 일이 있어 찾아왔습니다. 물론 보수는 넉넉히 드리죠. 암살… 맡아 주시겠습니까?<end>"

## 850029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "興味がないわけじゃない。話だけ聞いてみる<end>"
EN: "I'm not saying I'm uninterested. I'll listen, but that's all.<end>"
KO: "관심이 없는 건 아니야. 이야기만 들어 보지<end>"

## 850030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "断る<end>"
EN: "Decline.<end>"
KO: "거절한다<end>"

## 850031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、詳しいお話はロセン総督府で…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>それでは失礼…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "We'll discuss the details at the Rocen Governor-General's Office. If you'll excuse me.<end>"
KO: "자세한 이야기는 로센 총독부에서…. 그럼 실례하겠습니다….<end>"

## 850032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。では、失礼。<end>"
EN: "Very well. If you'll excuse me.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까. 그럼 이만.<end>"

## 850033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様、<line-break>お待ちしておりました。<end>"
EN: "Welcome, <value:$28>. You were expected.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님, 기다리고 있었습니다.<end>"

## 850034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、こちらへ…。<end>"
EN: "Right this way, please.<end>"
KO: "자, 이쪽으로….<end>"

## 850035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こちらが依頼主。<line-break>ロセン総督ブゾリ様です。<end>"
EN: "This is the client, Governor-General Buzori of Rocen.<end>"
KO: "이분이 의뢰인, 로센 총독 부조리 님입니다.<end>"

## 850036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃーい。<line-break>最近、売り出し中の冒険者、<line-break><value:$28>ちゃんね？<line-break>アタシはブゾリ、<line-break>ロセンの総督をやってるわ。<end>"
EN: "Welcooome! You're that up-and-coming adventurer, <value:$28>, aren't you? I'm Buzori, Governor-General of Rocen.<end>"
KO: "어서 와~. 요즘 이름을 날리는 모험자, <value:$28> 맞지? 나는 부조리, 로센 총독이야.<end>"

## 850037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふふ、けっこう、<line-break>かわいい顔してるじゃなーい？<end>"
EN: "My, my. Aren't you quite the pretty one?<end>"
KO: "후후, 꽤 귀여운 얼굴이잖아?<end>"

## 850039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうかしら？<end>"
EN: "What do you say?<end>"
KO: "어때?<end>"

## 850040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受ける<end>"
EN: "Accept<end>"
KO: "수락한다<end>"

## 850041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けない<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "맡지 않는다<end>"

## 850042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、裏切らないように<line-break>これに血で署名して…。<end>"
EN: "Then sign this in blood, so I know you won't betray me...<end>"
KO: "그럼 배신하지 않도록 여기에 피로 서명해….<end>"

## 850043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは前金。<end>"
EN: "Here's your advance.<end>"
KO: "이건 선금이야.<end>"

## 850044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロティを死体にしたら<line-break>来てちょうだい。<line-break>そのとき残りを払うわ。　　　　　　<line-break>急いだ方がいいわよ。<line-break>アタシも忙しいから、あなたひとりに<line-break>頼んでるわけじゃないから。<end>"
EN: "Come back when Lottie's dead. I'll pay you the rest then. You'd better hurry. I'm busy too, so you're not the only one I've hired.<end>"
KO: "로티를 시체로 만들고 오면 돼. 그때 나머지 돈을 줄게. 서두르는 게 좋아. 나도 바쁘니까 너 하나한테만 맡긴 게 아니거든.<end>"

## 850045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあね。<end>"
EN: "See you.<end>"
KO: "그럼 안녕.<end>"

## 850046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残念だわ…。それじゃ、<line-break>ここでアタシに約束してくれるかしら？<line-break>　<line-break>アタシがあの武器商人を殺すのを<line-break>邪魔しないって。<end>"
EN: "What a shame... Then will you promise me, here and now, that you won't interfere when I kill that arms dealer?<end>"
KO: "아쉽네…. 그럼 여기서 나한테 약속해 줄래? 내가 그 무기상인을 죽이는 걸 방해하지 않겠다고.<end>"

## 850047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "邪魔しない<end>"
EN: "I won't interfere.<end>"
KO: "방해하지 않는다<end>"

## 850048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "絶対阻止してみせる<end>"
EN: "I'll stop it, no matter what.<end>"
KO: "반드시 저지하겠어<end>"

## 850049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "素直ね。いい生き方だわ。<line-break>損しないわよ。　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>フフ…だけど、人の言うなりに動いて、<line-break>人の思うままに踊らされて、<line-break>それで生きてるって言えるのかしら？<end>"
EN: "So obedient. It's a good way to live. You won't lose out. Heh... But if you only do as you're told and let others pull your strings, can you really call that living?<end>"
KO: "솔직하네. 좋은 삶의 방식이야. 손해 볼 일은 없어. 후후… 하지만 남이 시키는 대로 움직이고, 남이 원하는 대로 춤추면서 그걸 살아간다고 할 수 있을까?<end>"

## 850050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かめさせて？<line-break>あなたの生きている意味を。<end>"
EN: "Let me see for myself what gives your life meaning.<end>"
KO: "확인하게 해 줘. 네가 살아 있는 의미를.<end>"

## 850051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見てるだけじゃなくって、実際に<line-break>体を動かしてみるのも悪くないでしょ。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、出てって。<line-break>あなたの生き方、美しくないわ。<end>"
EN: "Watching is one thing, but trying it for yourself wouldn't be so bad, would it? Still, get out. There's no beauty in the way you live.<end>"
KO: "보기만 하는 게 아니라 직접 몸을 움직여 보는 것도 나쁘지 않잖아. 하지만 나가 줘. 네 삶의 방식은 아름답지 않아.<end>"

## 850052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ、元気いいのねぇ。<line-break>気に入ったわ。<line-break>だから、いいこと教えてあげる。　<line-break>ロセンではねぇ、<line-break>アタシに逆らうものはいないわ。<line-break>アタシに逆らうことは命がけなの。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. You've got spirit. I like that, so I'll let you in on something. No one in Rocen defies me. Defy me, and you stake your life on it.<end>"
KO: "후후후, 기운이 넘치네. 마음에 들었어. 그러니까 좋은 걸 알려줄게. 로센에서는 나한테 거스르는 사람은 없어. 나한테 거스른다는 건 목숨을 거는 일이거든.<end>"

## 850053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "命がけで守るに値するモノなんて<line-break>何もないわ。フフフ、行きなさい。<line-break>別の者を頼むから。<line-break>あなたは夢を見るといい。<line-break>愚か者の夢を。夢から覚めたなら<line-break>こっちへいらっしゃい。<end>"
EN: "Nothing is worth risking your life to protect. Heh heh heh. Go on. I'll ask someone else. You can dream your fool's dream. When you wake from it, come back to me.<end>"
KO: "목숨 걸고 지킬 가치가 있는 건 아무것도 없어. 후후후, 가렴. 다른 사람에게 부탁할 테니. 넌 꿈을 꾸면 돼. 어리석은 자의 꿈을. 꿈에서 깨어나면 이쪽으로 오렴.<end>"

## 850054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（…ブゾリの刺客？）<end>"
EN: "(...An assassin sent by Buzoli?)<end>"
KO: "(…부조리의 자객?)<end>"

## 850055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "き、君は…？<end>"
EN: "Y-you are...?<end>"
KO: "너, 너는…?<end>"

## 850056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、お前は、<value:$28>！<line-break>よ、よくも、ロセンではアニキたちを！<line-break>　<line-break>アニキたちはお前との戦いのあと<line-break>ぎっくり腰になって、<line-break>すげえ腰痛にうめいているんだ！<end>"
EN: "Y-you're <value:$28>! How dare you, after what you did to the guys in Rocen! They threw their backs out fighting you, and now they're laid up groaning in agony!<end>"
KO: "너, 너는 <value:$28>! 로센에서 형님들을 그렇게 만들어 놓고! 형님들은 너와 싸운 뒤 허리를 삐끗해서 심한 요통에 신음하고 있다고!<end>"

## 850057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ついでだ。<line-break>その仇、取らせてもらう。<end>"
EN: "And while I'm here, I'll avenge them.<end>"
KO: "마침 잘됐다. 그 원수, 내가 갚겠다.<end>"

## 850058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "く、くそっ！<end>"
EN: "D-damn it!<end>"
KO: "제, 젠장!<end>"

## 850059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あ、ありがとう。<line-break>おかげで助かった。<end>"
EN: "Ah... thank you. You saved me.<end>"
KO: "…고, 고마워. 덕분에 살았어.<end>"

## 850060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お父様？　今の騒ぎは？<end>"
EN: "Father? What was that commotion?<end>"
KO: "아버님? 방금 소란은 뭐죠?<end>"

## 850061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、クリュセイスか…。刺客だ。<line-break>だが、この方に助けられたよ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, Khryuseis... An assassin attacked me, but this person saved me.<end>"
KO: "아아, 크류세이스인가… 자객이었어. 하지만 이분이 구해 줬지.<end>"

## 850062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは…。ありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "An assassin...? Thank you.<end>"
KO: "그건…. 감사합니다.<end>"

## 850063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は<value:$28>君だね。<line-break>最近、一躍有名になった冒険者だ。<end>"
EN: "You're <value:$28>, aren't you? The adventurer who's suddenly become so famous.<end>"
KO: "자네가 <value:$28>군이군. 최근 단숨에 유명해진 모험자지.<end>"

## 850064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん…。<line-break>本当にありがとうございます。<line-break>お怪我はありませんか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Thank you, truly. Are you hurt?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨…. 정말 감사합니다. 다친 곳은 없으세요?<end>"

## 850065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、やはり、<line-break>ロセン総督ブゾリのさしがねか…。<end>"
EN: "So Buzoli, the governor of Rocen, was behind it after all...<end>"
KO: "그런가, 역시 로센 총독 부조리의 사주였나….<end>"

## 850066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前の母さんの一族を皆殺しにした<line-break>カルラの懐刀とも言われる男だ。<line-break>　<line-break>重い税で、ロセンを趣味の悪い<line-break>リベルダムのまがい物に変え、<line-break>リベルダムに対抗している。<end>"
EN: "He's said to be the right-hand man of Karula, the woman who slaughtered your mother's entire clan. He has burdened Rocen with heavy taxes, turning it into a tasteless imitation of Liberdam to rival the city.<end>"
KO: "네 어머니 일족을 몰살한, 칼라의 오른팔이라고도 불리는 남자다. 무거운 세금으로 로센을 취향 나쁜 리벨덤의 모조품으로 바꾸고 리벨덤에 맞서고 있지.<end>"

## 850067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "クリュセイス…。<line-break>今、ロセンの人々にとっては　　　<line-break>お前だけが希望の光なのだ。<end>"
EN: "Khryuseis... Right now, you are the only ray of hope the people of Rocen have.<end>"
KO: "크류세이스…. 지금 로센 사람들에게는 너만이 희망의 빛이다.<end>"

## 850068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>君、<line-break>どうか、私の願いを聞いてくれ。<line-break>　<line-break>今、私はロセンの解放軍の<line-break>支援をしている。ロセンの人々を<line-break>ディンガルから解放するためだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, please hear my request. I am supporting Rocen's Liberation Army so that its people may be freed from Dyneskal.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 군, 부디 내 부탁을 들어 주게. 지금 나는 로센 해방군을 지원하고 있어. 로센 사람들을 딘갈에서 해방하기 위해서다.<end>"

## 850069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "正直、総督ブゾリの悪逆さのために<line-break>状況は芳しくない。<line-break>　<line-break>どうだろう。君ほどの者が<line-break>協力してくれれば、士気も高まり、<line-break>ブゾリにも対抗できると思うのだが…。<end>"
EN: "To be frank, Buzoli's atrocities have left us in a poor position. But with someone of your caliber helping us, morale would rise, and I believe we could stand against him. What do you say?<end>"
KO: "솔직히 총독 부조리의 악행 때문에 상황은 좋지 않다. 자네 같은 사람이 협력해 준다면 사기도 오르고 부조리에게도 맞설 수 있을 것 같은데…<end>"

## 850070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無論、解放軍の支援はきれいごとだけが<line-break>理由じゃない。ディンガルの魔手が<line-break>リベルダムに及ばないためでもある。<end>"
EN: "Of course, noble ideals are not my only reason for supporting the Liberation Army. I also mean to keep Dyneskal's grasp from reaching Liberdam.<end>"
KO: "물론 해방군을 지원하는 이유가 이상적인 명분뿐인 건 아니다. 딩갈의 마수가 리벨덤까지 뻗지 못하게 하기 위해서이기도 하지.<end>"

## 850071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルの将軍カルラは非情な女だ。<line-break>王家の人間を老人まで処刑したという。<line-break>　<line-break>あの女は戦火に踏みにじられた<line-break>人間の苦しみなどを知らないのだ。<line-break>人間の痛みがわからないのだ。<end>"
EN: "Karula, Dyneskal's general, is a merciless woman. They say she executed even the elderly members of the royal family. She knows nothing of the suffering of those trampled beneath the fires of war. She cannot understand another person's pain.<end>"
KO: "딘갈의 장군 카를라는 비정한 여자야. 왕가 사람들을 노인까지 처형했다더군. 그 여자는 전쟁에 짓밟힌 사람들의 고통을 몰라. 인간의 아픔을 모르는 거야.<end>"

## 850072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はリベルダムの市民として、<line-break>そんな奴らにリベルダムが、<line-break>蹂躙されるのを黙視できない…。　　　<line-break>そんな奴らに<line-break>ロセンが抑圧されているのも<line-break>看過できないのだ。<end>"
EN: "As a citizen of Liberdam, I cannot stand by and watch such people lay waste to my city. Nor can I ignore Rocen's oppression at their hands.<end>"
KO: "나는 리벨덤 시민으로서 그런 놈들에게 리벨덤이 유린당하는 걸 묵과할 수 없어…. 그런 놈들에게 로센이 억압받는 것도 보고만 있을 수 없다.<end>"

## 850073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "頼む、<line-break>私たちに力を貸してくれたまえ。　　　<line-break><value:$28>君。<end>"
EN: "Please, <value:$28>. Lend us your strength.<end>"
KO: "부탁하네. 우리에게 힘을 빌려주게. <value:$28> 군.<end>"

## 850074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。ありがとう。君のような<line-break>同志を得られて心強い限りだ。<line-break>　<line-break>では、また、来てくれたまえ。<line-break>そのとき、詳しい話をしよう。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. It is deeply reassuring to have a comrade such as you. Come back again, and we will discuss the details then.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 고맙다. 자네 같은 동지를 얻어 든든하군. 그럼 또 와 주게. 그때 자세히 이야기하지.<end>"

## 850075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。…しょせん、君たちのような　<line-break>自由人にはわからないのだな。<line-break>　<line-break>自分の故郷に、住んでいる町に<line-break>侵略者の魔の手が伸びようが、<line-break>君らはそんなことはどうでもよいのだ。<end>"
EN: "I see... In the end, free spirits like you cannot understand. It means nothing to you if an invader's grasp closes around your homeland or the town where you live.<end>"
KO: "그런가. …결국 너희 같은 자유인들은 이해 못 하는군. 자기 고향에, 살고 있는 마을에 침략자의 마수가 뻗어와도 너희에겐 아무래도 좋은 거겠지.<end>"

## 850076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君らはどこか別の所へ<line-break>行けばいいだけだからな。　　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "You can simply move on to somewhere else.<end>"
KO: "너희는 그냥 다른 곳으로 가면 될 뿐이니까.<end>"

## 850077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は君のことを<line-break>過大評価していたようだ。<line-break>　<line-break>これは礼金だ。<line-break>ありがとう。<line-break>さぁ、行きたまえ。<end>"
EN: "It seems I thought too highly of you. Here is your reward. Thank you. Now go.<end>"
KO: "내가 너를 과대평가했던 모양이군. 이건 사례금이다. 고맙다. 자, 가 봐라.<end>"

## 850079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気がついたか？　ちょうどいい。<line-break>今ロティも片づいたところだ。<line-break>お前も今から地獄に送ってやる。<end>"
EN: "Awake, are you? Perfect timing. I've just finished off Lottie. Now I'll send you to hell too.<end>"
KO: "정신 차렸나? 마침 잘됐군. 방금 로티도 처리한 참이다. 너도 지금부터 지옥으로 보내 주마.<end>"

## 850080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お父様…？<line-break>ものすごい物音がしましたが。<end>"
EN: "Father...? I heard a terrible crash.<end>"
KO: "아버님…? 엄청난 소리가 났는데요.<end>"

## 850081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっ、人が来やがったか。<line-break>運のいい奴だ。<end>"
EN: "Tch. Someone's coming. Lucky bastard.<end>"
KO: "쳇, 누가 왔나. 운 좋은 녀석이군.<end>"

## 850082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪いことは言わねえ、<line-break>ぬれぎぬがイヤなら早く逃げることだ。　<line-break>　<line-break>リベルダムじゃな、<line-break>非市民の言うことなんて無視される。<line-break>お前が犯人じゃないって言っても無駄だ。<end>"
EN: "Take my advice: if you don't want to be framed, get out of here fast. In Liberdam, no one listens to a non-citizen. It won't matter how much you protest your innocence.<end>"
KO: "나쁜 말 안 할 테니 누명 쓰기 싫으면 빨리 도망쳐. 리벨덤에선 비시민이 하는 말 따윈 무시돼. 네가 범인이 아니라고 해도 소용없어.<end>"

## 850083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お父様？<end>"
EN: "Father?<end>"
KO: "아버님?<end>"

## 850084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "き、君は…？<end>"
EN: "Y-you are...?<end>"
KO: "너, 너는…?<end>"

## 850085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お父様？<end>"
EN: "Father?<end>"
KO: "아버님?<end>"

## 850086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様でありますね。<line-break>総督から伺っております。<line-break>どうぞ、お通りください！<end>"
EN: "You are <value:$28>, correct? The governor said to expect you. Please, go right in!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님이시군요. 총독께 들었습니다. 지나가십시오!<end>"

## 850087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃーい。<line-break>待ってたわ！　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>嬉しいじゃない？<line-break>あのテロの親玉を殺してくれたなんて！<line-break>あ、これ、謝礼ね。<end>"
EN: "Welcome! I've been waiting for you! Isn't it wonderful? You killed that terrorist ringleader! Oh, and here's your reward.<end>"
KO: "어서 와~. 기다렸어! 기쁘지 않아? 그 테러 우두머리를 죽여 줬다니! 아, 이건 사례금이야.<end>"

## 850088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたって、できるのねー。<line-break>アタシ有能な人ってシビレちゃうの。<line-break>美しいって思うわ。<line-break>どう、アタシの下で働かない？<line-break>仕事はちょっとダーティでハードよ。<line-break>でも、やりがいはあるわ。<end>"
EN: "You really are good. Competence gives me such a thrill. It's beautiful. How would you like to work for me? The work is a little dirty and hard, but it's rewarding.<end>"
KO: "당신 꽤 능력 있네. 난 유능한 사람 보면 짜릿하거든. 아름답다고 생각해. 어때, 내 밑에서 일하지 않을래? 일은 좀 더럽고 힘들어. 그래도 보람은 있지.<end>"

## 850089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、さっそく、<line-break>仕事の説明にかかるわね。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ゾフォルの予言を知ってる？<line-break>魔王バロルに仕えた妖術宰相ゾフォル。<line-break>彼には予言能力があったの。<end>"
EN: "Then let's get straight to business. Do you know Zofor's prophecy? Zofor was the Sorcerous Chancellor who served Balor, Demon King. He had the gift of prophecy.<end>"
KO: "그럼 바로 일 설명을 시작할게. 조포르의 예언을 알아? 마왕 발로르를 섬긴 요술 재상 조포르. 그에게는 예언 능력이 있었어.<end>"

## 850090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今までにいくつもの予言をして　　　　<line-break>そのすべてが当たってるのよ。<line-break>　<line-break>一番有名なのは、魔王バロルが<line-break>バロルの娘の子に殺されるって<line-break>予言したことね。<end>"
EN: "He made many prophecies, and every one came true. The most famous foretold that Balor, Demon King, would be killed by his daughter's child.<end>"
KO: "지금까지 수많은 예언을 했고 전부 맞았어. 가장 유명한 건 마왕 발로르가 발로르의 딸의 아이에게 죽는다고 예언한 거지.<end>"

## 850091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バロルは予言の成就を避けようとした。<line-break>けれど、結局、孫に当たるネメア様に<line-break>殺されちゃったわ。<end>"
EN: "Balor tried to prevent the prophecy from coming true, but in the end, his own grandson, Nemea, killed him.<end>"
KO: "발로르는 예언의 성취를 피하려 했어. 하지만 결국 손자인 네메아 님에게 죽었지.<end>"

## 850092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのゾフォルが予言してるのよ。<line-break>ザギヴがディンガルを支配するって。<line-break>　<line-break>ザギヴなんて変な名前、<line-break>アタシ、あの規則規則の巨乳女の<line-break>他に聞いたことがないわ。<end>"
EN: "And Zofor prophesied that Zagiv would rule Dyneskal. Zagiv is such an odd name, and I've never heard of anyone else with it but that rule-obsessed woman with the enormous breasts.<end>"
KO: "그 조포르가 예언했어. 자기브가 딩갈을 지배한다고. 자기브처럼 이상한 이름은 나, 그 규칙 타령하는 거유 여자 말고는 들어 본 적 없어.<end>"

## 850093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼女、今、ディンガルの内政を<line-break>統括してるし、あの予言、<line-break>一応当たったことになるわよね。　　<line-break>おおかた、予言を恐れた<line-break>ベルゼーヴァあたりが、<line-break>そうしたんだと思うのよ。<end>"
EN: "She oversees Dyneskal's internal affairs now, so technically the prophecy has already come true. I imagine someone like Beelzeva put her there out of fear of it.<end>"
KO: "그 여자 지금 딘갈 내정을 총괄하고 있으니 그 예언도 일단 맞은 셈이지. 아마 예언을 두려워한 벨제바 같은 녀석이 그렇게 한 것 같아.<end>"

## 850094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だけど、そんなことでは<line-break>予言は終わらないって思うわけ。<end>"
EN: "But I don't believe the prophecy ends there.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그런 걸로 예언이 끝날 거라곤 생각하지 않아.<end>"

## 850095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、オカマのカンよ。<line-break>ザギヴはディンガルの皇帝になるわ。<line-break>　<line-break>ただ、ザギヴがずっと<line-break>皇帝でいられるかについては<line-break>ゾフォルは予言してないの。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Call it a queen's intuition. Zagiv will become emperor of Dyneskal. But Zofor never said how long she'd remain emperor.<end>"
KO: "후후, 오카마의 감이야. 자기브는 딘갈 황제가 될 거야. 다만 자기브가 계속 황제로 있을지는 조포르도 예언하지 않았어.<end>"

## 850096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこで、ね。　　　　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Which brings me to my point.<end>"
KO: "그래서 말인데.<end>"

## 850097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたを、ザギヴの次の<line-break>皇帝にしてあげようと思うわけ。<end>"
EN: "I intend to make you the next emperor after Zagiv.<end>"
KO: "당신을 자기브 다음 황제로 만들어 주려는 거야.<end>"

## 850098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたって、<line-break>結構イイ線いってるのよ。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>バロル帝の子でありながら、<line-break>バロル帝に刃向かって死んだ<line-break>悲劇の光皇太子ルグの遺児…。<end>"
EN: "You have the makings of it: the surviving child of the tragic Crown Prince of Light, Lug, Emperor Balor's own son, who died defying his father...<end>"
KO: "당신, 꽤 좋은 선을 타고 있어. 발로르 황제의 자식이면서 발로르 황제에게 맞서다 죽은 비운의 광황태자 루그의 유복자….<end>"

## 850099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう言っても通用すると思うわ。<line-break>ねえ、民衆はそういう英雄を<line-break>待ち望んでるわ。皇帝にならない？　<line-break>アタシは子供作る気ないもの、<line-break>ただ、一生ぜいたくさせてくれたら、<line-break>あとはあなたの好きにして結構よ。<end>"
EN: "I think people would believe that story. The masses are longing for just such a hero. Why not become emperor? I have no intention of having children. Keep me in luxury for the rest of my life, and you may do whatever you please.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 말해도 통할 거야. 있잖아, 민중은 그런 영웅을 기다리고 있어. 황제가 되지 않을래? 난 아이를 낳을 생각은 없으니까, 평생 사치만 하게 해 주면 그 뒤는 네 마음대로 해도 좋아.<end>"

## 850100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どお？<line-break>ディンガルの皇帝になってみない？　<end>"
EN: "Well? Wouldn't you like to become emperor of Dyneskal?<end>"
KO: "어때? 딘갈의 황제가 되어 보지 않을래?<end>"

## 850101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、そのためにあなたにまず、<line-break>やって欲しいことがあるの。<line-break>ザギヴをたらし込んで欲しいのよ。<end>"
EN: "But first, there's something I need you to do. I want you to seduce Zagiv.<end>"
KO: "그래서 우선 네가 해 줬으면 하는 일이 있어. 자기브를 꼬셔 줬으면 해.<end>"

## 850102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫。<line-break>あなたならできるわ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>それにザギヴみたいな女、<line-break>結構ぬくもりに飢えてたりするのよ。<line-break>フフ。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry. You can do it. A woman like Zagiv may be quite starved for warmth, you know. Heh.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아. 당신이라면 할 수 있어. 게다가 자기브 같은 여자도 의외로 온기를 그리워할 때가 있거든. 후후.<end>"

## 850103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "別に恋仲になれとか<line-break>結婚しろって言うわけじゃないのよ。<line-break>あの女の心のスキマに入り込むだけ。<end>"
EN: "I'm not telling you to become her lover or marry her. Just slip into the empty place in her heart.<end>"
KO: "딱히 연인이 되라거나 결혼하라는 건 아니야. 그 여자의 마음 틈새로 파고들기만 하면 돼.<end>"

## 850104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むろん、若いころは美しく、<line-break>恋の狩人とも言われたこのアタシが、<line-break>細かーく指示してあげるわ。<end>"
EN: "Naturally, I'll tell you exactly what to do. I was beautiful in my youth and known as a hunter of hearts, after all.<end>"
KO: "물론 젊을 때 아름다워서 사랑의 사냥꾼이라고도 불렸던 이 내가 아주 세세하게 지시해 줄게.<end>"

## 850105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた、アタシが仕組んで<line-break>解放軍にザギヴを襲わせたとき…<line-break>ザギヴを助けてるわよねえ。<end>"
EN: "When I arranged for the Liberation Army to attack Zagiv... you rescued her, didn't you?<end>"
KO: "너, 내가 꾸며서 해방군이 자기브를 습격하게 했을 때… 자기브를 구해 줬지?<end>"

## 850106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいのいいの、あんな奴ら、捨て駒よ。<line-break>それよりもザギヴの信頼を<line-break>あなたが勝ち得たことの方が大事だわ。<line-break>この調子でザギヴに取り入るの。<line-break>こまめにザギヴのいる<line-break>エンシャントの政庁に出入りしてね。<end>"
EN: "It's fine, it's fine. Those people were expendable. What matters is that you won Zagiv's trust. Keep ingratiating yourself with her. Visit the Ancient Government Hall often while she's there.<end>"
KO: "됐어 됐어, 그런 녀석들은 버리는 패야. 그보다 자기브의 신뢰를 네가 얻었다는 게 더 중요해. 이대로 자기브에게 잘 보여. 자기브가 있는 엔샨트 정청에 자주 드나들고.<end>"

## 850107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "特に、何か大変事が起こったときは、　<line-break>何をさしおいても<line-break>彼女のそばにいてちょうだい。<line-break>まずは彼女を皇帝に据えること。<line-break>いいわね。<end>"
EN: "Whenever something momentous happens, drop everything and stay by her side. First, make her emperor. Understood?<end>"
KO: "특히 큰일이 생기면 무엇보다 그녀 곁에 있어 줘. 우선 그녀를 황제로 세우는 것. 알겠지.<end>"

## 850108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた、ザギヴの好感度は<line-break>かなり高いわよねえ。あの女、<line-break>あなたのことを結構、信頼してるわよ。<line-break>あの女は古代の闇の怪物の殲滅に<line-break>入れ込んでいるみたいだから。<line-break>その仕事をこなして点数を稼ぐのよ。<end>"
EN: "Zagiv seems to like you very much. She trusts you quite a bit. She also appears obsessed with exterminating the ancient monsters of darkness, so complete those jobs and win more favor.<end>"
KO: "너, 자기브의 호감도가 꽤 높지? 그 여자, 널 제법 믿고 있어. 그 여자는 고대의 어둠 괴물 섬멸에 열중하는 것 같으니 그 일을 해치워 점수를 따는 거야.<end>"

## 850109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうそう、近いうちにあの女、<line-break>ウチを調べるっ言ってたわね。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>解放軍に襲わせて、<line-break>適当にごまかしちゃおうと<line-break>思ってたんだけど、使えるわ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, yes. She said she'd be investigating us soon. I was going to have the Liberation Army attack her and use the confusion to cover things up, but this could prove useful.<end>"
KO: "그래, 조만간 그 여자가 우리를 조사하겠다고 했지. 해방군이 습격하게 해서 적당히 얼버무리려 했는데, 쓸 만하겠어.<end>"

## 850110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの女が東方方面を調べるからって<line-break>護衛を依頼してきたら、必ず受けるの。<line-break>きっとあなたの株が上がるわ。<end>"
EN: "If she asks you to escort her while she investigates the eastern region, be sure to accept. That should raise her opinion of you.<end>"
KO: "그 여자가 동쪽 지역을 조사한다며 호위를 의뢰해 오면 꼭 맡아. 분명 네 평판이 올라갈 거야.<end>"

## 850111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた、ザギヴの受けがイイ感じよ。<line-break>このまま、がんばって<line-break>あの巨乳女に近づきなさい。<line-break>まず、エンシャントの墓地にいる　　<line-break>あの女に声をかけること。<line-break>そして…<end>"
EN: "You're making a good impression on Zagiv. Keep at it and get closer to that big-breasted woman. First, speak to her at the cemetery in Ancient. Then...<end>"
KO: "당신, 자기브한테 꽤 호감 사는 것 같아. 이대로 계속 그 가슴 큰 여자에게 접근해. 우선 엔샨트 묘지에 있는 그 여자에게 말을 걸어. 그리고…<end>"

## 850112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの女が入れ込んでいる古代の怪物の<line-break>殲滅の仕事をギルドで請け負って、<line-break>その仕事の数こなすのよ。<end>"
EN: "Take jobs from the Guild to exterminate the ancient monsters she's so obsessed with, and complete as many as you can.<end>"
KO: "그 여자가 심취해 있는 고대 괴물 섬멸 일을 길드에서 맡아서 그 일을 여러 번 해내는 거야.<end>"

## 850113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴはエンシャントの墓地の<line-break>自分の一族の墓によく出かけているわ。<line-break>見つけたら、積極的に話しかけて。<line-break>あのコ、人とのつきあい方を知らない<line-break>頭デッカチちゃんだから、結構ムッと<line-break>するだろうけど、ここはがまんよ。<end>"
EN: "Zagiv often visits her family grave in the cemetery at Ancient. If you see her there, make a point of speaking to her. She's a stuffy little intellectual who doesn't know how to deal with people, so she may get rather annoyed. You'll just have to endure it.<end>"
KO: "자기브는 엔샨트 묘지에 있는 자기 일족의 무덤에 자주 가. 찾으면 적극적으로 말을 걸어. 그 애는 사람 사귀는 법을 모르는 머리만 큰 애라 꽤 짜증 나겠지만, 여기선 참아.<end>"

## 850114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あとねえ。ティラの娘と呼ばれる<line-break>古代の闇の怪物退治に<line-break>あの女、異様な執念を燃やしてるのよ。<line-break>ギルドでその仕事を受けて、<line-break>その仕事をこなすのよ。<line-break>いつか、あの女の信頼が得られるわ。<end>"
EN: "One more thing. She's bizarrely obsessed with destroying the ancient monster of darkness known as the Daughter of Tira. Take the job at the Guild and see it through. Eventually, you'll earn her trust.<end>"
KO: "그리고 말이야. 티라의 딸이라 불리는 고대의 어둠 괴물 퇴치에 그 여자가 이상할 정도로 집착하고 있어. 길드에서 그 일을 받아서 해내는 거야. 그러면 언젠가 그 여자의 신뢰를 얻을 수 있을 거야.<end>"

## 850115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "頼りにしてるわよ<end>"
EN: "I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "믿고 있을게<end>"

## 850116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーら、残念ねーえ？<line-break>ま、いいわ。<line-break>アタシ、待ってるから。　<line-break>その気になったら<line-break>いつでも来てちょうだい。<end>"
EN: "Oh, what a shame. Well, never mind. I'll be waiting, so come back whenever you change your mind.<end>"
KO: "어머, 아쉽네? 뭐, 좋아. 난 기다릴 테니까 마음이 내키면 언제든 와.<end>"

## 850117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハァーイ、こちらを総督府の前まで<line-break>送ってあげてちょうだい？<end>"
EN: "Hellooo! Would you escort this person to just outside the governor's office?<end>"
KO: "네, 이쪽 분을 총독부 앞까지 데려다줘.<end>"

## 850118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様でありますね。<line-break>総督から伺っております。<line-break>どうぞ、お通りください！<end>"
EN: "You are <value:$28>, correct? The governor said to expect you. Please, go right in!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님이시군요. 총독께 들었습니다. 지나가십시오!<end>"

## 850119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃーい。<line-break>きっと来ると思ってたわ。<line-break>アタシの下で、働く気になったのね？<end>"
EN: "Welcome! I knew you'd come. So you've decided to work for me?<end>"
KO: "어서 와. 분명 올 줄 알았어. 내 밑에서 일할 생각이 든 거지?<end>"

## 850120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、さっそく、<line-break>仕事の説明にかかるわね。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ゾフォルの予言を知ってる？<line-break>魔王バロルに仕えた妖術宰相ゾフォル。<line-break>彼には予言能力があったの。<end>"
EN: "Then let's get straight to business. Do you know Zofor's prophecy? Zofor was the Sorcerous Chancellor who served Balor, Demon King. He had the gift of prophecy.<end>"
KO: "그럼 바로 일 설명을 시작할게. 조포르의 예언을 알아? 마왕 발로르를 섬긴 요술 재상 조포르. 그에게는 예언 능력이 있었어.<end>"

## 850121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今までにいくつもの予言をして　　　　<line-break>そのすべてが当たってるのよ。<line-break>　<line-break>一番有名なのは、魔王バロルが<line-break>バロルの娘の子に殺されるって<line-break>予言したことね。<end>"
EN: "He made many prophecies, and every one came true. The most famous foretold that Balor, Demon King, would be killed by his daughter's child.<end>"
KO: "지금까지 수많은 예언을 했고 전부 맞았어. 가장 유명한 건 마왕 발로르가 발로르의 딸의 아이에게 죽는다고 예언한 거지.<end>"

## 850122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バロルは予言の成就を避けようとした。<line-break>けれど、結局、孫に当たるネメア様に<line-break>殺されちゃったわ。<end>"
EN: "Balor tried to prevent the prophecy from coming true, but in the end, his own grandson, Nemea, killed him.<end>"
KO: "발로르는 예언의 성취를 피하려 했어. 하지만 결국 손자인 네메아 님에게 죽었지.<end>"

## 850123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのゾフォルが予言してるのよ。<line-break>ザギヴがディンガルを支配するって。<line-break>　<line-break>ザギヴなんて変な名前、<line-break>アタシ、あの規則規則の巨乳女の<line-break>他に聞いたことがないわ。<end>"
EN: "And Zofor prophesied that Zagiv would rule Dyneskal. Zagiv is such an odd name, and I've never heard of anyone else with it but that rule-obsessed woman with the enormous breasts.<end>"
KO: "그 조포르가 예언했어. 자기브가 딩갈을 지배한다고. 자기브처럼 이상한 이름은 나, 그 규칙 타령하는 거유 여자 말고는 들어 본 적 없어.<end>"

## 850124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼女、今、ディンガルの内政を<line-break>統括してるし、あの予言、<line-break>一応当たったことになるわよね。　　<line-break>おおかた、予言を恐れた<line-break>ベルゼーヴァあたりが、<line-break>そうしたんだと思うのよ。<end>"
EN: "She oversees Dyneskal's internal affairs now, so technically the prophecy has already come true. I imagine someone like Beelzeva put her there out of fear of it.<end>"
KO: "그 여자 지금 딘갈 내정을 총괄하고 있으니 그 예언도 일단 맞은 셈이지. 아마 예언을 두려워한 벨제바 같은 녀석이 그렇게 한 것 같아.<end>"

## 850125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だけど、そんなことでは<line-break>予言は終わらないって思うわけ。<end>"
EN: "But I don't believe the prophecy ends there.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그런 걸로 예언이 끝날 거라곤 생각하지 않아.<end>"

## 850126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、オカマのカンよ。<line-break>ザギヴはディンガルの皇帝になるわ。<line-break>　<line-break>ただ、ザギヴがずっと<line-break>皇帝でいられるかについては<line-break>ゾフォルは予言してないの。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Call it a queen's intuition. Zagiv will become emperor of Dyneskal. But Zofor never said how long she'd remain emperor.<end>"
KO: "후후, 오카마의 감이야. 자기브는 딘갈 황제가 될 거야. 다만 자기브가 계속 황제로 있을지는 조포르도 예언하지 않았어.<end>"

## 850127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこで、ね。　　　　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Which brings me to my point.<end>"
KO: "그래서 말인데.<end>"

## 850128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたを、ザギヴの次の<line-break>皇帝にしてあげようと思うわけ。<end>"
EN: "I intend to make you the next emperor after Zagiv.<end>"
KO: "당신을 자기브 다음 황제로 만들어 주려는 거야.<end>"

## 850129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたって、<line-break>結構イイ線いってるのよ。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>バロル帝の子でありながら、<line-break>バロル帝に刃向かって死んだ<line-break>悲劇の光皇太子ルグの遺児…。<end>"
EN: "You have the makings of it: the surviving child of the tragic Crown Prince of Light, Lug, Emperor Balor's own son, who died defying his father...<end>"
KO: "당신, 꽤 좋은 선을 타고 있어. 발로르 황제의 자식이면서 발로르 황제에게 맞서다 죽은 비운의 광황태자 루그의 유복자….<end>"

## 850130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう言っても通用すると思うわ。<line-break>ねえ、民衆はそういう英雄を<line-break>待ち望んでるわ。皇帝にならない？　<line-break>アタシは子供作る気ないもの、<line-break>ただ、一生ぜいたくさせてくれたら、<line-break>あとはあなたの好きにして結構よ。<end>"
EN: "I think people would believe that story. The masses are longing for just such a hero. Why not become emperor? I have no intention of having children. Keep me in luxury for the rest of my life, and you may do whatever you please.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 말해도 통할 거야. 있잖아, 민중은 그런 영웅을 기다리고 있어. 황제가 되지 않을래? 난 아이를 낳을 생각은 없으니까, 평생 사치만 하게 해 주면 그 뒤는 네 마음대로 해도 좋아.<end>"

## 850131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どお？<line-break>ディンガルの皇帝になってみない？　<end>"
EN: "Well? Wouldn't you like to become emperor of Dyneskal?<end>"
KO: "어때? 딘갈의 황제가 되어 보지 않을래?<end>"

## 850132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、そのためにあなたにまず、<line-break>やって欲しいことがあるの。<line-break>ザギヴをたらし込んで欲しいのよ。<end>"
EN: "But first, there's something I need you to do. I want you to seduce Zagiv.<end>"
KO: "그래서 우선 네가 해 줬으면 하는 일이 있어. 자기브를 꼬셔 줬으면 해.<end>"

## 850133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫。<line-break>あなたならできるわ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>それにザギヴみたいな女、<line-break>結構ぬくもりに飢えてたりするのよ。<line-break>フフ。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry. You can do it. A woman like Zagiv may be quite starved for warmth, you know. Heh.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아. 당신이라면 할 수 있어. 게다가 자기브 같은 여자도 의외로 온기를 그리워할 때가 있거든. 후후.<end>"

## 850134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "別に恋仲になれとか<line-break>結婚しろって言うわけじゃないのよ。<line-break>あの女の心のスキマに入り込むだけ。<end>"
EN: "I'm not telling you to become her lover or marry her. Just slip into the empty place in her heart.<end>"
KO: "딱히 연인이 되라거나 결혼하라는 건 아니야. 그 여자의 마음 틈새로 파고들기만 하면 돼.<end>"

## 850135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むろん、若いころは美しく、<line-break>恋の狩人とも言われたこのアタシが、<line-break>細かーく指示してあげるわ。<end>"
EN: "Naturally, I'll tell you exactly what to do. I was beautiful in my youth and known as a hunter of hearts, after all.<end>"
KO: "물론 젊을 때 아름다워서 사랑의 사냥꾼이라고도 불렸던 이 내가 아주 세세하게 지시해 줄게.<end>"

## 850136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた、アタシが仕組んで<line-break>解放軍にザギヴを襲わせたとき…<line-break>ザギヴを助けてるわよねえ。<end>"
EN: "When I arranged for the Liberation Army to attack Zagiv... you rescued her, didn't you?<end>"
KO: "너, 내가 꾸며서 해방군이 자기브를 습격하게 했을 때… 자기브를 구해 줬지?<end>"

## 850137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいのいいの、あんな奴ら、捨て駒よ。<line-break>それよりもザギヴの信頼を<line-break>あなたが勝ち得たことの方が大事だわ。<line-break>この調子でザギヴに取り入るの。<line-break>こまめにザギヴのいる<line-break>エンシャントの政庁に出入りしてね。<end>"
EN: "It's fine, it's fine. Those people were expendable. What matters is that you won Zagiv's trust. Keep ingratiating yourself with her. Visit the Ancient Government Hall often while she's there.<end>"
KO: "됐어 됐어, 그런 녀석들은 버리는 패야. 그보다 자기브의 신뢰를 네가 얻었다는 게 더 중요해. 이대로 자기브에게 잘 보여. 자기브가 있는 엔샨트 정청에 자주 드나들고.<end>"

## 850138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "特に、何か大変事が起こったときは、　<line-break>何をさしおいても<line-break>彼女のそばにいてちょうだい。<line-break>まずは彼女を皇帝に据えること。<line-break>いいわね。<end>"
EN: "Whenever something momentous happens, drop everything and stay by her side. First, make her emperor. Understood?<end>"
KO: "특히 큰일이 생기면 무엇보다 그녀 곁에 있어 줘. 우선 그녀를 황제로 세우는 것. 알겠지.<end>"

## 850139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた、ザギヴの好感度は<line-break>かなり高いわよねえ。あの女、<line-break>あなたのことを結構、信頼してるわよ。<line-break>あの女は古代の闇の怪物の殲滅に<line-break>入れ込んでいるみたいだから。<line-break>その仕事をこなして点数を稼ぐのよ。<end>"
EN: "Zagiv seems to like you very much. She trusts you quite a bit. She also appears obsessed with exterminating the ancient monsters of darkness, so complete those jobs and win more favor.<end>"
KO: "너, 자기브의 호감도가 꽤 높지? 그 여자, 널 제법 믿고 있어. 그 여자는 고대의 어둠 괴물 섬멸에 열중하는 것 같으니 그 일을 해치워 점수를 따는 거야.<end>"

## 850140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうそう、近いうちにあの女、<line-break>ウチを調べるっ言ってたわね。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>解放軍に襲わせて、<line-break>適当にごまかしちゃおうと<line-break>思ってたんだけど、使えるわ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, yes. She said she'd be investigating us soon. I was going to have the Liberation Army attack her and use the confusion to cover things up, but this could prove useful.<end>"
KO: "그래, 조만간 그 여자가 우리를 조사하겠다고 했지. 해방군이 습격하게 해서 적당히 얼버무리려 했는데, 쓸 만하겠어.<end>"

## 850141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの女が東方方面を調べるからって<line-break>護衛を依頼してきたら、必ず受けるの。<line-break>きっとあなたの株が上がるわ。<end>"
EN: "If she asks you to escort her while she investigates the eastern region, be sure to accept. That should raise her opinion of you.<end>"
KO: "그 여자가 동쪽 지역을 조사한다며 호위를 의뢰해 오면 꼭 맡아. 분명 네 평판이 올라갈 거야.<end>"

## 850142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなた、ザギヴの受けがイイ感じよ。<line-break>このまま、がんばって<line-break>あの巨乳女に近づきなさい。<line-break>まず、エンシャントの墓地にいる　　<line-break>あの女に声をかけること。<line-break>そして…<end>"
EN: "You're making a good impression on Zagiv. Keep at it and get closer to that big-breasted woman. First, speak to her at the cemetery in Ancient. Then...<end>"
KO: "당신, 자기브한테 꽤 호감 사는 것 같아. 이대로 계속 그 가슴 큰 여자에게 접근해. 우선 엔샨트 묘지에 있는 그 여자에게 말을 걸어. 그리고…<end>"

## 850143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの女が入れ込んでいる古代の怪物の<line-break>殲滅の仕事をギルドで請け負って、<line-break>その仕事の数こなすのよ。<end>"
EN: "Take jobs from the Guild to exterminate the ancient monsters she's so obsessed with, and complete as many as you can.<end>"
KO: "그 여자가 심취해 있는 고대 괴물 섬멸 일을 길드에서 맡아서 그 일을 여러 번 해내는 거야.<end>"

## 850144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ザギヴはエンシャントの墓地の<line-break>自分の一族の墓によく出かけているわ。<line-break>見つけたら、積極的に話しかけて。<line-break>あのコ、人とのつきあい方を知らない<line-break>頭デッカチちゃんだから、結構ムッと<line-break>するだろうけど、ここはがまんよ。<end>"
EN: "Zagiv often visits her family grave in the cemetery at Ancient. If you see her there, make a point of speaking to her. She's a stuffy little intellectual who doesn't know how to deal with people, so she may get rather annoyed. You'll just have to endure it.<end>"
KO: "자기브는 엔샨트 묘지에 있는 자기 일족의 무덤에 자주 가. 찾으면 적극적으로 말을 걸어. 그 애는 사람 사귀는 법을 모르는 머리만 큰 애라 꽤 짜증 나겠지만, 여기선 참아.<end>"

## 850145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あとねえ。ティラの娘と呼ばれる<line-break>古代の闇の怪物退治に<line-break>あの女、異様な執念を燃やしてるのよ。<line-break>ギルドでその仕事を受けて、<line-break>その仕事をこなすのよ。<line-break>いつか、あの女の信頼が得られるわ。<end>"
EN: "One more thing. She's bizarrely obsessed with destroying the ancient monster of darkness known as the Daughter of Tira. Take the job at the Guild and see it through. Eventually, you'll earn her trust.<end>"
KO: "그리고 말이야. 티라의 딸이라 불리는 고대의 어둠 괴물 퇴치에 그 여자가 이상할 정도로 집착하고 있어. 길드에서 그 일을 받아서 해내는 거야. 그러면 언젠가 그 여자의 신뢰를 얻을 수 있을 거야.<end>"

## 850146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "頼りにしてるわよ<end>"
EN: "I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "믿고 있을게<end>"

## 850147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あらん、また振られちゃったわけね。<line-break>ま、いいわ。アタシ、待ってるから。<line-break>その気になったら<line-break>いつでも来てちょうだい。<end>"
EN: "Oh my, rejected again. Well, never mind. I'll be waiting, so come back whenever you change your mind.<end>"
KO: "어머, 또 차였나 보네. 뭐, 좋아. 난 기다릴 테니까 마음이 내키면 언제든 와.<end>"

## 850148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハァーイ、こちらを総督府の前まで<line-break>送ってあげてちょうだい？<end>"
EN: "Hellooo! Would you escort this person to just outside the governor's office?<end>"
KO: "네, 이쪽 분을 총독부 앞까지 데려다줘.<end>"

## 850149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！　<value:$28>様<line-break>どうぞ、お通りください！<end>"
EN: "Right away! <value:$28>, please go right in!<end>"
KO: "예! <value:$28>님, 들어가십시오!<end>"

## 850150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーら、<value:$28>、<line-break>いらっしゃーい。<line-break>その後、調子はどう？<end>"
EN: "Why, <value:$28>! Welcome. How have things been going?<end>"
KO: "어머, <value:$28>, 어서 와-. 그 뒤로 상태는 어때?<end>"

## 850151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この調子よ。<line-break>ザギヴに張り付いていてね。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>奴を皇帝にするように<line-break>あなたが誘導し、<line-break>奴を自滅させるのよ。<end>"
EN: "Keep it up. Stay close to Zagiv. Guide her into becoming emperor, then make her destroy herself.<end>"
KO: "그 상태로 가. 자기브에게 붙어 있어. 녀석을 황제로 만들도록 네가 유도해서 스스로 무너지게 하는 거야.<end>"

## 850152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "特に、何か大変事が起こったときは<line-break>何をさしおいても、政庁の<line-break>彼女のそばにいてちょうだい。<line-break>いいわね。<end>"
EN: "Whenever something momentous happens, drop everything and stay beside her at the Government Hall. Understood?<end>"
KO: "특히 큰일이 생기면 다른 건 제쳐 두고 정청에 있는 그녀 곁에 있어 줘. 알겠지.<end>"

## 850153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ンフフ、<line-break>うまくやったみたいじゃなーい？<end>"

KO: "후후, 잘 해낸 모양이네?<end>"

## 850155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この調子でザギヴに取り入ってね。<line-break>こまめにザギヴのいる<line-break>エンシャントの政庁に出入りするの。<line-break>特に、何か大変事が起こったときは、<line-break>何をさしおいても<line-break>彼女のそばにいてちょうだい。<end>"

## 850156

## 850157

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml

JP: "美少年ベドブ、愛の狩人…。<line-break>フフフ、かわいいでしょう？<line-break>でも、中身は今のアタシ以下よ。<line-break>だって、守りたかった人を　　　<line-break>守れなかったんですもの…。<end>"
EN: "Bedob the beautiful boy, hunter of love... Heh heh. Adorable, isn't he? But inside, he's even less of a person than I am now. He couldn't protect the person he wanted to protect...<end>"
KO: "미소년 베도브, 사랑의 사냥꾼… 후후후, 귀엽지? 하지만 속은 지금의 나보다 못해. 지키고 싶었던 사람을 지키지 못했으니까…<end>"

## 850170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec085-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさいね、待たせちゃって。<line-break>ちょっとヤボ用があった物だから。<line-break>じゃ、報告を聞きましょうか。<end>"
EN: "Sorry to keep you waiting. I had a little business to take care of. Now, let's hear your report.<end>"
KO: "미안해, 기다리게 했네. 좀 볼일이 있었거든. 그럼 보고를 들어 볼까.<end>"

## 860001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ。<value:$28>様で<line-break>ありますね。<line-break>どうぞお通りください！<end>"
EN: "Yes, <value:$28>, correct? Please go right through!<end>"
KO: "네. <value:$28> 님이시군요. 지나가십시오!<end>"

## 860002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なーんだ、<value:$28>。<line-break>まーた、あんたなんだ。<line-break>１日に何度も来るなんてヒマなのねー。<line-break>会いに来てくれるのはうれしいけどさ、<line-break>次の戦いに備えて<line-break>強くなるのも忘れないでね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, it's you again, <value:$28>. You must have plenty of time on your hands, coming here more than once a day. I'm glad you want to see me, but don't forget to get stronger for the next battle, all right?<end>"
KO: "뭐야, <value:$28>. 또 너야? 하루에 몇 번이나 오다니 한가하네. 만나러 와 주는 건 기쁘지만 다음 싸움에 대비해서 강해지는 것도 잊지 마.<end>"

## 860003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハァーイ！　元気だった？<line-break>元気なくてもあたしの前では<line-break>元気出しててよね。<end>"
EN: "Hi there! Been well? Even if you haven't, at least try to perk up around me.<end>"
KO: "하이! 잘 지냈어? 기운이 없어도 내 앞에선 기운 내 줘.<end>"

## 860004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しーっかし、懐かしいよねー。<line-break>ここにさ、ゼネテスとあんたと<line-break>愚王ペウダをやっつけにきたんだよね。<line-break>それが、ディンガルとロストールという<line-break>世界を二分する国の将として敵対関係。<line-break>ちょっと、できすぎだよね。<end>"
EN: "Still, this brings back memories. You, Xenetes, and I came here to take down that fool King Peuda. And now Xenetes and I stand opposed as generals of Dyneskal and Rostorl, the two nations that divide the world. It's all a little too neat, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "이야, 정말 그립네. 여기서 제네테스와 너랑 함께 우왕 페우다를 쓰러뜨리러 왔었잖아. 그런데 이제는 딘갈과 로스톨, 세계를 양분하는 두 나라의 장수가 되어 적대 관계라니. 좀 지나치게 잘 짜인 이야기 같지 않아?<end>"

## 860005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しーっかし、懐かしいよねー。<line-break>ここにさ、アイリーンとあんたと<line-break>愚王ペウダをやっつけにきたんだよね。<end>"
EN: "Still, this brings back memories. You, Irene, and I came here to take down that fool King Peuda.<end>"
KO: "그런데 정말 그립네~. 여기에 아이린, 너랑 같이 바보 왕 페우다를 쓰러뜨리러 왔었지.<end>"

## 860006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しーっかし、懐かしいよねー。<line-break>ここに、あんたとさ、<line-break>愚王ペウダをやっつけにきたんだよね。<end>"
EN: "Still, this brings back memories. You and I came here to take down that fool King Peuda.<end>"
KO: "그나저나 정말 그립네~. 여기 너랑 같이 와서 우왕 페우다를 쓰러뜨렸었지.<end>"

## 860007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、落とし穴とか、そのままだから<line-break>気をつけてよね。<line-break>あの化け物はもういないけどね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and watch your step. The pitfalls and everything are still here. That monster's gone, though.<end>"
KO: "아, 함정 구덩이 같은 건 그대로니까 조심해. 그 괴물은 이제 없지만.<end>"

## 860008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "昔ね、ここにアイリーンとふたりで<line-break>ロセン王ペウダをやっつけに<line-break>潜入したことがあるの。<line-break>ところが、ペウダの奴<line-break>この部屋に落とし穴とか<line-break>用意してて、見事ハマっちゃってさ。<end>"
EN: "I once sneaked in here with Irene to take down Peuda, the King of Rocen. But that bastard had rigged this room with pitfalls and everything, and we walked right into one.<end>"
KO: "예전에 아이린이랑 둘이서 로센 왕 페우다를 해치우려고 여기에 잠입한 적이 있어. 그런데 페우다 녀석이 이 방에 함정 같은 걸 준비해 둬서 보기 좋게 걸려들었지 뭐야.<end>"

## 860009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なーんか、懐かしいやねー。<line-break>ガムシャラだったあのころがさ。<end>"
EN: "It really takes me back―to those days of charging ahead without a thought for anything else.<end>"
KO: "왠지 그립네. 무작정 달려들던 그 시절이 말이야.<end>"

## 860010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう、ここにいたんだよねー。<line-break>あたしの故郷を襲い、略奪した　　　　<line-break>あの愚王ペウダがさ。<line-break>まずはペウダ、そして奴を影で<line-break>操っていたリベルダムの武器商人ども。<line-break>そして、次はロストールを倒す…。<end>"
EN: "That's right. That fool King Peuda was here―the one who attacked and plundered my homeland. First Peuda, then the Liberdam arms dealers who pulled his strings from the shadows. And Rostorl is next...<end>"
KO: "그래, 여기에 있었지. 내 고향을 습격하고 약탈한 그 어리석은 왕 페우다가. 우선 페우다, 그리고 그를 뒤에서 조종했던 리벨덤의 무기상들. 그리고 다음은 로스톨을 쓰러뜨린다….<end>"

## 860011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、そんなわけで<line-break><value:$28>様のお力が必要なの。<line-break>強くなってね。期待してるわ。<end>"
EN: "So that's why I need your help, the great <value:$28>. Get stronger. I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그런 사정이라 <value:$28>님의 힘이 필요해. 강해져. 기대하고 있을게.<end>"

## 860012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼いたします。<line-break>青竜将軍閣下。<end>"
EN: "Excuse me, Azure Dragon General.<end>"
KO: "실례하겠습니다. 청룡장군 각하.<end>"

## 860013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…。<line-break>…<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Oh... <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아…. …<value:$28>.<end>"

## 860014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが、<line-break><value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "So you're <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "당신이 <value:$28>….<end>"

## 860015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将軍、お話があります。<line-break>どうぞ、人払いを…。<end>"
EN: "General, I need to speak with you. In private, if you please...<end>"
KO: "장군, 드릴 말씀이 있습니다. 사람들을 물려 주십시오….<end>"

## 860016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんじゃ、仕方ないわね。<line-break><value:$28>、悪いけど<line-break>外してくれない？<end>"
EN: "Well, I suppose it can't be helped. Sorry, <value:$28>, but could you leave us alone?<end>"
KO: "그럼 어쩔 수 없네. <value:$28>, 미안한데 잠깐 빠져 줄래?<end>"

## 860017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec086-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪いわね。<line-break>また、来てね。<end>"
EN: "Sorry about that. Come see me again.<end>"
KO: "미안해. 또 와.<end>"

## 870001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！　<value:$28>様<line-break>どうぞ、お通りください！<end>"
EN: "Yes, <value:$28>! Please, go right in!<end>"
KO: "예! <value:$28>님, 들어가십시오!<end>"

## 870002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うそーん。<line-break>あなたのこと愛してたのにー。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>…！<line-break>ブゾリとできてたのねー！<line-break>ひどいわひどいわ。<end>"
EN: "Nooo! And I loved you! <value:$28>...! You and Buzori were an item! That's awful! Just awful!<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼~. 당신을 사랑했는데~. <value:$28>…! 부조리와 그런 사이였던 거야~! 너무해, 너무해.<end>"

## 870003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いや、青い死神とも恐れられている<line-break>あたしにツッコミを入れたのわ<line-break>あんたが初めてだよ。<end>"
EN: "No, seriously. I'm feared as the Blue Reaper, and you're the first person who's ever called me out like that.<end>"
KO: "…아니, 푸른 사신이라고까지 두려움받는 나한테 태클 건 건 네가 처음이야.<end>"

## 870004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーっす。<value:$28>じゃん。<line-break>ブゾリの下でも働いてたんだ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, it's <value:$28>. So you've worked under Buzori, too.<end>"
KO: "어이, <value:$28>이잖아. 부조리 밑에서도 일했었구나.<end>"

## 870005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…にしても、あんたみたいな<line-break>かわいい少年がさ。<line-break>よく、ブゾリの毒牙にかからないよね。<end>"
EN: "Still, a pretty boy like you... I'm amazed you haven't fallen prey to Buzori.<end>"
KO: "…그나저나 너 같은 귀여운 소년이 말이야. 어떻게 부조리의 독니에 안 걸리는지 신기하네.<end>"

## 870006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…実は、もう、かかってたとか。<end>"
EN: "Unless you already have.<end>"
KO: "…사실은 이미 걸려 있었다든가.<end>"

## 870007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、冗談冗談。<line-break>あたしって、ホラ、その辺、<line-break>結構、理解があるから。<line-break>世間が冷たくても<line-break>あたしは味方よ。<end>"
EN: "Heh, I'm joking, I'm joking. I'm pretty open-minded about that sort of thing, you know. Even if the rest of the world turns cold, I'm on your side.<end>"
KO: "후후, 농담이야 농담. 나 있잖아, 그런 쪽은 꽤 이해심이 있거든. 세상이 차갑더라도 나는 네 편이야.<end>"

## 870008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ～から冗談だって言ってんでしょ！<line-break>ま、頬染められて、<line-break>うつむかれても対応に困るけど、さ。<end>"
EN: "I told you, I'm joking! Though if you blush and hang your head like that, I won't know what to do with you.<end>"
KO: "그러니까 농담이라고 했잖아! 뭐, 얼굴 붉히고 고개 숙여도 어떻게 반응해야 할지 곤란하지만.<end>"

## 870009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、あんたが男だったら、<line-break>ブゾリのエジキよね、エジキ。<end>"
EN: "Well, if you were a man, you'd be Buzori's prey for sure.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 네가 남자였다면 부조리의 먹잇감이었겠지, 먹잇감.<end>"

## 870010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーっす。<value:$28>じゃん。<line-break>ブゾリの下で働いてたんだ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, it's <value:$28>. So you've worked under Buzori.<end>"
KO: "어이, <value:$28>잖아. 부조리 밑에서 일했었지.<end>"

## 870011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…にしても、あんたみたいな<line-break>かわいい少年がさ。<line-break>よく、ブゾリの毒牙にかからないよね。<end>"
EN: "Still, a pretty boy like you... I'm amazed you haven't fallen prey to Buzori.<end>"
KO: "…그나저나 너 같은 귀여운 소년이 말이야. 어떻게 부조리의 독니에 안 걸리는지 신기하네.<end>"

## 870012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…実は、もう、かかってたとか。<end>"
EN: "Unless you already have.<end>"
KO: "…사실은 이미 걸려 있었다든가.<end>"

## 870013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、冗談冗談。<line-break>あたしって、ホラ、その辺、<line-break>結構、理解があるから。<line-break>世間が冷たくても<line-break>あたしは味方よ。<end>"
EN: "Heh, I'm joking, I'm joking. I'm pretty open-minded about that sort of thing, you know. Even if the rest of the world turns cold, I'm on your side.<end>"
KO: "후후, 농담이야 농담. 나 있잖아, 그런 쪽은 꽤 이해심이 있거든. 세상이 차갑더라도 나는 네 편이야.<end>"

## 870014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ～から冗談だって言ってんでしょ！<line-break>ま、頬染められて、<line-break>うつむかれても対応に困るけど、さ。<end>"
EN: "I told you, I'm joking! Though if you blush and hang your head like that, I won't know what to do with you.<end>"
KO: "그러니까 농담이라고 했잖아! 뭐, 얼굴 붉히고 고개 숙여도 어떻게 반응해야 할지 곤란하지만.<end>"

## 870015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、あんたが男だったら、<line-break>ブゾリのエジキよね、エジキ。<end>"

KO: "뭐, 네가 남자였다면 부조리의 먹잇감이었겠지, 먹잇감.<end>"

## 870016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ブゾリの配下？<end>"
EN: "One of Buzori's people?<end>"
KO: "부조리의 부하?<end>"

## 870017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたし、カルラ。<line-break>ブゾリの上司。ボス。偉い人。　<line-break>　<line-break>ディンガルの東方攻略軍司令。<line-break>でもって青竜将軍。<line-break>ヨロシクぅ。<end>"
EN: "I'm Karula. Buzori's superior. The boss. The big shot. Commander of Dyneskal's Eastern Campaign Army, and Azure Dragon General. Nice to meet you.<end>"
KO: "난 칼라. 부조리의 상사. 보스. 높은 사람. 딘갈 동방공략군 사령관. 그리고 청룡장군. 잘 부탁해~.<end>"

## 870018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ブゾリって、たぶん、<line-break>あんたみたいなの好みだよ。<end>"
EN: "I bet you're just Buzori's type.<end>"
KO: "부조리는 아마 당신 같은 타입을 좋아할걸.<end>"

## 870019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だって、これ見てよ。　　　　　　　<line-break>光太子ルグの絵。<line-break>　<line-break>あんたに会ったとき、<line-break>どっかで見た感じだなって思ったら、<line-break>あんた、光太子ルグに似てるのよ。<end>"
EN: "Just look at this. It's a portrait of Lug, the Prince of Light. When I met you, you seemed familiar somehow. Now I know why: you look like Lug.<end>"
KO: "이것 좀 봐. 광태자 루그의 그림이야. 너를 처음 봤을 때 어디서 본 듯하다 했더니, 너 광태자 루그를 닮았어.<end>"

## 870020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "知らないの？　光太子ルグってのはね。<line-break>魔王バロルの息子よ。<line-break>　<line-break>美少年で理想家で正義の人でやさしくて<line-break>思いやりがあって、民にも人気があって<line-break>切れ者だったってゆーわね。<end>"
EN: "You don't know? Lug, the Prince of Light, was the son of Balor, Demon King. They say he was a beautiful boy: an idealist, just, kind, thoughtful, popular with the people, and clever, too.<end>"
KO: "몰라? 광태자 루그는 말이야, 마왕 발로르의 아들이야. 미소년에 이상주의자고 정의로운 데다 상냥하고 배려심도 깊고, 백성들에게 인기도 많고 아주 유능했다더라.<end>"

## 870021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな坊やは長生きできないものよ。　<line-break>オヤジに逆らって、<line-break>殺されちゃったわ。<end>"
EN: "Boys like that never live long. He defied his old man and got himself killed.<end>"
KO: "그런 애송이는 오래 못 사는 법이야. 아버지한테 덤볐다가 죽어 버렸거든.<end>"

## 870022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うっそー。ベドブの絵まで<line-break>飾ってあんじゃん。悪趣味ー。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ベドブってのは、ルグの側近で乳兄弟。<line-break>絶世の美少年で、愛の狩人って<line-break>異名もあったくらい。<end>"
EN: "No way. There's even a portrait of Bedb hanging here. What awful taste. Bedb was Lug's close attendant and milk brother. He was said to be the most beautiful boy alive. They even called him the Hunter of Love.<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼. 베도브 그림까지 걸어 놨잖아. 취향 나쁘네. 베도브는 루그의 측근이자 젖형제였어. 절세의 미소년으로 '사랑의 사냥꾼'이라는 별명도 있었지.<end>"

## 870023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "切れ者で、まぁ、ネメア様に対する<line-break>ベルゼーヴァみたいな感じね。<line-break>要するに、あんな感じの関係。<end>"
EN: "Sharp, too. Sort of like Beelzeva is to Lord Nemea. You know, that kind of relationship.<end>"
KO: "머리가 잘 돌아가는 사람이고, 뭐 네메아 님에게 벨제바 같은 느낌이랄까. 요컨대 그런 관계야.<end>"

## 870024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、ベルゼーヴァほど、<line-break>忠義じゃなかったみたいだけどね。　　<line-break>　<line-break>ルグがバロルに刃向かって、<line-break>バロルにルグが殺されたとき、<line-break>自分だけは逃げて助かったみたいだし。<end>"
EN: "Though he doesn't seem to have been as loyal as Beelzeva. When Lug rebelled against Balor and Balor killed him, Bedb apparently escaped and saved his own skin.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 벨제바만큼 충성스럽진 않았던 모양이지만. 루그가 발로르에게 반기를 들었다가 발로르에게 죽었을 때 자기만 도망쳐 살아남은 것 같거든.<end>"

## 870025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "民衆っておバカだからあ、<line-break>ルグみたいなのに<line-break>すぐ肩入れすんじゃん？<line-break>だからベドブのほうは<line-break>とばっちり食らって、伝説ではね、<line-break>その後ガマガエルになったって言うわ。<end>"
EN: "The masses are so stupid. They're always quick to take the side of someone like Lug, aren't they? So Bedb got caught in the backlash. Legend says he became a toad afterward.<end>"
KO: "민중은 바보라서 루그 같은 녀석한테 금방 감정이입하잖아? 그래서 베도브는 엉뚱하게 피해를 입었고, 전설에선 그 뒤 두꺼비가 됐다고 하더라.<end>"

## 870026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…にしても、<line-break>このあたしを待たすだなんて。　　<line-break>　<line-break>あのブタガエルも<line-break>本物のガマガエルに<line-break>変えちゃらにゃいかんみたいやね。<end>"
EN: "Still, making me wait like this... Looks like I'll have to turn that pig-frog into a real toad.<end>"
KO: "…그나저나 날 기다리게 하다니. 저 두꺼비 녀석도 진짜 두꺼비로 만들어 줘야겠네.<end>"

## 870027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たぶん、待ってても無駄よー。<line-break>どっかで賄賂もらってるか、<line-break>悪巧みしてるかだから。<end>"
EN: "Waiting's probably pointless. Probably off somewhere taking bribes or plotting something nasty.<end>"
KO: "아마 기다려도 소용없을걸. 어디서 뇌물이나 받고 있거나 나쁜 꿍꿍이를 꾸미고 있을 테니까.<end>"

## 870028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃね。<end>"
EN: "See you.<end>"
KO: "그럼 안녕.<end>"

## 870029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ！　<value:$28>様<line-break>どうぞ、お通りください！<end>"
EN: "Yes, <value:$28>! Please, go right in!<end>"
KO: "예! <value:$28>님, 들어가십시오!<end>"

## 870030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルグ様…、もう少しです。<line-break>本来ディンガルの帝座にあるべきは…<line-break>　<line-break>ザギヴの小娘でも、ネメアでも<line-break>カルラでも、エリュマルクでもない。<line-break>ルグ様…あなただったはずなのです。<end>"
EN: "Lord Lug... it will not be much longer. Dyneskal's imperial throne was never meant for that Zagiv girl, nor for Nemea, Karula, or Elmark. Lord Lug... it should have been yours.<end>"
KO: "루그 님… 이제 조금만 더입니다. 본래 딘갈의 제위에 있어야 할 분은… 자기브 같은 계집애도, 네메아도, 카를라도, 에류마르크도 아닙니다. 루그 님… 당신이었어야 합니다.<end>"

## 870031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたがバロル帝に反旗を翻し、<line-break>敗れ、バロル帝の軍勢に囲まれたとき<line-break>あなたは私を瞬間移動で逃がしました。<line-break>…私はあなたに捨てられたと思いこみ、<line-break>あなたのもとに戻ることなく、魔法で<line-break>姿を変え、生き延びることにしました。<end>"
EN: "When you raised the banner of rebellion against Emperor Balor, you were defeated and surrounded by his forces. You teleported me to safety. But I convinced myself that you had cast me aside. Instead of returning to you, I used magic to change my form and chose to survive.<end>"
KO: "당신이 발로르 황제에게 반기를 들었다가 패하고 그의 군대에 포위되었을 때, 당신은 저를 순간이동으로 피신시켰습니다. …저는 당신에게 버림받았다고 믿어 버렸고, 당신 곁으로 돌아가지 않은 채 마법으로 모습을 바꿔 살아남기로 했습니다.<end>"

## 870032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その時の失敗でこの醜い姿になったのも<line-break>当然のことだったのですね…。だって<line-break>私は本当に愚かだったんですから…。<line-break>命がけで守るべきものなんて存在しない<line-break>…そんな愚かなことを自暴自棄になって<line-break>そのときは、信じていたのですから…。<end>"
EN: "It was only fitting that my failure then left me in this hideous form. I truly was a fool. In my despair, I believed there was nothing worth protecting with my life. Such utter foolishness.<end>"
KO: "그때 실패해서 이런 추한 모습이 된 것도 당연한 일이었군요…. 저는 정말 어리석었으니까요…. 목숨 걸고 지킬 것 따윈 없다… 그때는 자포자기해서 그런 어리석은 생각을 정말 믿고 있었어요….<end>"

## 870033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな私にあのコが教えてくれました。<line-break>あなたの姿をしたあのコが<line-break>すさんでいた私に教えてくれました。<line-break>私が間違っているって…。<line-break>命がけで守るものが存在しない人生…<line-break>そんなものに生きる価値はありません。<end>"
EN: "Then that child taught me. That child who looked like you showed me, bitter as I had become, that I was wrong. A life with nothing worth risking your life to protect is no life worth living.<end>"
KO: "그런 나에게 그 아이가 가르쳐 주었습니다. 당신의 모습을 한 그 아이가 황폐해져 있던 나에게, 내가 틀렸다고 가르쳐 주었죠…. 목숨을 걸고 지킬 것이 없는 인생… 그런 삶에는 살아갈 가치가 없습니다.<end>"

## 870034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルグ様、私はあのコを帝位につけます。<line-break>あなたではなくあのコを帝位につけ、<line-break>そして、今度こそ、守って見せます…。<end>"
EN: "Lord Lug, I will place that child upon the imperial throne. Not you, but that child. And this time, I will protect that child.<end>"
KO: "루그 님, 저는 그 아이를 제위에 올리겠습니다. 당신이 아니라 그 아이를 제위에 올리고, 이번에야말로 지켜 보이겠습니다….<end>"

## 870035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありゃ、<value:$28>じゃん。<line-break>元気ぃ？<end>"
EN: "Well, look who it is. How've you been, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "어라, <value:$28>잖아. 잘 지내?<end>"

## 870036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは、結構。<line-break><value:$28>はあたしの<line-break>部下なんだから、<line-break>いざというときのために<line-break>体調を整えておいてよね。<end>"
EN: "Good. You're under my command, <value:$28>, so keep yourself in fighting shape for when the time comes.<end>"
KO: "그거 좋네. <value:$28>은 내 부하니까 만일을 위해 몸 상태를 잘 관리해 둬.<end>"

## 870037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とは、言っても次の戦いまで、<line-break>もうちょいかかるから<line-break>しばらくは冒険を続けてて。<line-break>そんじゃ、そういうことで<line-break>ばりばり腕を上げちゃって<line-break>ちょうだいな。<end>"
EN: "That said, it'll be a little while before the next battle, so keep adventuring in the meantime. Off you go, then. Make sure you really hone those skills.<end>"
KO: "그래도 다음 전투까지는 좀 걸리니까 당분간 모험을 계속해. 그럼 그렇게 해서 팍팍 실력을 키워 줘.<end>"

## 870038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと、ちょっと。<line-break>あたし、言わなかったけ？<line-break>あたしの前では元気出してよね。<line-break>あまり元気そうじゃないと、<line-break>周りにいる人間にも伝染して、<line-break>やる気が下がっちゃうんだよね。<end>"
EN: "Hey, wait a minute. Didn't I tell you? Try to perk up when you're around me. If you don't look lively enough, it rubs off on everyone else and saps their motivation.<end>"
KO: "잠깐, 잠깐. 내가 말 안 했나? 내 앞에서는 기운 좀 내. 너무 기운 없어 보이면 주변 사람한테도 전염돼서 의욕이 떨어진단 말이야.<end>"

## 870039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁ、それ以上に<line-break>あたし嫌いなんだけどね。<line-break>くよくよしてる奴、見るの。<end>"
EN: "More importantly, I just hate looking at people who sit around moping.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그보다 난 그런 게 싫지만. 끙끙 고민하는 녀석 보는 거 말이야.<end>"

## 870040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんで、せっかく来てもらったけど、<line-break>まだ、しばらく戦争やんないから、<line-break>冒険を続けてて。<line-break>そんじゃ、そういうことで<line-break>ばりばり腕を上げちゃって<line-break>ちょうだいな。<end>"
EN: "Anyway, I appreciate you coming, but we won't be going to war for a while yet, so keep adventuring. Off you go, then. Make sure you really hone those skills.<end>"
KO: "모처럼 와 줬지만 당분간 전쟁은 안 할 거니까 모험을 계속해. 그럼 그런 걸로, 실력 팍팍 키워 두라고.<end>"

## 870041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "元気<end>"
EN: "Doing well.<end>"
KO: "건강<end>"

## 870042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうでもない<end>"
EN: "Not really.<end>"
KO: "그렇지도 않아<end>"

## 870043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec087-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カルラ様は、しばらく<line-break>お忙しいとのことです。<line-break>今は、お引き取りください。<end>"
EN: "Lady Karula will be occupied for some time. Please leave for now.<end>"
KO: "칼라 님은 당분간 바쁘시다고 합니다. 지금은 돌아가 주십시오.<end>"

## 870044
