## 880001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちぇっ、ちょっと見せてくれても<line-break>いいのにな。<end>"
EN: "Aw, come on. It wouldn't hurt to let me take a quick look.<end>"
KO: "쳇, 조금 보여 줘도 되잖아.<end>"

## 880002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わあっ。すいませんっ！<line-break>ま、まだ何もしてませんっ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>エステル・ズゥ、非市民ですっ！<line-break>職業は冒険者ですっ！<line-break>ちゃんとギルドに登録してま…。<end>"
EN: "Ah! Sorry! I-I haven't done anything yet! Esther Zuu, noncitizen! Occupation: adventurer! I'm properly registered with the Guild...<end>"
KO: "와앗. 죄송합니다! 아, 아직 아무것도 안 했어요! 에스텔 즈, 비시민입니다! 직업은 모험가예요! 제대로 길드에 등록도 했….<end>"

## 880003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんだ、都市治安警官じゃないのか。<line-break>おどかさないでよ…。まったく。<line-break>　<line-break>…って、治安警官があんまり非市民に<line-break>横柄で権力的なんで、ついヒクツに<line-break>なっちゃったボクもボクだけどね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you're not a city security officer. Don't scare me like that... Honestly. Then again, city security officers are so overbearing and authoritarian toward noncitizens that I automatically start groveling. I suppose that's my fault, too.<end>"
KO: "…뭐야, 도시 치안경찰이 아니었네. 놀라게 하지 마…. 정말. …뭐, 치안경찰이 비시민한테 너무 거만하고 권력적이라 나도 모르게 비굴해진 나도 문제지만.<end>"

## 880004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見てよ、この時計塔。<line-break>大陸一でっかい魔法仕掛けの時計。<end>"
EN: "Look at this clock tower. It's the biggest magic-powered clock on the continent.<end>"
KO: "봐, 이 시계탑. 대륙에서 가장 큰 마법 장치 시계야.<end>"

## 880005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、非市民にそんなもの見せる<line-break>必要はないってさ。<line-break>さっき断られちゃった。<line-break>だから、忍び込んで見ちゃおうって<line-break>決心したときにキミが来たんで、<line-break>ボクはビックリさせられたわけ。<end>"
EN: "But apparently there's no reason to show something like that to a noncitizen. They turned me away just now. I'd just decided to sneak in and see it for myself when you showed up, so you gave me quite a fright.<end>"
KO: "하지만 비시민에게 그런 걸 보여 줄 필요는 없대. 아까 거절당했어. 그래서 몰래 들어가 보겠다고 결심한 순간 네가 와서 깜짝 놀란 거야.<end>"

## 880006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのさ、<line-break>動いているってすごいよね…。<line-break>　<line-break>ボクの住んでいるところにも<line-break>魔法仕掛けのものがたくさん<line-break>あるんだけどさ…。<end>"
EN: "You know, it's amazing that it actually works... There are plenty of magic-powered things where I live, too, but...<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 움직인다는 거 대단하지…. 내가 사는 곳에도 마법 장치가 잔뜩 있지만 말이야….<end>"

## 880007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあね、<line-break>………………。<end>"
EN: "Well, bye...<end>"
KO: "그럼 안녕, ……………….<end>"

## 880008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…キミの名前、<line-break>ボク、まだ聞いてないよ！　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>こっちは、職務質問と間違えて<line-break>もうしゃべっちゃったんだから。<line-break>キミも教えないと公平じゃないよ！<end>"
EN: "Wait, I still haven't asked your name! I already told you all about myself because I thought you were questioning me. It's only fair that you tell me yours!<end>"
KO: "…네 이름, 나 아직 못 들었어! 나는 직무 질문인 줄 알고 이미 말했잖아. 너도 알려 줘야 공평하지!<end>"

## 880009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こうなりゃ、いっそ強行突破、<line-break>不法侵入といきますか。<line-break>ボクの興味はとめられないよ！<end>"
EN: "Well, if that's how it is, maybe I'll just force my way in. Trespassing it is! Nothing can stop my curiosity!<end>"
KO: "이렇게 된 이상 아예 강행 돌파, 불법 침입으로 갈까. 내 호기심은 아무도 못 막아!<end>"

## 880010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ね。<line-break>中身が見てみたいと思わない？<end>"
EN: "Don't you want to see what's inside?<end>"
KO: "있지. 안에 뭐가 들었는지 보고 싶지 않아?<end>"

## 880011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなに否定するんなら　　　　<line-break>別に何者でもいいけどさ。<line-break>　<line-break>とにかく、<line-break>ボク、リベルダムによくいるし、<line-break>また会おうよ。じゃあね！<end>"
EN: "If you're going to deny it that strongly, I guess it doesn't matter who you are. Anyway, I'm in Liberdam a lot, so let's meet again sometime. Bye!<end>"
KO: "그렇게까지 부정한다면 뭐, 누구든 상관없지만. 어쨌든 난 리벨덤에 자주 있으니까 또 만나자. 그럼 안녕!<end>"

## 880012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぅーん、　　　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>っていうのか。<line-break>　<line-break>とにかく、<line-break>ボク、リベルダムによくいるし、<line-break>また会おうよ。じゃあね！<end>"
EN: "Hmm. So your name's <value:$28>. Anyway, I'm in Liberdam a lot, so let's meet again sometime. Bye!<end>"
KO: "흐음, <value:$28>라고 하는구나. 아무튼 나 리벨덤에 자주 있으니까 또 만나자. 그럼 안녕!<end>"

## 880013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれ、<line-break><value:$28>じゃない？<end>"
EN: "Hey, isn't that <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "어라, <value:$28> 아니야?<end>"

## 880014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ、リベルダムのこと？<line-break>残念だけど、ボクは…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you mean Liberdam? Sorry, but I'm...<end>"
KO: "아, 리벨덤 이야기? 아쉽지만 나는….<end>"

## 880015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクがリベルダム出身じゃない<line-break>ことぐらいすぐわかるだって？<line-break>　<line-break>どーせボクは<line-break>小汚いカッコした非市民だよ。<line-break>キミだって非市民じゃないか？<end>"
EN: "You could tell right away I'm not from Liberdam? Well, I guess I'm just a grubby-looking non-citizen. But aren't you a non-citizen too?<end>"
KO: "내가 리벨덤 출신이 아니라는 건금방 알 수 있다고? 그래, 나는꾀죄죄한 차림의 비시민이야. 너도 비시민 아니야?<end>"

## 880016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "非市民じゃない<end>"
EN: "I'm not a non-citizen.<end>"
KO: "비시민 아니야<end>"

## 880017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "非市民で悪い？<end>"
EN: "What's wrong with being a non-citizen?<end>"
KO: "비시민이라 나빠?<end>"

## 880018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへーん。そんなのウソだね。　　　　<line-break>ボクの目は節穴じゃないぞ。<line-break>　<line-break>ボクは人を見る目には自信があるんだ。<line-break>キミってさぁ、ぜ～んぜん<line-break>市民って感じしないもん。<end>"
EN: "Heh! That's a lie. I know what I'm looking at. I'm a pretty good judge of people, you know. And you don't seem like a citizen at all.<end>"
KO: "헤헤~ 그런 건 거짓말이야. 내 눈은 장식이 아니거든. 난 사람 보는 눈에는 자신 있어. 너는 전~혀 시민 같은 느낌이 안 나.<end>"

## 880019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、言っておくけど、<line-break>悪い意味で言ってんじゃないよ。<line-break>　<line-break>自分では何もしてないのに<line-break>いばり散らしてるだけのリベルダム市民<line-break>なんて、ボク大っ嫌いだから。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and I don't mean that in a bad way. I can't stand Liberdam citizens who've never accomplished anything themselves and do nothing but lord it over everyone.<end>"
KO: "아, 말해 두는데 나쁜 뜻으로 한 말은 아니야. 자기는 아무것도 안 하면서 으스대기만 하는 리벨덤 시민 같은 건 난 정말 싫어하거든.<end>"

## 880020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことないよ。<line-break>むしろ、市民って聞くと<line-break>構えちゃうかな。<line-break>まぁ、ボクだって<line-break>市民はみんな悪者だなんて<line-break>考えてるわけじゃないけどさ。<end>"
EN: "Not at all. If anything, hearing that someone's a citizen puts me on my guard. Not that I think every citizen is bad, of course.<end>"
KO: "그렇지 않아. 오히려 시민이라고 하면 경계하게 되려나. 뭐, 나도 시민이 전부 나쁜 사람이라고 생각하진 않지만.<end>"

## 880021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボク、市民かどうかなんて<line-break>つまらないレベルで人を見ないから。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I don't judge people on something as petty as whether they're citizens.<end>"
KO: "난 시민인지 아닌지 같은 시시한 수준으로 사람을 보지 않아.<end>"

## 880022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあね、<value:$28>。<line-break>見たい試合があるからボクは行くよ。<line-break>またね。<end>"
EN: "Well, see you around, <value:$28>. There's a match I want to watch, so I'd better go. Bye!<end>"
KO: "그럼 안녕, <value:$28>. 보고 싶은 시합이 있어서 난 갈게. 또 보자.<end>"

## 880023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ、そうだよ。<line-break>ボクも時々闘技場に出るんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>見ての通りだから<line-break>そんなに強い相手とは<line-break>試合にならないけどね。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, that's right. I fight in the arena sometimes too. As you can see, though, I'm no match for anyone too strong.<end>"
KO: "아, 그래. 나도 가끔 투기장에 나가. 보다시피 강한 상대와는 경기가 안 되지만.<end>"

## 880024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういう<value:$28>は<line-break>どうなんだい？<line-break>結構、強いんじゃない？　　　<end>"
EN: "What about you, <value:$28>? You're pretty strong, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "그런 <value:$28>는 어때? 꽤 강한 편 아니야?<end>"

## 880025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まぁね<end>"
EN: "Yeah, I guess.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그렇지<end>"

## 880026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだまだ駆け出し<end>"
EN: "I'm still a beginner.<end>"
KO: "아직 풋내기<end>"

## 880027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう戻ってください！<line-break>前みたいにギリギリでは困ります！<end>"
EN: "Please come back now! We can't have you cutting it as close as last time!<end>"
KO: "이제 돌아가 주세요! 지난번처럼 아슬아슬하면 곤란해요!<end>"

## 880028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちゃんと戻るよ！<line-break>そんなにボクが信用できないの！？<end>"
EN: "I'll be back in time! Do you really trust me that little?!<end>"
KO: "제대로 돌아올 거야! 그렇게 내가 못 미더워!?<end>"

## 880029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふ～ん、やっぱりそうなんだ。　<line-break>なんとなくわかるもん。<line-break>　<line-break>剣聖レーグみたいな<line-break>圧倒的な闘気は感じないけど、<line-break>内に秘めてる何かを感じるよ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm, so I was right. I could just tell. You don't have the overwhelming fighting spirit of Reig the Sword Saint, but I can sense something hidden inside you.<end>"
KO: "흐음, 역시 그렇구나. 왠지 알겠어. 검성 레그 같은 압도적인 투기는 안 느껴지지만 안에 숨긴 무언가가 느껴져.<end>"

## 880030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "またまたぁ。<line-break>ボクって結構、目利きなんだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>キミもボクと同じで<line-break>まだまだ駆け出しってところ<line-break>なんじゃないの？<end>"
EN: "Oh, come on. I've got a pretty good eye for these things. You're still just starting out, same as me, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "또 그런다. 나 꽤 사람 보는 눈이 있어. 너도 나처럼 아직 풋내기 아니야?<end>"

## 880031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アハハハ。やっぱりね。<end>"
EN: "Ahahaha! I knew it.<end>"
KO: "아하하하. 역시 그랬네.<end>"

## 880032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふ～ん、ボクの気のせいかな？<line-break>それとも謙遜してるの？<line-break>　<line-break>ま、最初はみんな駆け出しさ。<line-break>ボクらの武器は、いわば<line-break>可能性だもんね。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. Maybe I was imagining it. Or are you just being modest? Well, everyone starts out as a beginner. You could say our weapon is our potential.<end>"
KO: "흐음, 내 착각인가? 아니면 겸손한 거야? 뭐, 처음엔 누구나 초보지. 우리 무기는 말하자면 가능성이니까.<end>"

## 880033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キミもそのうち、<line-break>レーグみたいに<line-break>強くなれるかもよ？　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Maybe someday you'll be as strong as Reig.<end>"
KO: "너도 언젠가는 레그처럼 강해질지도 몰라.<end>"

## 880034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうではありません。<line-break>何があるかわかりませんから<line-break>用心に用心を重ねなくては！<end>"
EN: "That is not the issue. We don't know what might happen, so we must take every possible precaution!<end>"
KO: "그렇지 않습니다. 무슨 일이 있을지 모르니 조심하고 또 조심해야 합니다!<end>"

## 880035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクの生まれた所？<line-break>…ここからはかなり遠いよ。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ボク、時々こうして<line-break>故郷を抜け出して、いろんなとこを<line-break>見てまわっているんだ。<end>"
EN: "Where was I born? A long way from here. Every so often, I slip away from home like this and travel around seeing all sorts of places.<end>"
KO: "내가 태어난 곳? …여기서 꽤 멀어. 가끔 이렇게 고향을 빠져나와 여기저기 구경하고 있어.<end>"

## 880036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、いまだにさ、<line-break>にぎやかな街に来ると<line-break>ワクワクしちゃうんだよね。　　　<line-break>キミもボクの気持ち<line-break>わかるよね？<end>"
EN: "Even now, though, I still get excited whenever I come to a bustling city. You know what I mean, don't you?<end>"
KO: "그래도 아직도 활기찬 도시에 오면 두근거려. 너도 내 기분 알지?<end>"

## 880037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "都会生まれだから、わからない<end>"
EN: "I was born in the city, so I wouldn't know.<end>"
KO: "도시에서 태어나서 모르겠어.<end>"

## 880038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくわかるよ<end>"
EN: "I know exactly what you mean.<end>"
KO: "잘 알아<end>"

## 880039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウッソだぁ、ボクにはわかるんだから。<line-break>キミ、絶対そんな感じしないもん。<line-break>　<line-break>ボクもリベルダムに最初に来たころは<line-break>市民じゃないってことに<line-break>引け目を感じてたけどさ。<end>"
EN: "No way! I can tell, you know. You don't seem like that at all. When I first came to Liberdam, I felt self-conscious about not being a citizen too.<end>"
KO: "거짓말~. 난 알 수 있거든. 넌 절대 그런 느낌이 아닌걸. 나도 처음 리벨덤에 왔을 땐 시민이 아니라는 것 때문에 주눅 들었지만 말이야.<end>"

## 880040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今は、自分じゃ何もしていないのに<line-break>市民の家に生まれたっていうだけで<line-break>市民だ市民だっていばってる方が、<line-break>よっぽどカッコ悪いって思うんだよね。<line-break>…生まれた家なんて<line-break>…自分には関係ないのにさ。<end>"
EN: "But now I think it's much more pathetic to be born into a citizen family, accomplish nothing yourself, and still strut around boasting about being a citizen. It's not like you chose the family you were born into.<end>"
KO: "지금은 자기가 아무것도 한 게 없으면서 시민 집안에서 태어났다는 이유만으로 시민이다 시민이다 하며 으스대는 쪽이 훨씬 꼴사납다고 생각해. …태어난 집안 같은 건… 자기 자신과는 상관없는데 말이야.<end>"

## 880041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あはっ！　やっぱりわかる？<line-break>そうなんだよ。いろんなモノを見て<line-break>感動していかなきゃダメだよね。<end>"
EN: "Aha! So you do understand! That's right. You have to go out, see all sorts of things, and let them move you.<end>"
KO: "아핫! 역시 알겠어? 그래. 여러 가지를 보고 감동해 나가야 하는 거지.<end>"

## 880042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あんな所に埋もれて<line-break>一生を終えるなんて、<line-break>ボク、ぜーったいヤダ！　<line-break>キミだって<line-break>同じ気持ちでここに来たんだろ？<end>"
EN: "No way am I spending my whole life buried away in a place like that! You felt the same way when you came here, didn't you?<end>"
KO: "…그런 곳에 묻혀 평생을 끝내다니, 난 절대로 싫어! 너도 같은 마음으로 여기 온 거지?<end>"

## 880043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、我々と一緒に。<end>"
EN: "Come along with us.<end>"
KO: "자, 우리와 함께.<end>"

## 880045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でもさ、そのなかの１個だけでも<line-break>動くのをやめてしまったら、<line-break>時計全体は止まってしまうんだよね…。<line-break>ひとつの部品がヤダって言ったら…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "But if even one of those parts stops moving, the whole clock stops, doesn't it...? If just one part says it doesn't want to...<end>"
KO: "하지만 그중 하나라도 움직임을 멈추면 시계 전체가 멈춰 버리잖아… 부품 하나가 싫다고 하면…<end>"

## 880046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アハハハ。<line-break>ボク、何をわけわかんないこと<line-break>言ってんだろ。<end>"
EN: "Ahahaha. What am I even talking about?<end>"
KO: "아하하하. 나, 무슨 말도 안 되는 소리를 하고 있는 거지.<end>"

## 880048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだお前は！？<line-break>邪魔するな！<end>"
EN: "Who are you?! Stay out of this!<end>"
KO: "뭐냐 네놈은!? 방해하지 마!<end>"

## 880049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "面倒だ。処理しろ！<end>"
EN: "What a nuisance. Deal with this!<end>"
KO: "귀찮다. 처리해!<end>"

## 880050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イタタタ！　くそっ！<end>"
EN: "Ow, ow, ow! Damn it!<end>"
KO: "아야야! 젠장!<end>"

## 880051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんてことしてくれたんだ！<line-break>あそこまでやることないじゃない！<end>"
EN: "What did you do that for?! You didn't have to go that far!<end>"
KO: "무슨 짓을 한 거야! 그렇게까지 할 필요는 없잖아!<end>"

## 880052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクはキミに助けてなんて<line-break>一言も言ってない。<line-break>急ぐんだ！　どいてよ！<end>"
EN: "I never asked you to save me. I'm in a hurry, so move!<end>"
KO: "난 네게 도와 달라고 한마디도 안 했어. 서둘러야 해! 비켜 줘!<end>"

## 880053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ、やだよ。<line-break>会っておきたい人がいるんだから！！<end>"
EN: "No, not yet. There's someone I want to see first!<end>"
KO: "아직 싫어. 만나 두고 싶은 사람이 있단 말이야!!<end>"

## 880054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Hm? Someone you want to see?<end>"
KO: "…? 만나두고 싶은 사람?<end>"

## 880055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "と、ともかく、<line-break>もうしばらく自由にさせてよ！<end>"
EN: "A-anyway, just let me do as I please a little longer!<end>"
KO: "아, 아무튼 조금만 더 자유롭게 있게 해 줘!<end>"

## 880056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "안 됩니다. 놓치지 않을 거예요!<end>"

## 880057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どいてよ、もう！<end>"
EN: "Oh, just get out of my way!<end>"

## 880058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そりゃ、確かに手を出したのは<line-break>こっちが先だけど…。<line-break>　<line-break>勝手に首を突っ込んできたのは<line-break>キミの方じゃないか。<end>"
EN: "All right, we did start it, but... you're the one who barged in without being asked.<end>"
KO: "그래, 먼저 손을 댄 건 우리 쪽이지만…. 멋대로 끼어든 건 네 쪽이잖아.<end>"

## 880059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "내버려 둘 수 없었다고…. <value:$28>….<end>"

## 880060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんな心配しなくたって<line-break>ボクも冒険者なんだ。<line-break>自分の身は自分で守れるよ。<end>"
EN: "Y-you don't have to worry so much. I'm an adventurer too. I can take care of myself.<end>"
KO: "그, 그렇게 걱정 안 해도 나도 모험자야. 내 몸은 내가 지킬 수 있어.<end>"

## 880061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、急ぐから。<end>"
EN: "Well, I have to hurry.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 급해서 이만.<end>"

## 880062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やぁ、…ひさしぶりだね。<end>"
EN: "Hey... It's been a while.<end>"
KO: "여어, …오랜만이네.<end>"

## 880063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しばらくさ、用があって<line-break>リベルダム離れてたんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>…この前は、ありがと。<line-break>ごめんね、礼も言わずに行っちゃって。<line-break>…怒ってる？<end>"
EN: "I had to leave Liberdam for a while on some business. And... thanks for the other day. Sorry I left without even thanking you. Are you mad at me?<end>"
KO: "한동안 볼일이 있어서 리벨덤을 떠나 있었어. …지난번엔 고마워. 미안해, 인사도 없이 가 버려서. …화났어?<end>"

## 880065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、砂漠でも大丈夫。<line-break>この水晶を持ってれば<line-break>きっと見つかる。<line-break>これ、首飾りと<line-break>引き合う様にできてるんだ。<end>"
EN: "No, the desert won't be a problem. If you have this crystal, I'm sure you'll find it. It's made to be drawn toward the necklace.<end>"
KO: "아니, 사막이어도 괜찮아. 이 수정을 가지고 있으면 반드시 찾을 수 있어. 이건 목걸이와 서로 끌리게 되어 있거든.<end>"

## 880066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "絶対、他の人じゃダメなんだ！<line-break>キミに…探してほしいんだ。<line-break>お願い…。ダメかな…？<end>"
EN: "It has to be you! I want you to find it. Please... Will you do it?<end>"
KO: "절대 다른 사람은 안 돼! 네가… 찾아줬으면 해. 부탁이야…. 안 될까…?<end>"

## 880067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがと！<line-break>よかった…。<line-break>　<line-break>じゃあ、はい。<line-break>この水晶がみちびいてくれるよ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you! What a relief... Here, take this. The crystal will guide you.<end>"
KO: "고마워! 다행이다…. 자, 여기. 이 수정이 길을 안내해 줄 거야.<end>"

## 880068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "頼んだよ、<value:$28>！<line-break>リベルダムのギルドで待ってるからさ！<end>"
EN: "I'm counting on you, <value:$28>! I'll be waiting at the Guild in Liberdam!<end>"
KO: "부탁해, <value:$28>! 리벨덤 길드에서 기다리고 있을게!<end>"

## 880069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エステルの首飾りを手渡した。<end>"
EN: "Handed over Esther's necklace.<end>"
KO: "에스텔의 목걸이를 건넸다.<end>"

## 880070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう！<line-break>見つけてきてくれたんだ！<end>"
EN: "Thank you! You found it for me!<end>"
KO: "고마워! 찾아와 준 거구나!<end>"

## 880071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、水晶の方はいいの！<line-break>キミが持っててくれるかな…？<end>"
EN: "Oh, don't worry about the crystal! Would you hold on to it for me...?<end>"
KO: "아, 수정은 됐어! 네가 가지고 있어 줄래…?<end>"

## 880072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかった…。そうじゃなきゃ…　　　　<line-break>意味がないんだ…。<line-break>　<line-break>あっ、えっ、その<line-break>…ハハハ、何でもないよ！　独り言！<line-break>あんまり根ほり葉ほり聞かないの！<end>"
EN: "Good... Otherwise, there wouldn't be any point... Oh! Uh, I mean... Ha ha ha, it's nothing! Just talking to myself! Don't go prying into everything!<end>"
KO: "다행이다…. 그렇지 않으면… 의미가 없어…. 아, 어, 그게… 하하하, 아무것도 아니야! 혼잣말이야! 너무 꼬치꼬치 캐묻지 마!<end>"

## 880073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ、あっ、報酬…？<line-break>アハハハ…。<line-break>…体で払うって、ダメ？<line-break>あ、変な意味じゃなくて、<line-break>ボクがキミのパーティーに<line-break>入るってことなんだけどね。<end>"
EN: "Huh? Oh, a reward...? Ah ha ha ha... Could I pay you with my body? Oh, not like that! I mean, I could join your party.<end>"
KO: "어, 아, 보수…? 아하하하…. …몸으로 갚는 건 안 돼? 이상한 뜻이 아니라 내가 네 파티에 들어간다는 얘기야.<end>"

## 880074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えーっ！　あのっ！　そのっ！<line-break>…ボクなんかで…いいの？<end>"
EN: "What?! Um! I mean... You'd really be all right with someone like me?<end>"
KO: "에엣! 저기! 그게! …저 같은 사람으로… 괜찮아요?<end>"

## 880075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…たっぷりこき使ってやる、<line-break>冒険の旅はつらいぞって…。<line-break>　<line-break>いやーん！<line-break>やっぱりボクって<line-break>肉体労働専門なのね？<end>"
EN: "You mean you'll work me to the bone and show me how hard the adventuring life is...? Nooo! So I really am just here for the heavy lifting?<end>"
KO: "…실컷 부려 먹어 줄 거야, 모험 여행은 힘들다고…. 싫어~! 역시 나는 육체노동 전문인 거야?<end>"

## 880076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もー！　冒険の仲間になろうって<line-break>言ってるだけじゃん！<line-break>ボク、待ってるからねー！<end>"
EN: "Come on! I'm only saying I want to join you on your adventures! I'll be waiting!<end>"
KO: "정말! 모험 동료가 되자고 말하는 것뿐이잖아! 나 기다릴 테니까!<end>"

## 880077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、ごめん。<line-break>ボク、ちょっと用事があって、しばらく<line-break>キミと一緒に冒険できないんだ…。<line-break>多分、呼び出しにも応えられない…。<line-break>でも……、絶対戻ってくるから、<line-break>何も聞かないで行かせてほしい。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I'm sorry. There's something I need to take care of, so I won't be able to go adventuring with you for a while. I probably won't be able to come when you call, either... But I promise I'll come back. Please, just let me go without asking any questions.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 미안해. 나 좀 볼일이 있어서 한동안 너와 함께 모험할 수 없어…. 아마 호출에도 응하지 못할 거야…. 그래도… 반드시 돌아올 테니까 아무것도 묻지 말고 보내 줘.<end>"

## 880078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 880086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 880087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 880088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ、あっ、おみやげね。<line-break>ハハハ……ない。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…ごめんなさい！<line-break>それじゃ、猫屋敷で呼んでくれんの<line-break>待ってるから！<end>"

## 880128

## 880129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、待ってください！<line-break>私たちは怪しいものではありません！<line-break>その水晶、エステル様からもらったんですね？<end>"
EN: "W-wait! We're not up to anything suspicious! Lady Esther gave you that crystal, didn't she?<end>"
KO: "자, 잠깐만요! 저희는 수상한 사람들이 아닙니다! 그 수정, 에스텔 님에게 받은 거죠?<end>"

## 880130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは、エステル様が、<line-break>あなたを信頼しているという何よりの証。<line-break>あなた様を信用してお話しします。<line-break>私たちは、砂漠の一族。<line-break>竜骨の砂漠の奥深くにある<line-break>ラドラスに住む一族です。<end>"
EN: "That is proof enough that Lady Esther trusts you. We trust you as well, so we will explain who we are. We are the Desert Clan, a people who live in Ladras, deep within the Dragonbone Desert.<end>"
KO: "그건 에스텔 님이 당신을 신뢰한다는 무엇보다 확실한 증거입니다. 당신을 믿고 말씀드리겠습니다. 우리는 사막의 일족. 용골의 사막 깊숙한 곳 라드라스에 사는 일족입니다.<end>"

## 880131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、エステル様は<line-break>我ら砂漠の一族の族長なのです。<end>"
EN: "Lady Esther is the chieftain of our Desert Clan.<end>"
KO: "그리고 에스텔 님은 우리 사막 일족의 족장이십니다.<end>"

## 880132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "以前お会いしたとき、<line-break>我々がエステル様と言い争っていたのは、<line-break>エステル様をラドラスに連れ戻すためでした。<end>"
EN: "When last we met, we were arguing with Lady Esther because we were trying to bring her back to Ladras.<end>"
KO: "전에 만났을 때 저희가 에스텔 님과 말다툼하고 있었던 건 에스텔 님을 라드라스로 데려가기 위해서였습니다.<end>"

## 880133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エステル様は好奇心がお強く、<line-break>よくラドラスを抜け出しては<line-break>冒険に行ってしまわれるものでして…。<end>"
EN: "Lady Esther is intensely curious. She often slips away from Ladras and goes off adventuring...<end>"
KO: "에스텔 님은 호기심이 강하셔서 자주 라드라스를 몰래 빠져나가 모험을 떠나시곤 합니다….<end>"

## 880134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まずは、我々とエステル様の関係を<line-break>おわかりいただけたでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "Do you now understand our relationship with Lady Esther?<end>"
KO: "우선 저희와 에스텔 님의 관계를 이해하셨겠습니까?<end>"

## 880135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。<line-break>以前、はからずもあなた様と刃を交えた無礼は<line-break>お許しください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. We did not mean to cross blades with you when last we met. Please forgive our discourtesy.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 전에 뜻하지 않게 당신과 칼을 맞댄 무례를 용서해 주세요.<end>"

## 880136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "問題はここからなのです。<line-break>実は、そのエステル様が<line-break>ここしばらく行方不明なのです。<end>"
EN: "Now we come to the problem. Lady Esther has been missing for some time.<end>"
KO: "문제는 지금부터입니다. 사실 그 에스텔 님이 최근 한동안 행방불명 상태입니다.<end>"

## 880137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我々も手を尽くしたのですが<line-break>居場所をつかむことができず…！<end>"
EN: "We have tried everything, but we cannot find her!<end>"
KO: "저희도 온 힘을 다했지만 행방을 알아낼 수 없었습니다…!<end>"

## 880138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エステル様に水晶を託された<line-break>あなた様を信用してお願いいたします。<line-break>どうか、エステル様を捜してください。　　<line-break>その水晶は、エステル様の首飾りと<line-break>引き合います。今、エステル様を捜せるのは<line-break>あなた様だけなのです。<end>"
EN: "Lady Esther entrusted that crystal to you, so we place our trust in you as well. Please, find her. The crystal is drawn to Lady Esther's necklace. You are the only one who can find her now.<end>"
KO: "에스텔 님께서 수정을 맡긴 당신을 믿고 부탁드립니다. 부디 에스텔 님을 찾아 주세요. 그 수정은 에스텔 님의 목걸이와 서로 끌립니다. 지금 에스텔 님을 찾을 수 있는 건 당신뿐입니다.<end>"

## 880139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとしても、<line-break>１ケ月以内に捜しだしてください。<end>"
EN: "Whatever it takes, you must find her within one month.<end>"
KO: "무슨 수를 써서라도 한 달 안에 찾아 주세요.<end>"

## 880140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。<line-break>すでに水晶にわずかな反応があるようですから、<line-break>おそらくリベルダムの近くでしょう。<line-break>水晶が導く場所を<line-break>探してみてください。<line-break>どうか、よろしくお願いします。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. The crystal already seems to be responding faintly, so she is probably somewhere near Liberdam. Try searching for the Place Revealed by the Crystal. Please, we are counting on you.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 벌써 수정에 약한 반응이 있는 걸 보니 아마 리벨덤 근처일 겁니다. 수정이 이끄는 장소를 찾아봐 주세요. 부디 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 880142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アハハ、物騒な世の中になったなぁ。<line-break>電撃がとんでくるなんて。<line-break>君、大丈夫？<end>"
EN: "Ahaha! The world's become a dangerous place, hasn't it? Electric bolts flying around and all. Are you all right?<end>"
KO: "아하하, 험악한 세상이 됐네. 전격이 날아오다니. 너 괜찮아?<end>"

## 880143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫、大丈夫。<line-break>彼女に手荒な真似はしてないよ。<line-break>　<line-break>動けないように<line-break>呪縛をかけちゃったけどね。<end>"
EN: "She's fine, she's fine. I haven't hurt her. I did put a binding spell on her so she couldn't move, though.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아, 괜찮아. 그 여자에게 거칠게 하진 않았어. 움직이지 못하게 속박을 걸긴 했지만.<end>"

## 880145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml

## 880146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勇者君、こいつはちょっち手強いよ！<line-break>がんばって！　死んだりしないでね！<end>"
EN: "Hey, hero, this one's a little tough! Good luck! Try not to die, okay?<end>"
KO: "용자군, 이 녀석 조금 강해! 힘내! 죽거나 하지 마!<end>"

## 880147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おみごと！　強いねぇ。<line-break>ご褒美に呪縛を解いてあげるよ。<end>"
EN: "Well done! You're strong. As a reward, I'll release her from the binding spell.<end>"
KO: "훌륭해! 강하구나. 상으로 저주를 풀어 줄게.<end>"

## 880148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、動ける！<end>"
EN: "Oh, I can move!<end>"
KO: "아, 움직여져!<end>"

## 880149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、よかったね。<line-break><value:$28>に感謝しないとね。<line-break>じゃ、またね。<line-break>あははっ。<end>"
EN: "Heh, good for you. Be sure to thank <value:$28>. See you around. Ahaha!<end>"
KO: "후후, 다행이네. <value:$28>에게 감사해야겠어. 그럼 또 봐. 아하하.<end>"

## 880150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、ありがとう！<line-break>助けに来てくれるって信じてた！<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>! I knew you'd come to save me!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 고마워! 구하러 와 줄 거라고 믿었어!<end>"

## 880151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ…！<line-break>どうして…ボクが<line-break>ラドラスの人間だって知ってるの？<end>"
EN: "What...?! How did you know I'm from Ladras?<end>"
KO: "엣…! 어떻게… 내가 라드라스 사람인 걸 알고 있어?<end>"

## 880152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ラドラスのみんながボクを捜してたの？<line-break>た、大変だ。<line-break>すぐに戻らないと。<line-break>ねぇ、ついてきてくれないかな？<line-break>途中でまた襲われると嫌だし、<line-break>それに話したいことができたし。<end>"
EN: "Everyone in Ladras was looking for me? Th-that's bad. I have to get back right away. Will you come with me? I don't want to be attacked again on the way, and now I have something I want to talk to you about.<end>"
KO: "라드라스 사람들이 날 찾고 있었어? 큰일이다. 바로 돌아가야 해. 있잖아, 같이 가 줄래? 도중에 또 습격당하면 싫고, 그리고 하고 싶은 이야기도 생겼거든.<end>"

## 880153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがと、<value:$28>。<line-break>じゃあ行こう。ラドラスへ。<line-break>　<line-break>ラドラスは<line-break>竜骨の砂漠の中にあるんだ。<line-break>ボクが案内するよ。<end>"
EN: "Thanks, <value:$28>. All right, let's go to Ladras. It's in the Dragonbone Desert. I'll show you the way.<end>"
KO: "고마워, <value:$28>. 그럼 가자. 라드라스로. 라드라스는 용골 사막 안에 있어. 내가 안내할게.<end>"

## 880154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここがラドラスの入り口。<line-break>ラドラスではこのテレポーターで<line-break>移動することが多いんだ。<end>"
EN: "This is the entrance to Ladras. We usually get around Ladras using this teleporter.<end>"
KO: "여기가 라드라스 입구야. 라드라스에서는 이 텔레포터로 이동하는 일이 많아.<end>"

## 880155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すごいでしょ。<line-break>ラドラスは古代に滅びた　　　　<line-break>魔道王国の遺産なんだ。<line-break>だから、いろんな力が眠っている。<line-break>ボクたち砂漠の一族は<line-break>それを守っているんだ。<end>"
EN: "Amazing, isn't it? Ladras is the legacy of a Magic Kingdom that fell in ancient times. All sorts of powers still lie dormant here. Our desert clan guards that legacy.<end>"
KO: "굉장하지. 라드라스는 고대에 멸망한 마도왕국의 유산이야. 그래서 여러 힘이 잠들어 있어. 우리 사막의 일족은 그걸 지키고 있지.<end>"

## 880156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクたち以外にも<line-break>いろんな仕掛けがラドラスの遺産を<line-break>守っているんだ。<line-break>不定期に行われる<line-break>族長がいるかいないかのチェックも<line-break>そのひとつなんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Besides us, all sorts of mechanisms protect Ladras's legacy. One is a check, carried out at irregular intervals, to see whether the clan chief is here.<end>"
KO: "우리 말고도 여러 장치가 라드라스의 유산을 지키고 있어. 불규칙하게 시행되는 족장이 있는지 없는지 확인하는 것도 그중 하나야.<end>"

## 880157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "族長がいなければ、<line-break>ラドラスは砂漠に沈む。<end>"
EN: "If the clan chief isn't here, Ladras sinks into the desert.<end>"
KO: "족장이 없으면 라드라스는 사막에 가라앉는다.<end>"

## 880158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "異邦人に侵略されたと見なして、<line-break>遺産を守るために一族ごとね。<line-break>　<line-break>だから、キミがボクを<line-break>助けてくれていなかったら、<line-break>ここは沈んでいたかもしれない。<end>"
EN: "It assumes we've been invaded by outsiders and sinks the entire clan along with Ladras to protect the legacy. So if you hadn't saved me, this place might have sunk.<end>"
KO: "이방인에게 침략당했다고 판단해서 유산을 지키기 위해 일족 전체와 함께 말이야. 그래서 네가 날 도와주지 않았다면 여긴 가라앉았을지도 몰라.<end>"

## 880159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、ついてきて。<end>"
EN: "Come on, follow me.<end>"
KO: "자, 따라와.<end>"

## 880160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お待ちしておりました！<line-break>エステル様！<end>"
EN: "Esther! Your arrival has been eagerly awaited!<end>"
KO: "기다리고 있었습니다! 에스텔 님!<end>"

## 880161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめん…。<line-break>心配かけて。<end>"
EN: "Sorry... for worrying you.<end>"
KO: "미안해…. 걱정 끼쳐서.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 880170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0エステルって、坊やの仲間よね。<line-break>この前、あたしたちが助けたわ。　<line-break>　<line-break>ずいぶん長いこと<line-break>囚われの身だったみたいで、<line-break>かなり衰弱してたわ。<end>エステルって、子猫ちゃんの仲間よね。<line-break>この前、あたしたちが助けたわ。<line-break>　<line-break>ずいぶん長いこと<line-break>囚われの身だったみたいで、<line-break>かなり衰弱してたわ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Esther's one of your companions, isn't she, little boy? We rescued her the other day. It looked as though she'd been held captive for a very long time. She was terribly weak.<end>Esther's one of your companions, isn't she, kitten? We rescued her the other day. It looked as though she'd been held captive for a very long time. She was terribly weak.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0에스텔은 꼬마의 동료지? 지난번에 우리가 구해 줬어. 꽤 오랫동안 붙잡혀 있었던 모양이라 많이 쇠약해져 있었어.<end>에스텔은 아기고양이의 동료지? 지난번에 우리가 구해 줬어. 꽤 오랫동안 붙잡혀 있었던 모양이라 많이 쇠약해져 있었어.<end>"

## 880171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうして助けに行かなかったわけ？<line-break>彼女、衰弱よりも何よりもそれが<line-break>つらかったみたいだったわ。<line-break>彼女、<line-break>あんたのパーティーを抜けるって。<line-break>あたしは大賛成してあげたわ。<end>"
EN: "Why didn't you go and rescue her? More than the weakness or anything else, that seemed to be what hurt her most. She says she's leaving your party. I told her I was all for it.<end>"
KO: "왜 구하러 가지 않았던 거야? 그 애, 쇠약해진 것보다 무엇보다 그게 더 괴로웠던 모양이더라. 그 애 네 파티에서 나간대. 난 적극 찬성해 줬어.<end>"

## 880172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仲間のこと、<line-break>あんなにほっとくなんて最低ね。　<line-break>　<line-break>ナーシェスが突然、神懸かり的に<line-break>あのダンジョンに行こうなんて<line-break>言い出さなかったら、どうなってたか。<end>"
EN: "Leaving one of your own like that is despicable. If Narscess hadn't suddenly had a flash of divine inspiration and suggested going to that dungeon, there's no telling what would have happened.<end>"
KO: "동료를 그렇게 내버려 두다니 최악이네. 나셰스가 갑자기 신들린 듯 그 던전에 가자고 하지 않았다면 어떻게 됐을지.<end>"

## 880173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あたし、ノエルの仲間でよかった。<line-break>ノエルはあたしがダルケニスでも<line-break>仲間として助けてくれたもの。<end>"
EN: "I'm glad I'm one of Noel's companions. Even though I'm a Darkenith, Noel helped me as one of her companions.<end>"
KO: "나, 노엘의 동료라서 다행이야. 노엘은 내가 다르케니스여도 동료로서 도와줬으니까.<end>"

## 880174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと幻滅だわ。<line-break>あたしの言いたいのはそれだけ。<end>"
EN: "I'm a little disappointed in you. That's all I wanted to say.<end>"
KO: "조금 실망이야. 내가 하고 싶은 말은 그게 전부야.<end>"

## 880176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、今さら何しに来たんだよ。<line-break>絶対、キミが助けてくれるって<line-break>信じてたのに…。<line-break>だけど、キミは来なかった…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "W-what are you doing here now? I was sure you'd come and save me... But you never came...<end>"
KO: "이, 이제 와서 왜 온 거야. 난 네가 반드시 구해 줄 거라고 믿었는데…. 그런데 넌 오지 않았어….<end>"

## 880177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "出てって！<line-break>ボク、みじめだよ！　　　　　　<line-break>出てって！！<end>"
EN: "Get out! I'm so humiliated! Get out!!<end>"
KO: "나가! 나 너무 비참해! 나가라고!!<end>"

## 880178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すばらしい…。これなら、<line-break>万一の事態にも対応できよう。<end>"
EN: "Excellent... With this, we can deal with any contingency.<end>"
KO: "훌륭하다…. 이 정도면 만일의 사태에도 대응할 수 있겠군.<end>"

## 880179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、保護魔法に引っかかったか？　　<line-break>やはり私の手には負えぬようだ。<line-break>　<line-break>しかし、あのお方が、<line-break>エステルという小娘を救ってまで<line-break>手札に残しておかれたのもうなずける。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Caught by the protection spell, have you? It seems you're still beyond my ability to deal with. Yet I see why that person went so far as to save the girl called Esther and keep her in reserve.<end>"
KO: "후, 보호 마법에 걸렸나? 역시 내 손에는 벅찬 모양이군. 하지만 그분이 에스텔이라는 계집아이까지 구해 가며 패로 남겨 둔 것도 이해가 된다.<end>"

## 880180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは誰かと思えば、<line-break>ご自分の仲間も助けない勇者殿！<line-break>立ち聞きとはお行儀が悪いですな？<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't the great hero who wouldn't even save their own companion! Eavesdropping is rather ill-mannered, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "누군가 했더니 자기 동료도 돕지 않는 용사님이군! 엿듣다니 예의가 나쁘지 않습니까?<end>"

## 880181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつになったら<line-break>この小言は終わるんだろ？<end>"
EN: "How long is this lecture going to go on?<end>"
KO: "언제쯤이면 이 잔소리가 끝날까?<end>"

## 880182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このバカものは勝手にラドラスを出て、<line-break>砂漠に首飾りを探しに<line-break>行っとったんじゃ。<line-break>自分の首飾りを砂漠に隠して、<line-break>誰かに取ってきてもらえば<line-break>その者とのきずなが永遠のものになる。<end>"
EN: "This fool left Ladras without permission and went off into the desert looking for a necklace. Hide your own necklace in the desert and have someone retrieve it, and the bond between you will last forever.<end>"
KO: "이 바보 녀석은 멋대로 라드라스를 나가 사막으로 목걸이를 찾으러 갔던 거야. 자기 목걸이를 사막에 숨기고 누군가에게 가져오게 하면 그 사람과의 인연이 영원해진다나.<end>"

## 880183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そんなバカげたまじないが　　　　　<line-break>最近、流行っとるんじゃ。<line-break>ほんに困ったもんじゃ。<end>"
EN: "That ridiculous charm has become quite the fashion lately. It's a real nuisance.<end>"
KO: "…그런 바보 같은 미신이 요즘 유행하고 있단다. 정말 곤란한 일이야.<end>"

## 880184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんにちは。<end>"
EN: "Hello.<end>"
KO: "안녕하세요.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 880186

## 880187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "흐음, 정말 나 혼자야? 전혀 그렇게는 안 보이는데?<end>"

## 880188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그리고 아큐류스에 한 명, 울칸에 한 명, 용의 자리에도 한 명 정도일까?<end>"

## 880191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、ついてきてもらおうか、<line-break>かわいこちゃん？<end>"
EN: "Now then, why don't you come with me, cutie?<end>"
KO: "자, 따라와 주실까, 귀여운 아가씨?<end>"

## 880192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが答え？　もっと紳士的に<line-break>願いたかったんだけどな。<end>"
EN: "Is that your answer? I'd hoped to make a more gentlemanly request.<end>"
KO: "그게 대답이야? 좀 더 신사적으로 부탁하고 싶었는데.<end>"

## 880193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "殺れ。<end>"
EN: "Kill them.<end>"
KO: "죽여.<end>"

## 880194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、そんなんじゃ<line-break>ぜんぜんダメだよ、エステル。<line-break>いつか命を落とすよ？<line-break>君は他のパーティーメンバーに<line-break>あまり貢献していない。<line-break>平たく言えば役立たずだね！<end>"
EN: "Heh heh, that's no good at all, Esther. You'll get yourself killed one day. You don't contribute much to the other members of your party. Put plainly, you're useless!<end>"
KO: "후후, 그런 식이면 전혀 안 돼, 에스텔. 언젠가 목숨을 잃을걸? 넌 다른 파티 멤버들에게 별로 기여하지 못하고 있어. 쉽게 말하면 쓸모없다는 거지!<end>"

## 880196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は地の巫女だ。<line-break>その気になれば、今の怪物なんて<line-break>ひとひねりだったろうに…。<end>"
EN: "You're the Priestess of Earth. If you chose to use your power, that monster would have been nothing to you...<end>"
KO: "넌 땅의 무녀야. 마음만 먹었으면 방금 괴물쯤은 단숨에 해치웠을 텐데….<end>"

## 880198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エステル、君こそが<line-break>ラドラスを支配した４人の巫女のひとり<line-break>ズゥの子孫なんだ。<line-break>そして、君こそが<line-break>地の精霊神グラジェオンに仕える、<line-break>強大な魔力を持つ地の巫女なんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Esther, you are a descendant of Zuu, one of the four priestesses who ruled Ladras. And you are the Priestess of Earth, who serves Grajeon, the Earth Spirit God, and wields immense magical power.<end>"
KO: "에스텔, 바로 네가 라드라스를 지배했던 네 무녀 중 한 명인 즈의 후손이야. 그리고 네가 바로 대지의 정령신 그라제온을 섬기는 강대한 마력을 지닌 대지의 무녀지.<end>"

## 880199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2エステル、シャリの言うことなんかに<line-break>耳を貸しちゃダメよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>21貴様の言うことなど<line-break>信じられぬな！<end><if><value:$01><equal>3貴様の言うことなぞ<line-break>信じられるか！<end><if><value:$01><equal>5へっ、てめえの言うことなんか<line-break>信じらんねえよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>42お前の言うことなんか<line-break>信じられるものか！<end><if><value:$01><equal>7貴様の言うことなど<line-break>信じられるものか！<end><if><value:$01><equal>38こいつの言うことなんかに<line-break>耳を貸すな！　エステル！<end><if><value:$01><equal>39あんたの言うことなんか<line-break>信じられないよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>10お前の言うことなんか<line-break>信じられない！<end><if><value:$01><equal>13フッ、貴様など<line-break>信じられるか！<end><if><value:$01><equal>14貴様の言うことなんぞ<line-break>信じられんわい！<end><if><value:$01><equal>15エステル、あんな奴の言うこと<line-break>聞かないほうがいいよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>4難しいこと、わかんない！<line-break>ウソ臭いかも！　きゃっ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>22ダメだよ、エステルぅ！<line-break>あいつの言うこと信じちゃ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19エステル、シャリの言うことは<line-break>信じちゃダメ！　危険だよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>40貴様の言うことなんか<line-break>信じられるものか！<end><if><value:$01><equal>41エステル、信じちゃダメ<line-break>シャリは何かたくらんでる！<end><if><value:$01><equal>9シャリぃ、エステル惑わして<line-break>今度は何をたくらんでる？<end><if><value:$01><equal>18拙者、貴様の<line-break>言うことなど信じぬわ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>17うさんくさいシャリの言うことなんて<line-break>信じられない、信じられないわぁ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>11あなたの言うことは<line-break>信じられないわ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>12…食えない奴だな。<line-break>今度は何をたくらんでいる？<end><if><value:$01><equal>8…エステルを惑わすのは<line-break>やめてください。<end><if><value:$01><equal>16貴様は信じられん。<end>…たわごとを。<end>"

As if I could believe you!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14I can't believe a word you say!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Esther, you'd better not listen to a word that guy says.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4I don't understand difficult things! But he sounds like he's lying! Eek!<end><if><value:$01><equal>22Don't, Esther! You can't believe anything he says!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Esther, don't believe Shalli! Shalli's up to something!<end><if><value:$01><equal>9Shalli, you're trying to mislead Esther again? What are you plotting this time?<end><if><value:$01><equal>18I do not believe a word you say!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17I can't believe anything that shady Shalli says! KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2에스텔, 샤리 말 같은 건 들으면 안 돼!<end><if><value:$01><equal>21네놈 말 같은 건 믿을 수 없군!<end><if><value:$01><equal>3네놈 말 따위 믿을 수 있겠나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5흥, 네놈 말 같은 건 못 믿어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>42네 말 같은 걸 믿을 수 있을 것 같냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7네놈 말 따위 믿을 수 있을 리가 없다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>38이 녀석 말 같은 건 듣지 마! 에스텔!<end><if><value:$01><equal>39네 말 같은 건 못 믿어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>10네 말 같은 건 못 믿어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13훗, 네놈 같은 걸 믿을 수 있겠나!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14네놈 말 따위 믿을 수 없다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15에스텔, 저런 녀석 말은 듣지 않는 게 좋아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4어려운 건 몰라! 거짓말 냄새 날지도! 꺅!<end><if><value:$01><equal>22안 돼, 에스텔! 저 녀석 말 믿으면 안 돼!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19에스텔, 샤리 말은 믿으면 안 돼! 위험해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>40네놈 말 같은 걸 믿을 수 있을 것 같냐!<end><if><value:$01><equal>41에스텔, 믿지 마. 샤리는 뭔가 꾸미고 있어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>9샤리, 에스텔을 현혹해서 이번엔 뭘 꾸미는 거야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>18나는 네놈 말 따위 믿지 않겠소!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17수상쩍은 샤리 말 따위 못 믿어, 못 믿는다고!<end><if><value:$01><equal>11네 말은 믿을 수 없어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12…만만찮은 녀석이군. 이번엔 뭘 꾸미는 거냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>8…에스텔을 현혹하는 건 그만두세요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>16네놈은 믿을 수 없다.<end>…헛소리를.<end>"

## 880200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ラドラスに戻って、隠された<line-break>グラジェオンの聖座を求めるといい。<line-break>確かめてから人を疑いなよ。あはは。<end>"
EN: "Go back to Ladras and seek Grajeon's hidden Holy Seat. See for yourself before you start doubting people. Ahaha.<end>"
KO: "라드라스로 돌아가 숨겨진 그라제온의 성좌를 찾아봐. 확인하고 나서 남을 의심하라고. 아하하.<end>"

## 880206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>なんだか変な感じなんだ。<line-break>いつもの見なれたラドラスなのに<line-break>誰かに見られてるような…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... <value:$28>. Something feels strange. This is the same familiar Ladras I've always known, but... I feel like someone's watching me.<end>"
KO: "아… <value:$28>…. 왠지 이상한 느낌이야. 늘 보던 라드라스인데 누군가에게 감시받는 것 같아….<end>"

## 880207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "…에스텔이여… 눈을 떠라… 나의 땅의 무녀여….<end>"

## 880208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　…我は地の精霊神　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　グラジェオンなり…<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　　我が座所を求めよ…<line-break>　　　　　　　我が試練を受けよ…<line-break>　　　　　　　我が望みをかなえよ…<end>"
EN: "I am Grajeon, spirit god of the earth... Seek the place where I dwell... Face my trial... Fulfill my wish...<end>"
KO: "…나는 땅의 정령신 그라제온…. 나의 자리를 찾아라…. 나의 시련을 받아라…. 나의 소망을 이루어라….<end>"

## 880209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こないだのシャリも言ってた…。<line-break>ボクが…地の巫女…。<line-break>ラドラスの４人の巫女のひとり…。<end>"
EN: "Shalli said it the other day, too... That I'm... the Priestess of Earth. One of the four priestesses of Ladras...<end>"
KO: "전에 샤리도 말했어…. 내가… 땅의 무녀…. 라드라스의 네 무녀 중 하나….<end>"

## 880211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。<line-break>あのさ、悪いんだけど<line-break>ちょっとつきあってくれないかな？<line-break>さっきの声…、地の精霊神に<line-break>会ってみたいんだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Sorry to ask, but would you come with me for a bit? That voice just now... I want to meet the Spirit God of Earth.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 저기, 미안한데 잠깐 같이 가 줄래? 아까 그 목소리… 땅의 정령신을 만나 보고 싶어.<end>"

## 880212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この柱…。<line-break>普段は壊れてて<line-break>ウンともスンともいわないけど…。<line-break>さっきの声の感じ…<line-break>きっと、この向こうに…。<end>"
EN: "This pillar... Usually it's broken and won't do a thing. But that voice just now... I'm sure it came from the other side.<end>"
KO: "이 기둥…. 평소에는 고장 나서 꿈쩍도 안 하지만…. 아까 그 목소리 느낌으로는… 분명 이 너머에….<end>"

## 880213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、よかったー！<line-break>いっきなりボクひとりで<line-break>飛ばされちゃうんだもんなー。　<line-break><value:$28>が来なかったら<line-break>どうしようかって思ったよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, thank goodness! I got whisked away all by myself out of nowhere. I didn't know what I'd do if you didn't show up, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "다, 다행이다~! 갑자기 나 혼자 날아가 버렸잖아. <value:$28>이 안 오면 어쩌나 했어.<end>"

## 880214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴメン、ゴメンって。<line-break>…でも、どうしても気になるんだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>きっとこの先に<line-break>地の精霊神がいるんだよ。<line-break>ねえ、行ってみようよ！<end>"
EN: "Sorry! I said I was sorry. But I just can't stop wondering about it. I'm sure the Earth Spirit God is somewhere up ahead. Come on, let's go see!<end>"
KO: "미안, 미안하다니까. …그래도 정말 신경 쓰여. 분명 이 앞에 대지의 정령신이 있어. 있지, 가 보자!<end>"

## 880215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　 　　　　　　　来たか…。　　　<line-break>　　　エステルよ…。ズゥの血を引く娘よ…。<line-break>　　 　　　　　我が地の巫女よ…。<end>"
EN: "You have come... Esther, daughter of Zuu's line... Priestess of Earth...<end>"
KO: "왔는가…. 에스텔이여…. 즈의 피를 이은 딸이여…. 나의 대지의 무녀여….<end>"

## 880216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクを、呼んでたよね？<line-break>ずっと…。<line-break>キミが、地の精霊神なの？<end>"
EN: "You've been calling me, haven't you? All this time... Are you the Earth Spirit God?<end>"
KO: "나를 부르고 있었지? 계속…. 네가 땅의 정령신이야?<end>"

## 880217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　いかにも我は　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　地の精霊神、グラジェオン。<line-break>　　　　　待っていたぞ。エステルよ。<end>"
EN: "Indeed. I am Grajeon, spirit god of the earth. I have awaited you, Esther.<end>"
KO: "그렇다. 나는 땅의 정령신 그라제온. 기다리고 있었다, 에스텔이여.<end>"

## 880218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねぇ！<line-break>ボクが地の巫女って、<line-break>どういうこと！？<end>"
EN: "Hey! What do you mean, I'm the Priestess of Earth?!<end>"
KO: "있지! 내가 땅의 무녀라니, 무슨 말이야!?<end>"

## 880219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　汝こそ、我に仕えし魔道の巫女　　　　<line-break>　　　　　ズゥの血と力を受け継ぐ者なり。<line-break>　　　　　しかし、その力は未だ目覚めず。<end>"
EN: "You are the one who inherited the blood and power of Zuu, the priestess of magic who served me. Yet that power still lies dormant.<end>"
KO: "그대야말로 나를 섬겼던 마도의 무녀 즈의 피와 힘을 이어받은 자다. 그러나 그 힘은 아직 깨어나지 않았다.<end>"

## 880220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねぇ、<line-break>その巫女の力って<line-break>どうすれば身につくの？<end>"
EN: "So what do I have to do to gain the priestess's powers?<end>"
KO: "있지, 그 무녀의 힘은 어떻게 해야 얻을 수 있어?<end>"

## 880221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、だって…。　　　　　　　　<line-break>ボク、<value:$28>の<line-break>力になりたいんだ。<line-break>これからも一緒に冒険するんだ！<line-break>足手まといになんて<line-break>絶対になりたくないんだ！！<end>"
EN: "B-because... I want to help <value:$28>. We're going to keep adventuring together! I don't ever want to be a burden!!<end>"
KO: "하, 하지만…. 난 <value:$28>에게 도움이 되고 싶어. 앞으로도 함께 모험할 거야! 발목을 잡는 존재는 절대 되고 싶지 않아!!<end>"

## 880222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　エステルよ…。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　ここより先に、<line-break>　　　　　フルーヴという巨人がおる。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　　　奴を倒すのだ。<line-break>　　　　　　さすれば我が力をもって<line-break>　　　　　 汝の力を引き出してやろう。<end>"
EN: "Esther... Beyond this place dwells a giant called Fleuve. Defeat it, and by my power, I shall draw out yours.<end>"
KO: "에스텔이여…. 이 앞에 프루브라는 거인이 있다. 그자를 쓰러뜨려라. 그러면 내 힘으로 그대의 힘을 끌어내 주겠다.<end>"

## 880224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、じゃあ行こう<end>"
EN: "All right, let's go.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 그럼 가자<end>"

## 880225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行く必要なんかないよ<end>"
EN: "No need to go.<end>"
KO: "갈 필요 같은 건 없어.<end>"

## 880226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホント！？<end>"
EN: "Really?!<end>"
KO: "정말!?<end>"

## 880227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "グラジェオン、<line-break>ボクたち行くよ！<end>"
EN: "Grajeon, we're going!<end>"
KO: "그라제온, 우리 간다!<end>"

## 880228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　よかろう。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　では我は、汝を<line-break>　　　　　　 精霊の座へと導かん。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　汝、気高き地の光を越え<line-break>　　　　　　彼の巨人に挑むがよい。<line-break>　　　 　　挑み、打ち破るがよい…。<end>"
EN: "Very well. I shall guide you to the Seat of the Spirits. Pass beyond the noble light of earth and face that giant. Challenge it, and strike it down...<end>"
KO: "좋다. 그럼 내가 그대를 정령의 자리로 인도하겠다. 그대여, 고귀한 땅의 빛을 넘어 저 거인에게 도전하라. 도전하여 쓰러뜨려라….<end>"

## 880229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そんな！！　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>だって、このままじゃ<line-break>ボク、本当にキミの足手まとい…。<end>"
EN: "N-no!! But at this rate, I really will just hold you back...<end>"
KO: "그, 그럴 수가!! 이대로라면 나, 정말 네 짐만 될 텐데….<end>"

## 880230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…キミの決めたことには従うよ。<line-break>でも、でも、ボク…。<end>"
EN: "...I'll go along with your decision. But... but I...<end>"
KO: "…네가 정한 일이라면 따를게. 하지만, 하지만 나….<end>"

## 880231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　うつろいやすき人の子よ。　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　気が変わったならば<line-break>　　　　　また我を訪ねるがよい。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　　　　さらばだ。<end>"
EN: "Inconstant child of man, if you change your mind, come and seek me again. Farewell.<end>"
KO: "변덕스러운 인간의 아이여. 마음이 바뀌었다면 다시 나를 찾아오너라. 잘 가거라.<end>"

## 880232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　どうした？　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　巨人を前に臆したか？<end>"
EN: "What is it? Have you lost your nerve before the giant?<end>"
KO: "왜 그러느냐? 거인을 앞에 두고 겁먹었느냐?<end>"

## 880233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことはない<end>"
EN: "Not at all.<end>"
KO: "그렇지 않아<end>"

## 880234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめておこうかな<end>"
EN: "Maybe I should call it off.<end>"
KO: "그만둘까<end>"

## 880235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　ならば行くがよい。　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Then go.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 가라.<end>"

## 880236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな！　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>ここまで来て…。<line-break>　<line-break>ボク、巫女の力が欲しいのに…。<line-break>キミの足手まといになんかならない<line-break>強い力が欲しいのに…。<end>"
EN: "No! Not after we've come this far... I want the priestess's power. I want to be strong enough that I won't hold you back...<end>"
KO: "그럴 수가! 여기까지 왔는데…. 난 무녀의 힘을 갖고 싶은데…. 네 발목을 잡지 않을 강한 힘을 갖고 싶은데….<end>"

## 880237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかったよ。<line-break>キミが決めたことには従うよ…。<end>"
EN: "Okay. If that's what you've decided, I'll go along with it...<end>"
KO: "알았어. 네가 정한 일이라면 따를게….<end>"

## 880238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フン…地の巫女か。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>グラジェオンめ…<line-break>闇の力の高まりを知り、<line-break>我を滅ぼさんと欲したか。<end>"
EN: "Hmph... So you are the Priestess of Earth. Curse you, Grajeon... You sensed the growing power of darkness and sought to destroy me?<end>"
KO: "흥… 땅의 무녀인가. 그라제온 녀석… 어둠의 힘이 커지는 걸 알고 나를 멸하려 한 건가.<end>"

## 880239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、汝らごときに倒される<line-break>このフルーヴではない。<line-break>来るがよい、精霊神のしもべよ！！<end>"
EN: "But Fleuve will not fall to the likes of you. Come, servants of the Spirit God!<end>"
KO: "하지만 너희 따위에게 쓰러질 이 프루브가 아니다. 덤벼라, 정령신의 종들이여!!<end>"

## 880240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚かなり、地の巫女よ。<line-break>精霊神に躍らされ、<line-break>我を撃ち、闇を呼ぶか…。<line-break>愚かなり…愚かなり…。<end>"
EN: "Foolish Priestess of Earth. Made to dance to the Spirit God's tune, you strike me down and call forth darkness...? Foolish... How foolish.<end>"
KO: "어리석구나, 대지의 무녀여. 정령신에게 놀아나 나를 치고 어둠을 부르는가…. 어리석구나… 어리석어….<end>"

## 880241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　よくやった…　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　　エステルよ…<line-break>　　　　　　　我が地の巫女よ…。<end>"
EN: "Well done... Esther... my Priestess of Earth...<end>"
KO: "잘했다… 에스텔이여… 나의 땅의 무녀여….<end>"

## 880242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　よくぞ、巨人フルーヴを倒した。　　　<line-break>　　　約束通り、汝の内なる力を引き出そう。<line-break>　　　　目覚めよ。地の巫女エステルよ。<end>"
EN: "Well done. You have defeated Giant Fleuve. As promised, I shall draw out the power within you. Awaken, Esther, Priestess of Earth.<end>"
KO: "잘도 거인 프루브를 쓰러뜨렸다. 약속대로 그대 안의 힘을 끌어내 주마. 깨어나라. 대지의 무녀 에스텔이여.<end>"

## 880243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　さあ、再び行くがよい。　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　汝のなすべき旅の道へ。<line-break>　　　　　さらばだ。我が地の巫女よ。<end>"
EN: "Now go forth once more, along the path you are meant to travel. Farewell, Priestess of Earth.<end>"
KO: "자, 다시 가거라. 그대가 가야 할 여행길로. 잘 가거라. 나의 땅의 무녀여.<end>"

## 880244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う、う～ん…。<line-break>なんだか、最後は妙に乱暴だったなあ。<line-break>フラフラするよ。<end>"
EN: "Ugh... That was oddly rough at the end. I feel all wobbly.<end>"
KO: "으, 으음…. 왠지 마지막은 이상하게 난폭했네. 비틀거려.<end>"

## 880245
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>大丈夫だった？　<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, are you all right?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 괜찮았어?<end>"

## 880246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う、うわ！<line-break>なんだこの服！？<line-break>　<line-break>ん…？<end>"
EN: "Whoa! What's with this outfit?! Hm...?<end>"
KO: "우, 우와! 뭐야 이 옷!? …응?<end>"

## 880247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだろ？　この感じ。<line-break>ボク、いろんなことがわかるような<line-break>不思議な感じだ…。<line-break>この服も…<line-break>巫女の服なんだ…。<line-break>精霊神がくれたのかな…。<end>"
EN: "What is this feeling? It's strange... like I can understand all sorts of things. And this outfit... it's a priestess's robe. Did the Spirit God give it to me...?<end>"
KO: "뭐지, 이 느낌. 나, 여러 가지를 알 것 같은 이상한 느낌이야…. 이 옷도… 무녀의 옷이구나…. 정령신이 준 걸까….<end>"

## 880248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これが、地の巫女になったって　　<line-break>ことなのかな…？<line-break>うーん、よくわかんないや。<line-break>たはっ、なんだかスースーするや。<line-break>…ね、<value:$28>、<line-break>このカッコ、どう思う？<end>"
EN: "Does this mean I've become the Priestess of Earth...? Hmm, I don't really get it. Hah, it's kind of drafty, too. Hey, <value:$28>... what do you think of this outfit?<end>"
KO: "이게 땅의 무녀가 됐다는 건가…? 음, 잘 모르겠어. 하하, 왠지 휑하네. …저기, <value:$28>, 이 모습 어때?<end>"

## 880249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ステキだと思う<end>"
EN: "I think it looks great.<end>"
KO: "멋지다고 생각해<end>"

## 880250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガラじゃない<end>"
EN: "It's not really you.<end>"
KO: "내 스타일이 아냐<end>"

## 880251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え、そ、そう！？<line-break>キミにそう言ってもらえると、<line-break>ボク、うれしいな…。<end>"
EN: "R-really?! Hearing that from you makes me happy...<end>"
KO: "어, 그, 그래!? 네가 그렇게 말해 주니 나, 기쁘다….<end>"

## 880252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ね、キミさえよければ<line-break>ボク、このカッコで<line-break>冒険に出てもいいよ？<end>"
EN: "You know, if you don't mind, I could even go adventuring dressed like this.<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 네가 괜찮다면 나 이 모습으로 모험을 떠나도 돼?<end>"

## 880253
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっは、やっぱそれは<line-break>さすがにダメかな。<line-break>確かに動きにくいしね。ハハ…。　　<line-break>冒険のときはいつもの服にするよ。<line-break>けど、九柱ではこのカッコでいるから<line-break>ステキなボクに会いに来てよ。<end>"
EN: "Ahaha, yeah, I guess that'd be a bit much. Besides, it's hard to move around in this. Haha... I'll wear my usual clothes when I go adventuring. But I'll dress like this at the Nine Pillars, so come and see how lovely I look.<end>"
KO: "아핫, 역시 그건 좀 안 되려나. 확실히 움직이기도 불편하고. 하하…. 모험할 땐 평소 옷으로 할게. 그래도 구주에서는 이 차림으로 있을 테니까 멋진 나를 보러 와 줘.<end>"

## 880254
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ん～。<line-break>あは、そうだよね。<line-break>うん、自分でもさ、そう思うんだ。<line-break>でも、これを着ていると<line-break>なんだか落ち着くんだ。<line-break>なんでだろうね。<end>"
EN: "Hmm... Ahaha, you're right. I think so too, honestly. But for some reason, wearing this makes me feel at ease. I wonder why.<end>"
KO: "…음. 아하, 그렇지. 응, 나도 그렇게 생각해. 그래도 이걸 입고 있으면 왠지 마음이 놓여. 왜일까.<end>"

## 880255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だからボク、九柱にいるときは<line-break>このカッコでいたいんだ。<line-break>冒険には今までの服で行くけどね。<line-break>…キミが気に入ってくれなくて<line-break>少し、残念だな…。<end>"
EN: "That's why I want to dress like this whenever I'm at the Nine Pillars. I'll still wear my usual clothes when I go adventuring, though. I'm a little disappointed you don't like it...<end>"
KO: "그래서 난 구주에 있을 때는 이 차림으로 있고 싶어. 모험할 땐 지금까지 옷으로 갈 거지만. …네가 마음에 들어 하지 않아서 조금 아쉽네….<end>"

## 880256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、ボク、自分の部屋に帰るから。<line-break>またあとでね！<end>"
EN: "Well, I'm going back to my room. See you later!<end>"
KO: "그럼 난 내 방으로 돌아갈게. 나중에 또 봐!<end>"

## 880257
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　来たか、我が地の巫女エステルよ…。　　<line-break>　　　　ならば、我は今一度汝に問う。<line-break>　　　汝、巨人を倒し、力を欲するか？<end>"
EN: "You have come, Esther, my Priestess of Earth... Then I ask you once more: will you defeat the giant and seek power?<end>"
KO: "왔느냐, 나의 땅의 무녀 에스텔이여…. 그렇다면 다시 한번 묻겠다. 그대, 거인을 쓰러뜨리고 힘을 원하는가?<end>"

## 880258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>来てくれたんだね。<end>"
EN: "Hi, <value:$28>. You came to see me!<end>"
KO: "안녕, <value:$28>. 와 줬구나.<end>"

## 880259
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへへ～。<line-break>ボクのこのカッコも、見なれると<line-break>だいぶいい感じでしょ？<end>"
EN: "Hee hee! Once you get used to seeing me like this, it actually looks pretty good, don't you think?<end>"
KO: "헤헤헤~. 내 이 차림도 익숙해지니 꽤 괜찮아 보이지?<end>"

## 880260
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっとした気分転換になるし、<line-break>もう少しここでこうしてるよ。<line-break>じゃ、またあとで合流ね。<end>"
EN: "It's a nice change of pace, so I think I'll stay like this a little longer. I'll catch up with you later.<end>"
KO: "조금 기분전환도 되고, 좀 더 여기서 이렇게 있을게. 그럼 나중에 다시 합류하자.<end>"

## 880261
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところでさあ…。<line-break>う～、う～、<line-break>やっぱりボクも外に行きたいなあ…。<line-break><value:$28>、ボクも<line-break>連れていってくれないかなあ？<line-break>ボク、きっとキミの役に立てるよ。<end>"
EN: "By the way... Nngh... I really do want to go outside. <value:$28>, couldn't you take me with you? I know I could be useful.<end>"
KO: "그런데 말이야…. 으으, 으으, 역시 나도 밖에 나가고 싶어…. <value:$28>, 나도 데려가 주면 안 될까? 나, 분명 네 도움이 될 거야.<end>"

## 880262
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、一緒に行こう<end>"
EN: "Then come with me.<end>"
KO: "그럼 같이 가자.<end>"

## 880263
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君はここにいるべきだよ<end>"
EN: "You should stay here.<end>"
KO: "넌 여기 있어야 해.<end>"

## 880264
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホント！？<line-break>やったあー！！<end>"
EN: "Really?! Hooray!<end>"
KO: "정말!? 해냈다-!!<end>"

## 880265
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "って、おい！！　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>もうパーティー満員じゃない！！<line-break>ホントにもう！<line-break>ま、しょうがないか。<line-break>今度はボクも一緒に連れて行ってよね。<end>"
EN: "Hey, hold on! Your party's already full! Honestly! Oh well, I guess it can't be helped. Take me with you next time, okay?<end>"
KO: "아니, 잠깐!! 벌써 파티가 꽉 찼잖아!! 정말! 뭐, 어쩔 수 없지. 다음엔 나도 꼭 데려가 줘.<end>"

## 880266
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よーし、がんばるぞー！<line-break>よろしくね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "All right, I'll do my best! I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 힘내자! 잘 부탁해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 880267
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐむ～。<line-break>そう言われるとは思ったけど…。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、いつかまたボクも<line-break>一緒に冒険させてよね。<line-break>約束だよ！<end>"
EN: "Hmph. I thought you might say that... But let me come adventuring with you again someday, all right? Promise!<end>"
KO: "으음~. 그렇게 말할 줄 알았지만…. 그래도 언젠가 나도 같이 모험하게 해 줘. 약속이야!<end>"

## 880268
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>ラドラスはどう？<line-break>まだちょっと迷ったりする？<end>"
EN: "Hi, <value:$28>. How are you finding Ladras? Do you still get a little lost?<end>"
KO: "안녕, <value:$28>. 라드라스는 어때? 아직 좀 헤매기도 해?<end>"

## 880269
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "せっかく来たんだから<line-break>色々見てまわってよ。<line-break>じゃ、またあとで合流ね。<end>"
EN: "Since you're here, have a look around. I'll catch up with you later.<end>"
KO: "모처럼 왔으니 여기저기 구경해 봐. 그럼 나중에 다시 합류하자.<end>"

## 880270
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところでさあ…。<line-break>ボクも外に行きたいなあ…<line-break>な～んて…。<line-break>ねえ、<value:$28>。<line-break>ボクもキミの冒険に<line-break>連れていってくれないかなあ？<end>"
EN: "By the way... I kind of wish I could go outside too... Just a thought. Hey, <value:$28>, couldn't you take me along on your adventure?<end>"
KO: "그런데 말이야…. 나도 밖에 나가고 싶다…랄까…. 있지, <value:$28>. 나도 네 모험에 데려가 주면 안 될까?<end>"

## 880271
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぶ～。　　　　　　　　<line-break>そうかな～。<line-break>つまんないの～。<line-break>でも、いつかまたボクも<line-break>一緒に冒険させてよね。<line-break>約束だよ！<end>"
EN: "Boo. You really think so? That's no fun. But let me come adventuring with you again someday, all right? Promise!<end>"
KO: "부우~. 그런가~. 재미없어~. 그래도 언젠가 또 나도 같이 모험하게 해 줘. 약속이야!<end>"

## 880272
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>何回もボクに会いに来てくれるなんて<line-break>うれしいよ。<end>"
EN: "Hi, <value:$28>. I'm so happy you keep coming to see me.<end>"
KO: "안녕, <value:$28>. 몇 번이나 날 만나러 와 주다니 기뻐.<end>"

## 880273
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ～。<line-break>さては、ボクのこのカッコ、<line-break>かなり気に入ってるでしょ？<end>"
EN: "Aha! You really like this look of mine, don't you?<end>"
KO: "아~. 역시 내 이 모습, 꽤 마음에 든 거지?<end>"

## 880274
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボク、もう少しここでこうしてるよ。<line-break>じゃ、またあとで合流ね。<end>"
EN: "I think I'll stay like this a little longer. I'll catch up with you later.<end>"
KO: "난 조금 더 여기서 이러고 있을게. 그럼 나중에 다시 합류하자.<end>"

## 880275
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "って、おい！！<line-break>そういえばパーティー満員じゃない！！<line-break>ホントにもう！<line-break>ま、しょうがないか。<line-break>今度はボクも一緒に連れて行ってよね。<end>"
EN: "Hey, hold on! Come to think of it, your party's already full! Honestly! Oh well, I guess it can't be helped. Take me with you next time, okay?<end>"
KO: "아니, 잠깐!! 그러고 보니 파티가 꽉 찼잖아!! 정말이지! 뭐, 어쩔 수 없나. 다음엔 나도 같이 데려가 줘.<end>"

## 880276
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐむむ～。<line-break>やっぱりそう言うの～？<line-break>しょうがないか…。<line-break>でも、いつかまたボクも<line-break>一緒に冒険させてよね。<line-break>約束だよ！<end>"
EN: "Nnngh. You're really going to say that? Oh well... But let me come adventuring with you again someday, all right? Promise!<end>"
KO: "으으~. 역시 그렇게 말하는 거야~? 어쩔 수 없네…. 그래도 언젠가 또 나도 같이 모험하게 해 줘. 약속이야!<end>"

## 880277
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>また来てくれたんだ。<line-break>うれしいよ。<end>"
EN: "Hi, <value:$28>. You came back! I'm so happy.<end>"
KO: "안녕, <value:$28>. 또 와줬구나. 기뻐.<end>"

## 880278
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ところでさあ…<line-break>やっぱりボクも外に行きたいなあ…。<line-break>　<line-break>ねえ、<value:$28>。<line-break>お願い！　ボクもキミの冒険に<line-break>連れていってくれないかな？<end>"
EN: "By the way... I really do want to go outside after all. Please, <value:$28>! Couldn't you take me along on your adventure?<end>"
KO: "그런데 말이야… 역시 나도 밖에 나가고 싶어… 있잖아, <value:$28>. 부탁이야! 나도 네 모험에 데려가 주면 안 될까?<end>"

## 880279
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぶ～。　　　　　　　　<line-break>またそう言うんだ～。<line-break>つまんないの～。<line-break>でも、いつかまたボクも<line-break>一緒に冒険させてよね。<line-break>約束だよ！<end>"
EN: "Boo. You're saying that again? That's no fun. But let me come adventuring with you again someday, all right? Promise!<end>"
KO: "뿌우~. 또 그런 말 하는구나~. 재미없어~. 그래도 언젠가 나도 같이 모험하게 해 줘. 약속이야!<end>"

## 880280
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見ての通り移動の間にしか行けなくなって<line-break>しまったので案内の必要はなくなった。<line-break>これからは何を話せばよいのやら…。<end>"
EN: "As you can see, the Transit Chamber is the only place accessible now, so there's no need for me to show you around anymore. Now what am I going to talk about...?<end>"
KO: "보다시피 이동할 때에만 갈 수 있게 되었으니 안내는 필요 없어졌다. 이제부터는 무슨 이야기를 해야 할지….<end>"

## 880281
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ラドラスが浮上したときは<line-break>どうなることかと思ったけど、<line-break>無事に着地できてほんとうに良かったわ。<line-break>死んでしまったら外の世界を冒険する<line-break>ことができなくなるものね。<line-break><value:$28>さんありがとう。<end>"
EN: "When Ladras rose into the air, I was afraid of what might happen, but I'm so glad it landed safely. I couldn't explore the outside world if I'd died, after all. Thank you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "라드라스가 떠올랐을 땐 어떻게 되는 줄 알았는데 무사히 착륙해서 정말 다행이야. 죽어버리면 바깥세상을 모험할 수 없게 되잖아. <value:$28> 씨, 고마워.<end>"

## 880295
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 880296
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 880297
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그럼 우리도 계속 찾아보겠습니다. 가자.<end>"

## 880298
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec088-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、ごめん。<line-break>ボク、そろそろまた、<line-break>ラドラスへ戻らなきゃならないんだ。<line-break>キミの呼び出しにも応えられない…<line-break>でも、ボクがチェックを受けなくちゃ、<line-break>ラドラスが…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, I'm sorry. I have to go back to Ladras again soon. I won't be able to come when you call, either... But if I don't get checked, Ladras will...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 미안해. 나 슬슬 다시 라드라스로 돌아가야 해. 네 호출에도 응할 수 없어… 하지만 내가 검사를 받지 않으면 라드라스가….<end>"
