source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、待ってください！<line-break>私たちは怪しいものではありません！<line-break>その水晶、エステル様からもらったんですね？<end>"
EN: "W-wait! We're not up to anything suspicious! Lady Esther gave you that crystal, didn't she?<end>"
KO: "자, 잠깐만요! 저희는 수상한 사람들이 아닙니다! 그 수정, 에스텔 님에게 받은 거죠?<end>"

## 890003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは、エステル様ご自身の<line-break>意志でしか外れないもの。<line-break>あなた様を信用してお話しします。<line-break>私たちは砂漠の一族。<line-break>竜骨の砂漠奥深くにある魔道王国の遺産、<line-break>ラドラスに住まう者です。<end>"
EN: "That crystal cannot be removed unless Lady Esther wills it. We trust you, so we will explain. We are the desert clan, inhabitants of Ladras, a legacy of the Magic Kingdom deep within the Dragonbone Desert.<end>"
KO: "그건 에스텔 님 본인의 의지로만 벗겨지는 것입니다. 당신을 믿고 말씀드리겠습니다. 우리는 사막의 일족. 용골의 사막 깊숙한 곳에 있는 마도왕국의 유산, 라드라스에 사는 자들입니다.<end>"

## 890004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、エステル様は<line-break>我ら砂漠の一族の族長なのです。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "And Lady Esther is the chief of our desert clan.<end>"
KO: "그리고 에스텔 님은 우리 사막 일족의 족장이십니다.<end>"

## 890005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "中世、世界は神聖王国と魔道王国に<line-break>大きく二分されていました。<line-break>魔道王国は魔道技術により栄えていました。　　<line-break>しかし、支配者層に内部分裂が起こり、<line-break>そこを神聖王国の征服王ラムゼーにつかれ、<line-break>滅亡してしまったのです。<end>"
EN: "In the Middle Ages, the world was largely divided between the Holy Kingdom and the Magic Kingdom. The Magic Kingdom prospered through its sorcerous technology. But its ruling class split into opposing factions, and Ramsey, the Holy Kingdom's Conqueror King, seized the opportunity to destroy it.<end>"
KO: "중세에 세계는 신성왕국과 마도왕국으로 크게 양분돼 있었습니다. 마도왕국은 마도 기술로 번영했죠. 하지만 지배층의 내분이 일어났고 그 틈을 신성왕국의 정복왕 람제에게 찔려 멸망했습니다.<end>"

## 890006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "滅亡時、魔道王国の支配者のひとり、ズゥは<line-break>その魔道技術がラムゼーの手に渡るのを恐れ、<line-break>魔法で砂漠を作って魔道王国を砂に沈めました。<line-break>私たちは、その砂漠に埋もれた<line-break>魔道王国の遺産を、外部の人間から守るために<line-break>残された砂漠の一族なのです。<end>"
EN: "As the kingdom fell, one of its rulers, Zuu, feared that its sorcerous technology would fall into Ramsey's hands. Using magic, Zuu created a desert and buried the Magic Kingdom beneath the sands. We are the desert clan left behind to protect the kingdom's buried legacy from outsiders.<end>"
KO: "멸망 당시 마도왕국의 지배자 중 하나였던 즈는 그 마도기술이 람제의 손에 넘어가는 것을 두려워해 마법으로 사막을 만들고 마도왕국을 모래에 가라앉혔습니다. 우리는 그 사막에 묻힌 마도왕국의 유산을 외부인에게서 지키기 위해 남겨진 사막의 일족입니다.<end>"

## 890007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無論、砂の上で生きられるわけはありません。　<line-break>我々は、ラドラスで生活しているのです。<line-break>　<line-break>ラドラスでは、砂漠の中にいるのが<line-break>ウソのように暮らしやすい環境になっていて、<line-break>食べ物も飲み物も用意してくれるのです。<end>"
EN: "Naturally, we cannot live on the surface of the sands. We live in Ladras. Life there is so comfortable that it is hard to believe we are in the middle of a desert. Ladras even provides our food and drink.<end>"
KO: "물론 모래 위에서 살 수는 없습니다. 우리는 라드라스에서 생활하고 있습니다. 라드라스는 사막 한가운데라는 게 믿기지 않을 만큼 살기 좋은 환경이고 음식과 물도 제공됩니다.<end>"

## 890008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、この技術が外部の者に渡らないように、<line-break>不定期的にラドラスが族長の血統の者が<line-break>ラドラス内にいるかを調べます。<line-break>ラドラス内に族長がいないと、ラドラスが<line-break>異邦人によって占拠されたと判断され、<line-break>魔道技術の秘密を守るため、砂に沈むのです。<end>"
EN: "To keep this technology from falling into outside hands, Ladras checks at irregular intervals to ensure that someone of the chief's bloodline is present. If the chief is absent, Ladras concludes that outsiders have occupied the city and sinks beneath the sand to protect the secrets of its sorcerous technology.<end>"
KO: "다만 이 기술이 외부인에게 넘어가지 않도록 라드라스는 불규칙적으로 족장 혈통의 사람이 라드라스 안에 있는지 조사합니다. 족장이 없으면 이방인이 라드라스를 점거했다고 판단해 마도기술의 비밀을 지키기 위해 모래 속으로 가라앉습니다.<end>"

## 890009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そこで、族長であるエステル様なのです。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>エステル様は好奇心がお強く、<line-break>よく、ラドラスを抜けて<line-break>砂漠の外へ冒険に出かけられます。<end>"
EN: "That brings us to our chief, Lady Esther. She is intensely curious and often slips out of Ladras to go adventuring beyond the desert.<end>"
KO: "…그래서 족장인 에스텔 님입니다. 에스텔 님은 호기심이 많아 자주 라드라스를 빠져나와 사막 밖으로 모험을 나가십니다.<end>"

## 890011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今回は、会いたい人がいるからと<line-break>聞き分けていただけず、<line-break>あのような事態になったのです。<end>"
EN: "This time, she insisted that there was someone she wanted to see and refused to listen to us. That is how matters came to such a pass.<end>"
KO: "이번에는 만나고 싶은 사람이 있다며 말을 듣지 않으셔서 그런 일이 벌어진 겁니다.<end>"

## 890012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、あのときのことはもういいのです。<line-break>本題に入りましょう。<line-break>また、チェックの時が近づいております。<line-break>ですが、エステル様が戻られないのです。<line-break>我々はエステル様の身に<line-break>何か起こったのではと案じております。<end>"
EN: "But never mind what happened then. Let us come to the point. Another inspection is drawing near, but Lady Esther has not returned. We fear something may have happened to her.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그때 일은 이제 됐습니다. 본론으로 들어가죠. 다시 점검할 때가 가까워지고 있습니다. 그런데 에스텔 님이 돌아오지 않으셨습니다. 우리는 에스텔 님께 무슨 일이 생긴 건 아닌지 걱정하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 890013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エステル様に水晶を託された<line-break>あなた様を信用してお願いいたします。<line-break>どうか、エステル様を捜してください。　　<line-break>その水晶は、エステル様の首飾りと<line-break>引き合います。今、エステル様を捜せるのは<line-break>あなた様だけなのです。<end>"
EN: "Lady Esther entrusted that crystal to you, so we place our trust in you as well. Please, find her. The crystal is drawn to Lady Esther's necklace. You are the only person who can find her now.<end>"
KO: "에스텔 님께서 수정을 맡긴 당신을 믿고 부탁드립니다. 부디 에스텔 님을 찾아 주세요. 그 수정은 에스텔 님의 목걸이와 서로 끌립니다. 지금 에스텔 님을 찾을 수 있는 건 당신뿐입니다.<end>"

## 890014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エステル様が戻らなければ、<line-break>我々はあと１ケ月で、ラドラスもろとも　　<line-break>砂に沈むことになります。<line-break>どうか、エステル様を捜してください。<line-break>あと１ケ月しかないんです。<line-break>お願いします、<value:$28>さん。<end>"
EN: "If Lady Esther does not return, then one month from now we will sink beneath the sand along with Ladras. Please find her. We have only one month left. I beg you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "에스텔 님이 돌아오지 않으면 우리는 한 달 뒤 라드라스와 함께 모래에 가라앉게 됩니다. 제발 에스텔 님을 찾아 주세요. 이제 한 달밖에 남지 않았어요. 부탁드립니다, <value:$28> 씨.<end>"

## 890015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。<line-break>どうか、よろしくお願いします。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. We are counting on you.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 부디 잘 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 890016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（反応した…。リベルダムから<line-break>　そう遠くないダンジョンだな…。）<end>"
EN: "(It reacted... That dungeon isn't far from Liberdam...)<end>"
KO: "(반응했다… 리벨덤에서 그리 멀지 않은 던전이군…)<end>"

## 890017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大変だ、<value:$28>！<line-break>エステルがシャリにさらわれちゃった！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, it's terrible! Shalli kidnapped Esther!<end>"
KO: "큰일이야, <value:$28>! 에스텔이 샤리에게 납치됐어!<end>"

## 890018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>それは…？　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>お姉ちゃんの首飾り…。<line-break>これでお姉ちゃんの居所が<line-break>わかるんだね？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, what's that...? Sis's necklace... We can use it to find out where she is, right?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 그건…? 언니의 목걸이…. 이걸로 언니가 어디 있는지 알 수 있는 거지?<end>"

## 890019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…竜骨の砂漠の真ん中…。<end>"
EN: "The middle of the Dragonbone Desert...<end>"
KO: "…용골 사막 한가운데….<end>"

## 890020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ラドラスのあたりだよ？<line-break>どうなってるの？　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ねぇ、<value:$28>、<line-break>とにかく助けに行かなきゃ！<end>"
EN: "That's near Ladras, isn't it? What's going on? Come on, <value:$28>, we have to go save her!<end>"
KO: "라드라스 근처야? 어떻게 된 거야? 있잖아, <value:$28>, 어쨌든 구하러 가야 해!<end>"

## 890021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕、とにかく、情報をあつめるよ。<line-break>用があったら猫屋敷で呼んで。じゃね！<end>"
EN: "I'll start gathering information. If you need me, call for me at Cat Manor. See you!<end>"
KO: "난 일단 정보를 모아 볼게. 볼일 있으면 고양이 저택에서 불러. 그럼!<end>"

## 890022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様！<line-break>エステル様が、エステル様が！<line-break>シャリという魔術師にさらわれてしまいました！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! Lady Esther... Lady Esther has been kidnapped by a sorcerer named Shalli!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님! 에스텔 님이, 에스텔 님이! 샤리라는 마술사에게 납치됐습니다!<end>"

## 890023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは…エステル様の首飾りの水晶…。　　<line-break>それがあればエステル様の居場所が<line-break>わかるはず…。<end>"
EN: "That is... the crystal from Lady Esther's necklace. With it, we should be able to find her...<end>"
KO: "그건… 에스텔 님 목걸이의 수정…. 그게 있으면 에스텔 님이 어디 있는지 알 수 있을 거야….<end>"

## 890024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…竜骨の砂漠の真ん中…。<line-break>我らがラドラス…？　バカな！　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "The middle of the Dragonbone Desert... Our Ladras...? Impossible!<end>"
KO: "…용골의 사막 한가운데… 우리 라드라스가…? 말도 안 돼!<end>"

## 890025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お姉ちゃんの首飾りが…。<line-break>これでお姉ちゃんの居所が<line-break>わかるんだね？<end>"
EN: "Sis's necklace... We can use it to find out where she is, right?<end>"
KO: "언니의 목걸이가…. 이걸로 언니가 있는 곳을 알 수 있는 거지?<end>"

## 890026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…竜骨の砂漠の真ん中…。　<line-break>ラドラスのあたりだよ？<line-break>どうなってるの？<line-break>ねぇ、<value:$28>、<line-break>とにかく助けに行かなきゃ！<end>"
EN: "The middle of the Dragonbone Desert... That's near Ladras, isn't it? What's going on? Come on, <value:$28>, we have to go save her!<end>"
KO: "…용골 사막 한가운데…. 라드라스 근처잖아? 어떻게 된 거야? <value:$28>, 일단 구하러 가야 해!<end>"

## 890027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（…ここには確か、<line-break>　地の精霊神の座所への道が…。）<end>"
EN: "(If I remember correctly, there is a path here leading to the Seat of the Earth Spirit God...)<end>"
KO: "(…여기엔 분명 땅의 정령신이 있는 곳으로 가는 길이….)<end>"

## 890028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（エステルは、ここに、<line-break>　いるのかもしれない…）<end>"
EN: "(Esther might be here...)<end>"
KO: "(에스텔은 여기에 있을지도 몰라…)<end>"

## 890029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ラドラスのチェックを受けている間は、<line-break>エステル様は外に出られないのです。<line-break>お待ちください。<end>"
EN: "Lady Esther cannot go outside while Ladras is carrying out its inspection. Please wait.<end>"
KO: "라드라스의 검사를 받는 동안은 에스텔 님은 밖으로 나올 수 없습니다. 기다려 주세요.<end>"

## 890030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかげでチェックに間に合ったよ。<line-break>ありがとう、<value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>ねぇ、ちょっと座って話そっか。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I made it in time for the check. Thank you, <value:$28>. Hey, why don't we sit down and talk for a bit?<end>"
KO: "덕분에 점검에 맞췄어. 고마워, <value:$28>. 자, 잠깐 앉아서 이야기할까.<end>"

## 890031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、この衣装？<line-break>巫女の正装なんだ。<line-break>似合う？<end>"
EN: "Oh, this outfit? It's a priestess's formal dress. Does it suit me?<end>"
KO: "아, 이 옷? 무녀의 정장이야. 어울려?<end>"

## 890032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えへへ、ありがと。<line-break>ねえ、座らない？<end>"
EN: "Hehe, thanks. Hey, want to sit down?<end>"
KO: "헤헤, 고마워. 있잖아, 앉지 않을래?<end>"

## 890033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "チェックが終わったあと、<line-break>ボク、いつもここに来るんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>ここは、ラドラスから外が見える<line-break>たったひとつの場所だから。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I always come here after the check. It's the only place in Ladras where you can see the world outside.<end>"
KO: "점검이 끝나면 난 늘 여기 와. 라드라스에서 바깥을 볼 수 있는 유일한 장소거든.<end>"

## 890034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ラドラスはね、古代の魔道王国の<line-break>遺産のおかげで、砂漠の真ん中でも<line-break>生活できる環境が整えられているんだ。<line-break>だから飲み物にも食べ物にも<line-break>困ったことがない…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "The legacy of the Magic Kingdom makes Ladras livable even in the middle of the desert. We've never had to worry about food or drink...<end>"
KO: "라드라스는 고대 마도왕국의 유산 덕분에 사막 한가운데서도 살 수 있는 환경이 갖춰져 있어. 그래서 마실 것도 먹을 것도 부족했던 적이 없어….<end>"

## 890035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でもボクたちは、古代の魔道王国の遺産<line-break>っていうのが、他にどんなすごいことを<line-break>秘めているのか、知らないんだ。<line-break>動いているのも、このラドラスの<line-break>居住システムだけ。<line-break>他の魔道文明は眠ったまんまさ。<end>"
EN: "But we have no idea what other wonders the legacy of the Magic Kingdom might hold. The only thing still operating is Ladras's habitation system. The rest of its magical civilization remains dormant.<end>"
KO: "하지만 우리는 고대 마도왕국의 유산이라는 게 또 어떤 엄청난 힘을 품고 있는지 몰라. 움직이고 있는 것도 이 라드라스의 거주 시스템뿐이야. 다른 마도 문명은 여전히 잠든 채지.<end>"

## 890036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔道王国の遺産は、魔道王国を支配した<line-break>４人の巫女の血を引く者が集まった時、<line-break>復活するという…。<line-break>ボクは一応、ここの族長なんだけど、<line-break>それでも、そういう巫女とか、<line-break>遺産とかのことはよく知らないんだ。<end>"
EN: "They say the legacy of the Magic Kingdom will awaken when the descendants of the four priestesses who ruled it come together... I'm supposed to be the chief here, but even I don't know much about those priestesses or the legacy.<end>"
KO: "마도왕국의 유산은 마도왕국을 지배한 네 무녀의 피를 이은 자들이 모였을 때 부활한다고 해…. 나는 일단 여기 족장이지만 그래도 그런 무녀나 유산 이야기는 잘 몰라.<end>"

## 890037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでも復活の日まで、<line-break>ボクたちはこのラドラスに飼われて、<line-break>ここを守り続けなくちゃならない。<line-break>それが運命…ってやつ。<line-break><line-break><end>"
EN: "Even so, until the day it awakens, Ladras will keep us penned up here, and we'll have to go on guarding it. That's what they call fate...<end>"
KO: "그래도 부활의 날까지 우리는 이 라드라스에 매여 이곳을 계속 지켜야 해. 그게 운명…이라는 거지.<end>"

## 890038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うんざりだ。<line-break>ボクはそんなことのためだけに<line-break>生きていくなんて気はない。<line-break>こんな所でずっと、誰かのために<line-break>埋もれて生きていくなんて…<line-break>がまんできないんだ。<end>"
EN: "I'm sick of it. I won't live my whole life just for that. I can't stand the thought of staying buried in a place like this forever, living for someone else...<end>"
KO: "지긋지긋해. 난 그런 것만을 위해 살아갈 생각 없어. 이런 곳에서 계속 누군가를 위해 묻혀 살아간다니… 견딜 수 없어.<end>"

## 890039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ね、また、一緒に旅をしよ？<line-break>いいでしょ？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Hey, let's travel together again. All right?<end>"
KO: "있지, 또 같이 여행하자. 응?<end>"

## 890040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "パーティーに加える<end>"
EN: "Add Esther to the party<end>"
KO: "파티에 합류시킨다<end>"

## 890041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今はいい<end>"
EN: "Not right now<end>"
KO: "지금은 됐어<end>"

## 890042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "がんばるからね。<line-break>出発するときに、合流するよ。<line-break>じゃあ、あとでね。<end>"
EN: "I'll do my best. I'll meet up with you when you're ready to leave. See you later.<end>"
KO: "힘낼게. 출발할 때 합류할게. 그럼 나중에 봐.<end>"

## 890043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん、わかった。<line-break>じゃあ、猫屋敷で呼んでね。<line-break>待ってるから。<end>"
EN: "All right. Then call for me at Cat Manor. I'll be waiting.<end>"
KO: "응, 알았어. 그럼 고양이 저택에서 불러 줘. 기다릴게.<end>"

## 890044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、猫屋敷で呼んでね。<line-break>待ってるから。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Then call for me at Cat Manor. I'll be waiting.<end>"
KO: "그럼 고양이 저택에서 불러 줘. 기다리고 있을게.<end>"

## 890045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんばんは、エステル。<line-break>ごきげんはいかが？<end>"
EN: "Good evening, Esther. How are you?<end>"
KO: "안녕하세요, 에스텔. 기분은 어떠세요?<end>"

## 890046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリ！<end>"
EN: "Shalli!<end>"
KO: "샤리!<end>"

## 890047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うわ、嫌われたもんだなあ。<line-break>僕は君に会うために<line-break>飛んできたってのにさ。<line-break>ねえ、エステル。<line-break>君って、<value:$28>の<line-break>役に立ってるの？<end>"
EN: "Wow, you really do hate me. And after I came flying all this way just to see you. Tell me, Esther―are you any use to <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "와, 완전히 미움받았네. 난 널 만나려고 날아왔는데. 있지, 에스텔. 너 <value:$28>에게 도움이 되고 있긴 해?<end>"

## 890048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な…！<line-break>いきなり何を言い出すんだよ！？<end>"
EN: "Wh-what?! Where did that come from?!<end>"
KO: "뭐…! 갑자기 무슨 소리야!?<end>"

## 890049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら、こうやって君はいつも<line-break><value:$28>に支えられてる。<line-break>もしかして、足手まといなんじゃない？<end>"
EN: "See? <value:$28> is always the one supporting you. Maybe you're just getting in the way.<end>"
KO: "봐, 이렇게 넌 늘 <value:$28>에게 도움받고 있잖아. 혹시 짐만 되는 거 아냐?<end>"

## 890051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから僕はね、エステル。<line-break>君の本当の力を引き出してあげたいと<line-break>思って、君を迎えに来たんだ。<line-break>君は古代魔道王国ラドラスの<line-break>４人の巫女のひとり、<line-break>ズゥの血を引く地の巫女なんだよ。<end>"
EN: "That's why I've come to take you with me, Esther. I want to bring out your true power. You're the Priestess of Earth, descended from Zuu―one of the four priestesses of the ancient Magic Kingdom Ladras.<end>"
KO: "그래서 나는 말이야, 에스텔. 네 진짜 힘을 끌어내 주고 싶어서 널 데리러 왔어. 너는 고대 마도왕국 라드라스의 네 무녀 중 하나, 즈의 피를 이은 땅의 무녀야.<end>"

## 890053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は本当はすごい魔力を持っているし、<line-break>古代の魔道文明だってよみがえらせる<line-break>力を持っているんだよ。<line-break>なのに、君は全然それに気付かずに<line-break>そうやって毎日を過ごしてる。<end>"
EN: "You possess tremendous magical power. You even have the power to revive the ancient magical civilization. Yet you've gone on living day after day without ever realizing it.<end>"
KO: "너는 사실 엄청난 마력을 지녔고 고대 마도문명까지 되살릴 힘이 있어. 그런데도 전혀 눈치채지 못한 채 그렇게 매일을 보내고 있지.<end>"

## 890054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありゃりゃ。<line-break>なんだか、エステル連れて行くのが　<line-break>気まずい感じになっちゃったな。<line-break>ま、今はそんなに急いでないから<line-break>出なおしてくることにするよ。<line-break>じゃあねーん。<end>"
EN: "Whoops. Taking Esther with me now would be rather awkward. Well, I'm not in any great hurry, so I'll come back another time. See you!<end>"
KO: "이런. 왠지 에스텔을 데려가기 어색한 분위기가 됐네. 뭐, 지금은 그렇게 급하지 않으니 다시 올게. 그럼 안녕.<end>"

## 890055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクが…地の巫女…？<line-break>魔道王国ラドラスの巫女…？<end>"
EN: "I'm... the Priestess of Earth? A priestess of Magic Kingdom Ladras...?<end>"
KO: "내가… 땅의 무녀…? 마도왕국 라드라스의 무녀…?<end>"

## 890056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、アハハ…、ゴメン。<line-break>ちょっと、面食らっちゃった。<line-break>　<line-break>シャリもいなくなったし<line-break>もう大丈夫だよ。<line-break>さ、寝よ寝よ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, haha... sorry. That caught me off guard. Shalli's gone now, so everything's fine. Come on, let's get some sleep.<end>"
KO: "아, 아하하… 미안. 좀 당황했어. 샤리도 없어졌고 이제 괜찮아. 자, 자자.<end>"

## 890057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "き、キミは…シャリ！！<end>"
EN: "Y-you're... Shalli!!<end>"
KO: "너, 너는… 샤리!!<end>"

## 890058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "空中都市ラドラス、<line-break>それを復活させるためには<line-break>４人の巫女が必要なんだ。<line-break>ねえ、エステル。<line-break>君が必要なんだ。<line-break>一緒に来てくれないかな？<end>"
EN: "It takes four priestesses to restore the Floating City Ladras. Esther, I need you. Won't you come with me?<end>"
KO: "공중도시 라드라스를 부활시키려면 네 명의 무녀가 필요해. 에스텔, 네가 필요해. 같이 와 주지 않을래?<end>"

## 890059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰がキミなんかと！<line-break>絶対、お断りだよ！！<end>"
EN: "Like I'd ever go anywhere with you! Absolutely not!!<end>"
KO: "누가 너 따위랑! 절대 사양이야!!<end>"

## 890060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大体、巫女ってなんだよ！？<line-break>ボクはそんなのじゃないよ！<end>"
EN: "And what's all this about priestesses!? I'm not one of them!<end>"
KO: "대체 무녀가 뭔데!? 난 그런 게 아니라고!<end>"

## 890061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれ、まだ知らないんだっけ？<line-break>君って、古代の巫女の血を引く<line-break>ラドラスの地の巫女なんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you still don't know? You carry the blood of the ancient priestesses. You're the Priestess of Earth of Ladras.<end>"
KO: "어라, 아직 몰랐어? 넌 고대 무녀의 피를 이은 라드라스의 땅의 무녀야.<end>"

## 890062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な！？<line-break>なんなんだよいきなり！！　　<line-break>　<line-break>ボクはエステル！<line-break>確かにラドラス生まれだけど、<line-break>巫女なんて知るもんか！！<end>"
EN: "What!? Where did that come from!? I'm Esther! Sure, I was born in Ladras, but I don't know anything about being a priestess!!<end>"
KO: "뭐!? 갑자기 뭐야!! 난 에스텔이야! 라드라스 태생이긴 하지만 무녀 따위 알 게 뭐야!!<end>"

## 890063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、<line-break>君って僕のことを<line-break>ホントに邪険にするんだね。<line-break>でも、来てもらうよ。<line-break>空中都市ラドラスが飛ぶことは<line-break>願いだからね。<end>"
EN: "Honestly, you really don't want anything to do with me, do you? But you're coming anyway. The wish is for the Floating City Ladras to fly.<end>"
KO: "이런, 넌 정말 날 매정하게 대하는구나. 그래도 같이 가 줘야겠어. 공중도시 라드라스가 나는 건 소원이니까.<end>"

## 890064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ラドラスが…飛ぶ？<line-break>…空中都市だって？<line-break>そんなこと…。<line-break>…仮にキミの言ってることが<line-break>正しいとして、ラドラスを飛ばすことが<line-break>キミの願いなのか！？<end>"
EN: "Ladras... will fly? You mean it's really a Floating City? That's... Even if what you're saying is true, is making Ladras fly your wish!?<end>"
KO: "라드라스가… 난다고? …공중도시라고? 그럴 수가…. …설령 네 말이 맞다고 해도, 라드라스를 띄우는 게 네 소원이냐!?<end>"

## 890065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…僕の願い？　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>ううん、違うよエステル。<line-break>僕に願いなんかはないよ。<line-break>これは君の祖先たちの願いさ。<line-break>これを飛ばすことを願いつつ消えた<line-break>幾千幾万もの生命の遠い願い…。<end>"
EN: "My wish? No, Esther. I don't have any wishes. This is the wish of your ancestors―the distant wish of the thousands upon tens of thousands of lives that vanished longing to see it fly...<end>"
KO: "…내 소원? 아니야, 에스텔. 나한테 소원 같은 건 없어. 이건 네 조상들의 소원이야. 이것을 띄우길 바라며 사라져 간 수천 수만 생명의 아득한 소원….<end>"

## 890067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱり来たね、<value:$28>。<line-break>もうお互い全然新鮮味がないね。　<line-break>こういう展開ってさ。<line-break>君は、僕がまた怪物を呼び出して<line-break>君と戦わせたりするって思ってる。<line-break>そうでしょ？<end>"
EN: "I knew you'd come, <value:$28>. This sort of thing has become rather predictable for both of us, hasn't it? You think I'm going to summon another monster and make you fight it. Am I right?<end>"
KO: "역시 왔네, <value:$28>. 이제 우리 사이엔 전혀 신선함이 없네. 이런 전개 말이야. 넌 내가 또 괴물을 불러내서 너와 싸우게 할 거라고 생각하지? 그렇지?<end>"

## 890068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、今回はちょっと趣向を変えて　<line-break>そういうまどろっこしいのは<line-break>なしで行こうかな。<line-break>お互い、面倒なのはイヤでしょ？<line-break>じゃ、エステルはもらっていくから。<line-break>バイバ～イ。<end>"
EN: "But I think I'll change things up this time and skip all that tedious business. Neither of us wants the bother, right? So I'll just take Esther. Bye-bye!<end>"
KO: "하지만 이번엔 조금 취향을 바꿔서 그런 번거로운 건 빼고 갈까 해. 서로 귀찮은 건 싫잖아? 그럼 에스텔은 데려갈게. 바이바~이.<end>"

## 890069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハァイ、エステル。<line-break>相変わらず元気そうだね。<end>"
EN: "Hi, Esther. Looking lively as ever.<end>"
KO: "하이, 에스텔. 여전히 건강해 보이네.<end>"

## 890070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…シャリ！<end>"
EN: "…Shalli!<end>"
KO: "…샤리!<end>"

## 890071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たゆまぬ努力のかいあって<line-break>他の巫女たちは僕の手に落ちたよ。<line-break>残るは、君だけさ。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to my tireless efforts, all the other priestesses have fallen into my hands. You're the only one left.<end>"
KO: "끊임없는 노력 끝에 다른 무녀들은 모두 내 손에 넘어왔어. 이제 남은 건 너뿐이야.<end>"

## 890072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクを…どうしようっていうんだ！？<end>"
EN: "What are you planning to do... with me?!<end>"
KO: "나를… 어떻게 하려는 거야!?<end>"

## 890073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 890074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれ、まだ知らないんだっけ？<line-break>君って、古代の巫女の血を引く<line-break>ラドラスの地の巫女なんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you still don't know? You carry the blood of the ancient priestesses. You're the Priestess of Earth of Ladras.<end>"

## 890075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な！？<line-break>なんなんだよいきなり！！　　<line-break>　<line-break>ボクはエステル！<line-break>確かにラドラス生まれだけど、<line-break>巫女なんて知るもんか！！<end>"

## 890076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん～、なんか、もう<line-break>ゴチャゴチャ説明するのも<line-break>面倒になってきちゃったな。<line-break>君を連れて行く。<line-break>もう時間もないんだ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm... I'm getting tired of explaining all this. You're coming with me. There's no time left.<end>"

## 890077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、君なの？　ここで僕が怪物を<line-break>召還して、君たちが戦っているうちに<line-break>エステルをさらうってパターン？<line-break>いいかげん、飽きたよ。<line-break>そもそも、僕の実力なら<line-break>そんなことする必要ないからね。<end>"
EN: "You again? What, am I supposed to summon a monster here and abduct Esther while you fight it? I'm tired of that routine. With my power, there's no need for any of that.<end>"
KO: "또 너야? 여기서 내가 괴물을 소환하고 너희가 싸우는 사이 에스텔을 납치하는 패턴? 이제 질렸어. 애초에 내 실력이라면 그럴 필요도 없거든.<end>"

## 890078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec089-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、最後の巫女は連れてくよ。<line-break>これで空中都市ラドラスは晴れて<line-break>大空に飛びたつことができる。<end>"
EN: "Well, I'll be taking the last priestess. Now the Floating City Ladras can finally take to the skies.<end>"
KO: "그럼 마지막 무녀는 데려갈게. 이걸로 공중도시 라드라스는 드디어 하늘로 날아오를 수 있어.<end>"
